We extend FunctionGemma 270M-it to practical Android workflows by introducing MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, a synthetic, schema-validated dataset of ~9,500 conversations covering fifteen device-control categories, including messaging, phone calls, camera/screenshot, brightness control, device-status queries, flashlight control, and application management. We fine-tune the 270M model with TRL supervised fine-tuning under completion-only loss, producing an extended specialist and a combined model trained jointly with Google's MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE. On MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, end-to-end accuracy improves from 29.3% for the base model and 17.2% for Google's Mobile-Actions variant to 76.5%. The combined model retains 76.5% on MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED and reaches 82.3% on MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE, down from the 90.3% of Google's Mobile-Actions specialist, representing an 8.0-percentage-point trade-off in return for doubling category coverage. We release the dataset, fine-tuned models, reproducible training/evaluation pipeline, and an Android demo, highlighting compact local function calling as a practical path towards low-latency and privacy-preserving mobile assistants.

</details>


### [100] [Deep Reinforcement Learning on Item-Compatibility Graphs for One-Dimensional Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25397)
*M. Aslı Aydın*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端、尺寸无关的图强化学习框架求解一维装箱问题（1D-BPP），将装箱过程建模为物品兼容图上的马尔可夫决策过程，通过图神经网络 actor-critic 策略与随机束搜索解码，实现零样本泛化到任意规模实例，并在 BPPLIB 基准上系统评估，将构造式启发式的平均最优性差距从 2.66% 降至 2.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1D-BPP 是经典 NP-hard 组合优化问题，应用广泛，但现有基于深度强化学习的装箱方法多针对 2D/3D 变体，面向 1D-BPP 的智能化学习求解器十分稀缺，因此亟需一种数据驱动且能零样本泛化的 1D-BPP 求解方法。

Method: 将装箱过程建模为物品兼容图（item-compatibility graph）上的马尔可夫决策过程，每个动作合并两个可相容的部分箱；采用图神经网络 actor-critic 策略提取关系特征，经强化学习训练，并用随机束搜索（stochastic beam search）解码；对图编码器、DRL 算法、奖励函数、训练分布和超参数进行系统的经验研究。

Result: 在完整 BPPLIB 基准上零样本评估，本文策略将构造式启发式的平均最优性差距从 2.66% 降至 2.31%，在结构化实例上改进最大；与同样在基准上评估的学习基线相比，在九个家族的大多数上取得更低差距，且跨实例分布更稳定；在最困难的基准家族上优于依赖列生成与整数规划的 SOTA 学习求解器，且完全不用求解器；分组遗传算法整体仍领先，本文分析了残余差距的来源。

Conclusion: 所提图强化学习框架为 1D-BPP 提供了一个端到端、尺寸无关且可零样本泛化的学习范式，虽在整体上不及分组遗传算法，但在多数家族尤其是最困难家族上优于现有学习求解器，且稳定性和泛化性突出，残余差距的分析为后续改进指明方向。

Abstract: The one-dimensional bin packing problem (1D-BPP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with applications ranging from logistics and manufacturing to cloud resource management. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has become a competitive paradigm for data-driven optimization, most learned packing methods target 2D and 3D variants, and intelligent learned solvers for 1D-BPP remain scarce. In this paper, we present a novel end-to-end, size-agnostic graph reinforcement learning framework for 1D-BPP. We formulate the packing process as a Markov decision process on an item-compatibility graph, serving as a structural knowledge representation in which every action merges two partial bins that fit together. A graph neural network actor-critic policy extracts relational features from this representation and is trained through reinforcement learning and decoded by stochastic beam search, enabling a single trained model to generalize zero-shot to instances of any size. We conduct a systematic empirical study across graph encoders, DRL algorithms, reward functions, training distributions, and hyperparameters. Evaluated zero-shot on the full BPPLIB benchmark against a constructive heuristic, a grouping genetic algorithm, and recent learned methods, our data-driven policy lowers the mean optimality gap of the constructive heuristic from 2.66\% to 2.31\%, with the largest gains on structured instances. Against learned baselines evaluated on the same benchmark, it attains a lower gap on most of the nine families and is far more stable across instance distributions. On the hardest benchmark family, it outperforms a state-of-the-art learned solver that relies on column generation and integer programming, while using no solver at all. A grouping genetic algorithm remains ahead overall, and we analyze where and why the residual gap arises.

</details>


### [101] [A Practical Recipe for Semi-Supervised Federated ASR: Online Pseudo-Labels with Server Update Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25471)
*Wonho Bae,Zakaria Aldeneh,Martin Pelikan,Jan "Honza" Silovsky,Tatiana Likhomanenko,Sheikh Shams Azam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究半监督联邦学习（SSFL）在自动语音识别（ASR）中的收敛稳定性问题，揭示教师模型（teacher）与服务器标注锚点（anchor）两个设计轴的耦合关系，并提出实用指南，显著缩小与全监督FL的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSFL在ASR场景中尤其脆弱：伪标签错误会沿输出序列累积、并在多轮训练中复合，导致模型发散，与全监督联邦学习之间存在巨大性能差距。作者旨在找出缩小该差距的关键设计因素。

Method: 围绕两个耦合设计轴展开系统性实验：(1) 教师轴——比较每客户端在线教师（客户端自身演化模型）、广播全局教师（轮内固定的单服务器模型）以及过渡型教师（第r轮由全局切换为在线）；(2) 锚点轴——分析服务器在轮间持续用标注数据训练（交错训练）对稳定性的影响，并考察数据增强与批大小引入的输入/梯度噪声对收敛的调控作用，结合种子数据分散度及与客户端数据的重叠度进行分析。

Result: 在线教师在未稳定时会自行发散，但稳定后匹配或超越全局教师——域内决定性领先、域偏移下则具竞争力；随种子数据增强，在线教师优势收窄，过渡型教师可匹配或超越两者。服务器轮间持续的锚点训练对防止在线教师漂移至关重要，交错训练比种子模型本身更决定收敛。教师与锚点两轴不可分离：激进的教师选择仅在锚点稳定训练后才有效，而稳定所需程度取决于种子数据分散度及其与客户端数据的重叠，具有域依赖性。最终方案在11对中的9对超越此前最强方法，域内平均提升20.8%、跨域提升10.0%。

Conclusion: SSFL用于ASR训练时，教师与锚点两轴需协同设计：在线/过渡教师配合服务器轮间标注数据的交错训练可稳定并提升性能；所需稳定强度由种子数据分散度与客户端数据重叠度决定。这些指南使SSFL逼近全监督FL的性能上限。

Abstract: Semi-supervised federated learning (SSFL) trains models on clients' unlabeled data using a teacher to generate pseudo-labels, with a small labeled seed dataset on the server. Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is particularly fragile here: pseudo-label errors compound across the output sequence and across training rounds into divergence, leaving a large gap to fully-supervised FL. We show that closing this gap turns on two coupled design axes -- the teacher (which model generates the pseudo-labels) and the anchor (the server-side updates on labeled data that stabilize training). On the teacher axis, a per-client online teacher (each client's own evolving model) diverges on its own, but once stabilized it matches or beats the broadcast global teacher (one server model, fixed within a round) -- decisively in-domain and competitively under domain shift. As the seed grows stronger and the online teacher's advantage narrows, a transitioning teacher (global $\rightarrow$ online at round $r$) matches or beats both. On the anchor axis, the server must keep training on labeled data between rounds -- otherwise the online teacher drifts -- and this interleaving, more than the seed model, governs convergence. The two axes are inseparable: aggressive teacher choices pay off only once the anchor stabilizes training, which is highly sensitive to data augmentation and batch size -- the settings that govern how much input and gradient noise the server injects. How much stabilization is needed is domain-dependent, governed by the dispersion of the seed data and its overlap with client data. These findings yield guidelines for SSFL in ASR training, improving over the strongest prior method on 9 of 11 pairs, by $20.8\%$ on average in-domain and $10.0\%$ cross-domain, narrowing the gap to fully-supervised FL.

</details>


### [102] [Predictive Uncertainty for Neural CAE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25430)
*Kaustubh Tangsali,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Kelvin Lee,Carmelo Gonzales,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究几何条件神经代理模型在CAE中的不确定性量化方法，对比了高斯过程、MC dropout和深度集成三种方法在三个工业级数据集上的表现，发现各方法在误差排序和区间覆盖上有用，但相对性能随数据集和评价标准变化，UQ方法需依据下游CAE决策选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理模型能大幅加速CAE工作流，但在设计中应用需要对随几何形状、空间预测场和工程关注量变化仍有效的可靠不确定性估计，而现有UQ方法在几何条件神经代理上的适应性尚未充分评估。

Method: 对比一种闭式方法（基于高斯过程）和两种采样方法（具体MC dropout与深度集成），将其适配到几何条件神经代理上；在外部空气动力学（DrivAerStar、AirFRANS）和碰撞动力学（汽车碰撞）三个大规模工业相关的CAE数据集上进行评估；从不确定性量级可信度、误差位置识别、对陌生输入的响应以及对派生工程量的信息性等维度检验，并采用基于验证的重标定来调节区间覆盖率。

Result: 在DrivAerStar上三种方法均倾向于给预测误差较大的位置分配更高不确定性，基于验证的重标定使区间覆盖率在不相交的分布内测试集上接近名义值；AirFRANS和汽车碰撞数据集也显示有用的误差排序和区间估计，但方法的相对性能随数据集和评价准则而变化。

Conclusion: UQ方法和评价指标应根据预期下游CAE决策来选取，不存在放之四海而皆准的最优方法。

Abstract: Neural surrogates can substantially accelerate computer-aided engineering (CAE) workflows, but their use in design requires uncertainty estimates that remain meaningful across varying geometries, spatial prediction fields, and engineering quantities of interest. We investigate how established uncertainty quantification (UQ) approaches behave when adapted to geometry-conditioned neural surrogates. We compare one closed-form and two sampling-based approaches-a Gaussian process (GP)-based method, concrete Monte Carlo (MC) dropout, and deep ensembles-and evaluate them on three large, industry-relevant CAE datasets for external aerodynamics and crash dynamics.
  We examine whether predicted uncertainties have credible magnitudes, identify locations with larger prediction errors, respond to unfamiliar inputs, and remain informative for derived engineering quantities. On the DrivAerStar dataset, where all three methods are compared, each generally assigns higher uncertainty to locations with larger prediction errors, and validation-based rescaling brings interval coverage close to nominal on a disjoint in-distribution test set. Results on AirFRANS and automotive crash also show useful error ranking and interval estimates, but the relative performance of the methods changes with the dataset and evaluation criterion. UQ methods and evaluation metrics should therefore be selected based on the intended downstream CAE decision.

</details>


### [103] [Continuous Optimization for p-adic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25501)
*Julian Salazar,Dimitri Kanevsky,Matt Harvey,Pascal Getreuer,Lucas Dixon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出在 p-adic 参数上实现原生、连续梯度下降的机器学习方法，通过 Berkovich 仿射线这一路径连通扩张克服 Q_p 的全不连通性，使连续优化与反向传播成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p-adic 数 Q_p 全不连通，标准损失在远离极小值处平坦，导致现有原生优化器只能做离散的组合搜索；作者希望实现连续优化，并处理 R 上线性模型无法表达的 XOR 类任务及 Martins (2025) 提出的开放问题。

Method: 借助 Q_p 的 Berkovich 仿射线——一个保持其等距映射、并唯一延拓其解析映射的路径连通扩张（度量为树），在拥有可解释点与局部导数的结构上构造梯度下降、动量与 Adam 变体，并实现反向传播。

Result: 梯度下降的近似能有效学习系数在 Q_p 中的线性模型，完成模运算（一种 R 上线性模型无法表达的 XOR 类任务）；同时演示了动量、Adam、线性回归以及在二元编码层级结构（Quillian 语义网络）上的分类。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了 p-adic 机器学习中连续优化的难题，回应了 Martins (2025) 提出的开放问题，并开源了库（github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml）。

Abstract: We present the first method for native, continuous gradient descent for machine learning models with $p$-adic parameters. Existing native optimizers are discrete, mostly combinatorial searches, as the $p$-adic numbers $\mathbb{Q}_p$ are totally disconnected, with standard losses that are flat away from their minima. To enable continuous optimization, we propose working with $\mathbb{Q}_p$ via its Berkovich affine line: a canonical, path-connected expansion of $\mathbb{Q}_p$ that preserves its isometries and uniquely extends its analytic maps. This hull is a metric tree with interpretable points and local derivatives, which we show enables effective optimizers and backpropagation. We formulate gradient descent and show that its approximations efficiently learn linear models with coefficients in $\mathbb{Q}_p$ to do modular arithmetic, an XOR-like task not expressible by linear models in $\mathbb{R}$. We also demonstrate momentum and Adam variants, linear regression, and classification on binary-encoded hierarchies (Quillian semantic networks), addressing open problems posed by Martins (2025). Library at https://github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml

</details>


### [104] [Lightweight Ranking Heads: Accelerating Multi-Task Experimentation in Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25433)
*Sanjay Surendranath Girija,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Yanhao Jiang,Shawn Andrews,Lukasz Heldt,Yi Wu,Aditya Mahajan,Mohit Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lightweight Ranking Heads（Light Heads）框架，用于在生产级多任务排序模型中动态注入新任务，避免了模型冷启动和骨干网络重训，将多任务实验周期从数周缩短至数天，并已在 YouTube 规模成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生产级推荐系统依赖复杂的多任务排序模型，引入新任务时常面临三类瓶颈：(1) 与已有任务产生负向任务冲突的风险；(2) 重训骨干模型和下游模型带来高昂成本；(3) 调优奖励组合公式导致开发与实验周期漫长。核心挑战在于提升实验迭代速度。

Method: 提出 Light Heads 框架，面向持续在线学习环境设计：利用 stop-gradients（停止梯度）和 stateless daily training（无状态每日训练）实现严格的任务隔离，降低负向任务冲突风险；采用集中式配置使 Light Heads 可同时挂载到多个模型，加速训练数据生成和下游模型协同训练。文中详述了系统架构，并分析了无状态冷启动头的训练动态及其与完整头的性能对比。

Result: 该框架已成功部署于 YouTube 规模的生产环境，将多任务实验迭代周期从数周缩短至数天；Light Heads 使得新排序任务能够快速进行 A/B 实验并部署，并产出可量化的生产价值。

Conclusion: Light Heads 框架通过轻量级、隔离式的任务头设计，有效解决了多任务推荐系统中新任务引入的冲突与重训瓶颈，显著提升了实验速度，其工业级部署验证了该方法的可行性与实际价值。

Abstract: Modern production-scale recommender systems rely on complex, multi-task ranking models. Introducing new prediction tasks into these massive systems often causes bottlenecks - it risks negative task conflicts with existing tasks, and can lead to long development and experimentation cycles due to the expensive retraining of backbone models and downstream models or tuning of reward combination formulas. To address the critical challenge of slow experimentation velocity, we introduce the Lightweight Ranking Heads (Light Heads) framework. Designed for continuous online learning environments, Light Heads enable the dynamic injection of new tasks into existing multi-task ranking models, effectively obviating the need for model cold-starting and retraining of backbone models. By utilizing stop-gradients and stateless daily training, this design strictly isolates new tasks, mitigating the risk of adverse task conflicts. Crucially, this framework uses a centralized configuration that allows Light Heads to be added to multiple models simultaneously, unblocking faster training data generation and co-training of downstream models. Successfully deployed at YouTube scale, this approach reduces the iteration cycle for multi-task experimentation from several weeks to days. In this paper, we detail the system architecture, analyze the training dynamics of stateless cold-started heads, compare their performance to full heads, and demonstrate how Light Heads have enabled the rapid A/B experimentation and deployment of new ranking tasks that yield measurable production value.

</details>


### [105] [PermuFormer: Multi-Task Pretraining for Permutation Representation in Algebraic Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25438)
*Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PermuFormer，一个在大规模多任务、多编码语料（28 亿 token）上预训练的自回归 Transformer，用于代数组合学中排列相关任务，并在多种下游任务上超越从头训练的同构模型、MLP 基线及规模相近的微调通用语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学 AI 领域对前沿推理大语言模型关注较多，但窄领域专用模型仍是重要组成部分。专用模型通常直接在数学对象（如图、数列）上训练，而非文本描述。然而从头训练专用模型的常见做法可能限制了其学习"领域感知"表征的能力，无法充分捕捉数学的多面性。

Method: 提出 PermuFormer：一个在 28 亿 token 的多任务、多编码语料上预训练的自回归 Transformer，专注于代数组合学中的排列任务。预训练后在下游基础任务（预训练未见过的）和更复杂的研究级任务上进行微调，并与相同架构从头训练、基线 MLP、规模相近的微调通用语言模型对比。此外分析了模型内部机制，如不同任务可否从提示的内部表征线性解码、是否需要多轮生成后才能解码答案。

Result: PermuFormer 是微调基础任务和研究级任务的有效起点，经常优于从头训练的相同架构、基线 MLP 以及规模相近的微调通用语言模型。内部机制分析表明：部分任务可直接从提示的内部表征线性解码答案，而其他任务需要多轮生成后才能解码。

Conclusion: 多样化的预训练是学习可复用、领域感知表征的有效方法，可作为下游任务微调的起点。PermuFormer 证明了针对代数组合学排列任务的专用模型预训练能够显著提升性能，并揭示了模型内部求解任务的不同机制。

Abstract: Diverse pretraining has been shown to be an effective method for learning reusable, domain-aware representations that provide a starting point for fine-tuning on downstream tasks. While much of the excitement in AI for math has been concentrated in the use of frontier reasoning models to solve well-specified problems through the medium of language, narrow, specialized models remain an important component of the AI for math ecosystem. In contrast to large language models, specialized models are usually trained directly on the mathematical objects themselves (e.g., graphs, sequences of numbers) rather than the textual descriptions that characterize these objects. However, the common practice of training specialists from scratch may limit their ability to develop domain-aware representations that capture the multifaceted nature of mathematics. In this paper, we describe an approach to pretraining for permutation-focused tasks in algebraic combinatorics. We introduce PermuFormer, an autoregressive transformer trained on a 2.8 billion token multi-task, multi-encoding corpus. We show that PermuFormer is an effective starting point for fine-tuning on basic tasks unseen during pretraining and more complex research-level tasks, frequently outperforming the same architecture trained from scratch, baseline MLPs, and a fine-tuned generic language model of comparable size. We also analyze some of the internal mechanisms by which PermuFormer learns to solve training tasks. For example, we show that while some tasks can be linearly decoded directly from the internal representation of the prompt, other tasks require multiple rounds of generation before the answer can be decoded.

</details>


### [106] [Efficient Cost-Aware LLM Evaluation via Bayesian Bandit Gittins Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25645)
*Qian Xie,Yueli He,Nairen Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GittinsEval，一种面向 LLM 配置选择的成本感知贝叶斯 bandit 方法，基于贝叶斯最优的 Gittins 策略决定下一步评测哪个配置及何时停止，能以 1%–2% 的穷举成本获得近零简单遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大量候选 LLM 配置与基准测试项上穷举评测以寻找高性能配置成本高昂，因此需要一种成本高效、能在线决定评测对象并自适应停止的配置选择策略。

Method: 将配置选择建模为成本感知的贝叶斯 bandit 问题，提出 GittinsEval：采用贝叶斯最优的 Gittins 策略决定下一个评测的配置及停止时机；扩展该策略，利用 LCB 风格评分考虑后验不确定性，实现在完全评测与部分评测配置上的任意时刻推荐规则。方法在离线预计算后仅需轻量在线更新，计算高效。

Result: 在 GSM8K、PIQA、AlpacaEval 和 MMLU 响应矩阵上，GittinsEval 持续具有竞争力：在大样本基准上显著优于配置级贝叶斯优化，在大候选任务上优于成本无关的 bandit 基线。GittinsEval 常以 1%–2% 的穷举评估成本达到近零简单遗憾，自适应停止规则通常在 1%–10% 成本时触发。

Conclusion: GittinsEval 是一种高效且鲁棒的 LLM 配置选择方法，凭借贝叶斯最优策略与成本感知停止机制，以极小评测成本实现高质量配置推荐，优于现有贝叶斯优化与成本无关 bandit 基线。

Abstract: Exhaustively evaluating every candidate LLM configuration on every benchmark item to identify a high-performing one is costly. We formulate configuration selection as a cost-aware Bayesian bandit problem and propose GittinsEval, which draws on the Bayesian-optimal Gittins policy to determine which configuration to evaluate next and when to stop. We extend the policy with an anytime recommendation rule over both fully and partially evaluated configurations, using an LCB-style score to account for posterior uncertainty. GittinsEval is computationally efficient, requiring only lightweight online updates after offline precomputation. Across GSM8K, PIQA, AlpacaEval, and MMLU response matrices, GittinsEval is consistently competitive, with particularly strong gains over configuration-level Bayesian optimization on large-example benchmarks and over cost-unaware bandit baselines on large-candidate tasks. Crucially, GittinsEval often attains near-zero simple regret using only 1% to 2% of the exhaustive-evaluation cost; it also offers an adaptive stopping rule that typically triggers at 1% to 10%.

</details>


### [107] [Mean Velocity Matching: Rethinking Generative Dynamics in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25444)
*Yunhong Zhang,Changjie Cao,Zhihua Zhang,Bingli Liu,Zongjie Cao,Zongyong Cui,Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出均值速度匹配（MVM），通过单个学习场直接参数化扩散模型中的随机逆向动力学，无需单独估计或重建分数，并引入√t缩放参数化解决t→0附近回归目标无界的问题，在ImageNet上取得有竞争力的FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于速度的生成模型虽能学习单一输运场，但其标准形式是确定性的；随机扩展通常需要额外的分数信息或中间的速度到分数重建。作者希望保留单场预测能力，同时直接支持随机逆向动力学。

Method: 提出均值速度匹配（MVM）：构造一个高斯扰动过程，使面向恢复的速度 (x₀-x_t)/t 的条件期望直接构成逆向SDE的漂移项，从而单个学习场即可参数化随机逆向过程。为应对直接回归该速度在t→0附近无界的问题，引入√t缩放参数化以保持逆向动力学并得到有界训练目标。同一学习场还可导出确定性概率流ODE，统一研究随机与确定性采样。

Result: 基于Transformer的生成模型在ImageNet 32×32上达到给定NFE下的FID（\MVMImageNetThirtyTwoFID），在ImageNet 256×256上达到给定NFE下的FID（\MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixFID）。受控SDE-ODE对比显示：在极低NFE下ODE表现更优，而在函数评估次数充足时随机逆向过程获得更低FID。

Conclusion: MVM提供了对随机逆向动力学的直接单场参数化，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量，并统一了随机与确定性采样框架。

Abstract: This work studies prediction parameterization for stochastic generative dynamics in diffusion models. Existing velocity-based generative models provide the simplicity of learning a single transport field, but their standard formulation is deterministic, whereas stochastic extensions generally require additional score information or an intermediate velocity-to-score reconstruction. To retain single-field prediction while directly supporting stochastic reverse dynamics, this paper introduces Mean Velocity Matching (MVM). MVM constructs a Gaussian perturbation process for which the conditional expectation of a restoration-oriented velocity, $(x_0-x_t)/t$, directly forms the reverse-SDE drift. Consequently, a single learned field is sufficient to parameterize the stochastic reverse process without separately estimating or reconstructing the score. Because direct regression of this velocity becomes unbounded near $t=0$, MVM further introduces a $\sqrt{t}$-scaled parameterization that preserves the reverse dynamics while yielding a bounded training target. The same learned field also induces a deterministic probability-flow ODE, enabling stochastic and deterministic sampling to be studied within a unified formulation. Experiments with Transformer-based generative models achieve an FID of $\MVMImageNetThirtyTwoFID$ at \MVMImageNetThirtyTwoNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $32\times32$ and $\MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixFID$ at \MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $256\times256$. Controlled SDE--ODE comparisons further show that the ODE performs better under very low NFE, whereas the stochastic reverse process achieves lower FID when sufficient function evaluations are available. These results demonstrate that MVM provides a direct single-field parameterization of stochastic reverse dynamics while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Class Marginals: Bounding Rehearsal Gaps without Freezing Class Co-occurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25735)
*Congren Dai,Nat Roongjirarat,Fei Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究连续学习中类别重放的时间间隔（rehearsal gap，排练间隔）问题，提出随机遍次重放（RPR），在固定 C 个常驻类别、重放批大小 b ≤ C 的条件下，保证每个类在每次打乱遍历中恰好重放一次，从而保持时间平均的类别边际平衡，并将每个间隔上界约束为 2⌈C/b⌉−1。实验显示 RPR 相比独立平衡检索提升最终平均准确率 0.72–1.67 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别平衡重放只控制类别频率，而未确定同一类别相继重放之间的间隔。作者认为这一排练间隔与类别边际、类别共现相互独立，是影响连续学习中分类器偏差的重要时序因素，因此需要单独研究并显式控制。

Method: 提出随机遍次重放（RPR）调度器：在每次打乱（shuffled pass）中访问每个常驻类别一次；对固定 C 个常驻类别和重放批大小 b ≤ C，RPR 保持平衡的时间平均类别边际，并将每个排练间隔上界约束为 2⌈C/b⌉−1，同时在常驻集变化时给出依赖于类别更替（churn）的界。调度器不使用未来类别信息，也不增加重放样本或前向传递。通过线性头部 ER-ACE 诊断实验分析"类别同时缺席于输入批和重放批"所导致的单向分类器偏差梯度，并检验去除输入损失掩码对调度效应的影响。

Result: 在主要的 ER-ACE 实验中，RPR 相对于储蓄池（reservoir）存储下的独立类别平衡检索，将最终平均准确率提升 0.72–1.67 个百分点，在平衡存储下也观察到正向效果；预训练 ViT 在测试的 LT10 流和小重放批次上表现出正向效果，而匹配的较大批次对照则无实质影响。固定周期与复用遍次的对照改变了不止一个时序统计量，因此实验无法将排练间隔长度与其他时序依赖完全隔离。准确率效果取决于学习器和运行体制。

Conclusion: 排练间隔是独立于类别边际与类别共现的时序维度，RPR 通过每个打乱遍次访问一次常驻类别来显式约束间隔并保持边际平衡，能够在多种设置下提升持续学习准确率；但固定周期和复用遍次等对照无法单独隔离间隔长度的影响，效果依赖学习器与运行体制。

Abstract: Class-balanced replay controls class frequency but does not determine the interval between successive replay appearances of a class. We study this interval, the rehearsal gap, separately from the class marginal and class co-occurrence, and introduce randomised-pass replay (RPR), which visits each resident class once per shuffled pass. For a fixed set of C resident classes and replay batch size b less than or equal to C, RPR preserves the balanced time-averaged class marginal and bounds every gap by 2*ceil(C/b)-1; a churn-conditional bound applies while the resident set changes. The scheduler uses no future class information and adds no replay examples or forward passes. In a linear-head ER-ACE diagnostic, joint absence from the incoming and replay batches produces a one-sided classifier-bias gradient. Longer absence episodes are associated with larger negative bias displacement, and removing the incoming-loss mask attenuates the scheduling effect. In the primary ER-ACE experiments, RPR improves final average accuracy by 0.72-1.67 percentage points relative to independent class-balanced retrieval under reservoir storage, with positive effects also observed under balanced storage. Pretrained ViTs show positive effects on the tested LT10 streams with small replay batches, while matched larger-batch controls show no material effect. Fixed-cycle and reused-pass controls change more than one temporal statistic, so the experiments do not isolate rehearsal-gap length from all other forms of temporal dependence. The accuracy effects depend on the learner and operating regime.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Scalar Sensitivity: Activation-Aware Mixed-Precision LLM Quantization with Cross-Layer Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25916)
*Akihiro Yoshida,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出混合精度权重量化常用的标量敏感度代理（MCKP求解）会因忽略激活相关性而产生高达 $\sqrt{κ(A)κ(B)}$ 的乘性失真，使模块间敏感度排序不可靠；为此提出两阶段方法 CASA，用 Kronecker 分解 Hessian 的激活感知度量替代标量代理，并用跨层局部搜索优化位宽分配，在多个 LLM 与不同位预算下取得更低困惑度，尤其在超低位宽（<3 bit）下优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度量化把权重矩阵的 Hessian 压缩成单一标量并独立处理各模块，无法刻画激活相关的二次型结构，导致模块间敏感度排序失真，使基于标量代理的 MCKP 求解在 LLM 上不可靠。

Method: 提出两阶段方法 CASA：(1) 用 Kronecker-factored Hessian 推导激活感知度量替换标量代理，将 MCKP 化为连续松弛有闭式解的形式；(2) 用跨层感知的局部搜索，基于端到端模型损失评估位宽更新。

Result: 在多个 LLM 与不同位预算上，CASA 比最新标量代理基线取得更低困惑度，尤其在每权重 <3 bit 的超低位宽下优势明显；零样本精度增益与各模型模块的平均条件数相关，验证了失真上界可作为标量代理失效的实用指标。

Conclusion: 标量敏感度代理存在固有失真，CASA 通过激活感知度量与跨层局部搜索显著提升超低位宽混合精度量化的性能，且条件数可作为选取量化策略的实用判据。

Abstract: Mixed-precision weight quantization is commonly formulated as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), yet existing solvers rely on scalar sensitivity proxies that collapse each weight matrix's Hessian into a single number and treat every module independently. We prove that even the optimal scalar proxy incurs multiplicative distortion up to $\sqrt{κ(\mathbf{A})κ(\mathbf{B})}$ relative to the full activation-aware quadratic, where $κ(\mathbf{A})$ and $κ(\mathbf{B})$ denote the condition numbers of the input- and output-side Hessian factors. This bound varies from $10^1$ to $10^{13}$ for typical LLM modules, making inter-module sensitivity ranking unreliable. To address these limitations, we propose Cross-layer Activation-aware Sensitivity Allocation (CASA), a two-phase method. In Stage 1, the scalar proxy is replaced by an activation-aware metric derived from the Kronecker-factored Hessian, reducing the MCKP to a form whose continuous relaxation admits a closed-form solution. In Stage 2, a cross-layer-aware local search evaluates bit-width updates using the end-to-end model loss. Experiments on multiple LLMs across different bit budgets show that CASA achieves lower perplexity than the latest scalar-proxy baselines, especially at ultra-low bit-widths ($<3$ bits per weight). Moreover, the performance gain in zero-shot accuracy tracks the per-model average condition-number over modules, confirming the distortion bound as a practical indicator of scalar-proxy failure.

</details>


### [110] [Terminal Shrinkage Averaging Reveals a Schedule-Estimator Interaction in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25482)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"终端收缩平均（TSA）"，通过将原始最终迭代值与近期检查点平均值进行插值，在保持训练后期优化进度的同时降低返回模型的终端方差，从而分离学习率调度与模型估计器两个设计选择，并在NanoChat系列实验及时间到GPT-2基准中验证了收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练通常直接返回原始最终迭代值，这耦合了两个设计选择：生成参数轨迹的学习率调度与构造部署模型的估计器。促进优化进度的调度可能不同于最小化最终迭代值方差的调度，因此分离二者可在训练后期保持进度的同时降低返回模型的波动。

Method: 提出终端收缩平均（TSA），在原始最终迭代值与近期检查点平均值之间进行插值，以平衡近期进度与终端方差；并在局部二次近似下分析TSA如何改变最优终端学习率调度，通过一系列受控的NanoChat实验检验其相互作用。

Result: 组合调度与估计器在depth-22 NanoChat上提升了验证质量；一次用于定标的时间到GPT-2运行比实验中使用的公开基线完成得更快，提供了基准加速的初步证据。

Conclusion: 通过分离学习率调度与模型估计器，TSA能在训练后期维持优化进展的同时降低终端方差，其收益能够迁移到更大规模的模型，并初步展示出基准加速潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) pretraining conventionally returns the raw final iterate. This couples two design choices: the learning-rate schedule that generates the parameter trajectory and the estimator that constructs the deployed model (e.g. the raw final iterate or a checkpoint average). A schedule that promotes optimization progress may differ from one that minimizes variation in the raw final iterate. Separating these choices creates an opportunity to maintain progress late in training while reducing variation in the returned model. To this end, we propose \emph{Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)}, which interpolates between the raw final iterate and the average of recent checkpoints to balance recent progress against terminal variation. We analyze how TSA changes the preferred terminal learning-rate schedule under a local quadratic approximation and test this interaction through a sequence of controlled NanoChat experiments. Finally, we demonstrate that the resulting gains transfer to depth-22 NanoChat, where the combined schedule and estimator improve validation quality. A qualifying time-to-GPT-2 run also finishes faster than the public baseline used in our experiments, providing preliminary evidence of benchmark acceleration.

</details>


### [111] [xWhyL: Causal Interactive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26037)
*Nicholas Tagliapietra,Florian Peter Busch,Moritz Willig,Matej Zečević,Lavdim Halilaj,Juergen Luettin,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 xWhyL 框架，将可解释人工智能（XAI）与因果推理反向连接，即从人类解释中学习因果模型，并证明其能突破观测性因果发现的局限，同时通过"因果拔河"（Causal Tug-of-War）机制拒绝错误解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学早已表明，人类解释的驱动力本身就是学习因果的机制，但人工智能领域长期忽视了从溯因（abductive）信号中学习。XAI 通常用因果模型生成解释，却很少探索解释反过来能为因果学习做什么，即"解释为因果服务"这一方向基本空白。

Method: 作者提出 xWhyL 形式化框架，发展一套数学理论将解释转化为与观测数据互补的学习信号；在此基础上提出"因果拔河"概念处理解释与数据冲突的情形，并证明框架在何种条件下会拒绝错误设定（misspecified）的解释而非吸收它们。其实践实例为因果交互学习（CIL），展示专家解释如何高效支撑因果发现并区分正确与错误解释。

Result: 该框架能够突破仅依赖观测数据的因果发现的固有局限；数学上证明了可拒绝与数据冲突的错误解释的条件；因果交互学习（CIL）实例表明专家解释能高效支持因果发现并区分解释的正误。

Conclusion: 解释不仅能由因果模型生成（XAI 方向），还能反向作为学习信号帮助构建因果模型，二者形成互补闭环。通过形式化框架与因果交互学习，可以从解释中可靠地学习因果结构，并在解释与证据冲突时拒绝错误解释，从而开辟了连接因果推理与可解释 AI 的新方向。

Abstract: Explanations are central to causal reasoning, and cognitive science has long established that the human drive to explain is itself a mechanism for learning about causality. Despite this, learning from those abductive signals is largely ignored in artificial intelligence. While explainable AI (XAI) increasingly draws on causal models to generate explanations, the converse direction about what explanations can do for causality remains largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we propose xWhyL, a formal framework connecting causality and XAI by learning causal models from explanations. We develop a mathematical theory that translates explanations into a learning signal complementary to observational data, and demonstrate how it enables overcoming the limits of observational causal discovery. As explanations can be derived from incorrect beliefs and clash with data, a tension we call the Causal Tug-of-War, we prove conditions under which our framework rejects misspecified explanations rather than absorbing them. Our practical instantiation, Causal Interactive Learning (CIL), shows how expert explanations can efficiently support causal discovery and distinguish correct from incorrect explanations.

</details>


### [112] [Learning Defensive Policies against Diverse Inference Attacks for Smart Meter Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25484)
*Ruichang Zhang,Mustafa A. Mustafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于代理引导的分层强化学习框架，通过电池负载整形来防御智能电表数据上的非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）隐私推断攻击，在真实数据集上显著降低了攻击者的推断精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电表数据虽能细粒度反映家庭能耗，但暴露了用户隐私。NILM 攻击可从聚合信号中推断家电级信息并泄露敏感行为模式。实际中攻击者模型未知且异构，使得鲁棒防御极具挑战性。因此需要一种能在多样且未见过的攻击者面前泛化、降低家电级信息可恢复性的黑盒防御方法。

Method: 将智能电表隐私保护形式化为黑盒推断防御问题，提出代理引导的分层强化学习框架。该框架学习基于电池的负载整形策略，向聚合信号中注入真实但具有误导性的家电级签名，破坏 NILM 所利用的结构化模式。其中，自监督的聚合结构隐私探针以重建误差作为代理奖励来干扰可恢复的负载结构；签名库使扰动与家电相关且可通过电池控制物理实现。理论分析表明，代理引导优化在攻击者多样性下提升了推断鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实数据集 UK-DALE 和 REDD 上的实验显示出强大的跨模型与跨家电泛化能力。面对六个未见过的 NILM 攻击者（UK-DALE 上四种家电、REDD 上五种家电），所提防御平均将家电级 RMSE 分别提高 107% 和 166%，并将 F1 分数分别降低 79% 和 80%。

Conclusion: 代理引导的分层强化学习防御能够有效、鲁棒地保护智能电表数据免受多样化且未知的 NILM 推断攻击，在破坏家电级信息可恢复性的同时保证扰动的物理可实现性，并具备良好的跨模型与跨家电泛化性能。

Abstract: Smart meter (SM) data provides fine-grained visibility into household energy consumption, but also exposes users to privacy risks. Inference attacks, known as non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM), can perform appliance-level inference from aggregate signals and recover sensitive behavioral patterns. In practice, attacker models are unknown and heterogeneous, making robust defense challenging. We formulate SM privacy protection as a black-box inference defense problem, aiming to reduce the recoverability of appliance-level information while generalizing across diverse and unseen attackers. We propose a proxy-guided hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that learns battery-based load-shaping policies to inject realistic but misleading appliance-level signatures into the aggregate signal, thereby disrupting the structured patterns exploited by NILM. A self-supervised aggregate-structure privacy probe provides a reconstruction-error-based surrogate reward for disrupting recoverable load structure, while a signature library makes the perturbations appliance-relevant and physically realizable through battery control. We provide theoretical rationale showing that proxy-guided optimization improves inference robustness under attacker diversity. Experiments on real-world datasets UK-DALE and REDD demonstrate strong cross-model and cross-appliance generalization. Across six unseen NILM attackers, covering four appliances on UK-DALE and five on REDD, our proposed defense increases average appliance-level RMSE by 107% and 166%, respectively, while reducing F1 score by 79% and 80%.

</details>


### [113] [Hill Sampling for Test-Time Scaling: A Simple and Better Alternative to Repeated Sampling, Evolution, and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25510)
*Jacob Beck,Philip V. Ogren,Ari Kobren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hill Sampling，一种极简的测试时计算方法：从冻结 LLM 重复采样程序编辑，保留迄今最优程序并以此作为后续采样的条件，在多个算法问题上达到或超越复杂方法（如 AlphaEvolve），并发现测试时学习权重反而不如零学习率的进化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期系统通过复杂进化搜索框架或测试时训练更新参数来提升 LLM 在可验证科学/算法问题上的表现，作者质疑这些复杂机制是否必要，希望找到简单且有效的测试时计算分配策略。

Method: 提出 Hill Sampling：从冻结的 LLM 中反复采样候选程序编辑，保留迄今找到的最优程序，并把该程序作为后续所有采样的条件。在 circle packing、集合的和/差、Erdos 最小重叠问题三个任务上，用三个开放权重模型评估。此外，对直接作用于 LLM 权重的进化策略（ES）进行了以参数规模计的最大规模研究，并对比零学习率的 ES。

Result: Hill Sampling 在 circle packing 上取得已发表方法中的新 SOTA，在 Erdos 最小重叠问题上超越 AlphaEvolve 基准，在有限集合的和与差问题上也取得强结果；circle packing 和 Erdos 结果仅需在 8 块 H100 GPU 上运行数小时。进化策略直接学习权重的效果比把学习率设为零更差；零学习率下方法仍通过固定随机扰动在权重空间搜索，扰动有助于探索，但 token 采样的随机性更强，而重复采样仍明显弱于 Hill Sampling。

Conclusion: 建议采用简单测试时计算分配策略：先反复对迄今最优已验证解进行编辑采样，再考虑引入存档、多样性机制、进化框架或测试时参数学习等额外复杂度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can improve solutions to verifiable scientific and algorithmic problems by spending additional computation at test time. Recent systems achieve strong results with increasingly elaborate evolutionary search harnesses or by updating model parameters during test-time training. We ask how much of this machinery is necessary. We introduce Hill Sampling, a simple procedure that repeatedly samples candidate program edits from a frozen LLM, retains the best program found so far, and conditions all subsequent samples on that program. We evaluate the method on circle packing, sums/differences of sets, and Erdos' minimum-overlap problem using three open-weight models. Hill Sampling sets a new state of the art on circle packing among published methods, improves over the AlphaEvolve reference on Erdos' minimum-overlap problem, and achieves strong results on sums and differences of finite sets. The circle-packing and Erdos results require only hours of wall-clock time on eight NVIDIA H100 GPUs. To our knowledge, we also conduct, the largest study, by parameter count, of evolution strategies (ES) applied directly to LLM weights at test time. Surprisingly, learning the weights is worse than setting the ES learning rate to zero: at zero learning rate, the method is still searching in weight space through fixed random perturbations. Those perturbations can help exploration, but randomness from token sampling is stronger still, and repeated sampling remains substantially weaker than Hill Sampling. These results suggest a simple test-time compute allocation strategy: repeatedly sample edits to the best verified solution found so far, before introducing additional complexity such as adding archives, diversity mechanisms, evolutionary scaffolds, or test-time parameter learning.

</details>


### [114] [Gap-Free Streaming PCA Beyond Rank-One Updates: Near-Optimal Rates and Applications to Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26508)
*Anming Gu,Syamantak Kumar,Kevin Tian,Chutong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对 Oja 算法在无特征间隙的流式主成分分析（PCA）问题中给出新的理论分析，证明其可达到近最优误差率，并得到近乎匹配的下界；同时将结果推广到 Rayleigh 商意义下的近似 PCA，并应用于差分隐私 PCA，解决了相关猜想（至对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 PCA 旨在单次遍历数据流即可恢复主导谱子空间。此前达到近最优速率的算法要么依赖特征间隙假设，要么仅限于秩一更新，无法处理最一般、无特征间隙的情形。作者希望去除这些限制，并进一步应用于差分隐私 PCA 场景。

Method: 作者对 Oja 算法提出新的分析框架：仅利用每次随机更新（individual stochastic updates）的二阶矩界，绕过了此前近最优分析所需的几乎必然（almost sure）界以及对应的离线矩阵 Bernstein 界。在该证明框架下，无需任何特征间隙假设即可给出近最优误差率，并补充了一个近乎匹配的下界；同时将结论拓展到基于 Rayleigh 商的近似 PCA 定义。

Result: 在无特征间隙的最一般流式 PCA 设定下，Oja 算法被证明达到近最优速率，且下界与上界近乎匹配。作为主要应用，作者给出了亚高斯（sub-Gaussian）数据下无间隙的差分隐私 PCA 保证，从而在至多对数因子的意义上解决了 [Bro26] 的猜想 1.1。同时解决了 [JJK+16] 提出的关于 Rayleigh 商形式近似 PCA 的开放问题。

Conclusion: 通过新的二阶矩分析技术，作者成功将 Oja 算法的近最优保证推广到无特征间隙的一般情形，并延伸至 Rayleigh 商近似 PCA 和差分隐私 PCA。该工作填补了流式 PCA 在无间隙设定下近最优分析的空缺，并给出了与之匹配的下界。

Abstract: Streaming principal component analysis (PCA) seeks to recover a leading spectral subspace in a single pass over a data stream. We give a new analysis of the ubiquitous Oja's algorithm [Oja82] for the most general, gap-free variant of this problem, where no eigengap assumptions are made on the underlying mean matrix, complemented by a nearly-matching lower bound. Prior works achieving near-optimal rates for streaming PCA either required gap assumptions [JJK+16, HNWW21], or were limited to rank-one updates [AZL17, Lia23]. Our proof only uses a second moment bound on the individual stochastic updates, bypassing the almost sure bounds needed by prior near-optimal analyses, and the analogous offline matrix Bernstein bound. We also extend our result to a Rayleigh quotient notion of approximate PCA, addressing an open question of [JJK+16]. As our main application, we give gap-free differentially private PCA guarantees for sub-Gaussian data, settling Conjecture 1.1 of [Bro26] up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [115] [A JEPA Recipe for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25541)
*Mingyu Jeon,Suwan Cho,Jae Young Suh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究在表格基础模型的先验之上，将世界模型自监督（联合嵌入预测架构 JEPA）的潜在项与值预测目标联合训练。此前潜在项易坍缩并拖垮编码器；作者提出一种配方使潜在项存活至收敛，但 JEPA 臂在 147 个真实数据集上仍逊于纯值目标臂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型通过上下文预测单元格值进行学习，而世界模型自监督要求表征空间的预测（LeCun 2022; Assran 2023）。作者尝试把两者的目标结合，但在早期实验中 JEPA 的潜在项坍缩，进而使编码器退化为常数映射。作者想知道在何种条件下联合训练能让潜在项存活，并评估其相对纯值目标的综合表现与代价。

Method: 提出一种训练配方：值头读取编码器字段（而非预测器）的输出；目标采用指数移动平均（EMA）差值。两个训练臂（JEPA 与 value-only）都以平台期停止规则终止，不设固定步数预算。收敛后各取一次运行，在 147 个真实数据集上评测分类与回归，并按数据集名称唯一化、按层（stratum）和基准（benchmark）统计胜负，同时比较达到平台期所需的步数与墙钟时间。

Result: 在收敛时，JEPA 臂落后于纯值臂：分类胜负比 32:70（按数据集名称唯一化后为 29:63），回归 8:24；分类差距较小、回归差距较大，且在各层与各基准中胜负倾向一致。JEPA 臂达到平台期所需的步数是纯值臂的 1.42 倍，墙钟时间为其 1.66 倍。此前固定步数预算会将值臂仍在持续提升的减速误判为性能天花板。

Conclusion: 即便在使潜在项存活至收敛的配方下，联合 JEPA 训练仍未能超越纯值目标，反而更慢、更贵且在各维度普遍落后。文中暗示"预测表征"这一世界模型式自监督方向在当前表格基础模型先验下相对值预测并不占优。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models learn to predict cell values in context, whereas world-model self-supervision asks for prediction in representation space (LeCun, 2022; Assran et al., 2023). On a tabular foundation-model prior, the latent term of a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) collapsed in our earlier runs and took the encoder with it to a constant map. We report a recipe under which the latent term survives to convergence beside the value objective: the value head reads the encoder field rather than the predictor, and the target is an exponential moving average (EMA) difference. To bound its cost against the value-only arm, both arms train until a plateau rule stops them, with no fixed step budget. A fixed horizon had confounded a slowdown with a ceiling, since the value-only arm was still improving well past the usual budget. At convergence, in one run per arm, the JEPA arm trails the value-only arm across 147 real datasets, 32:70 wins to losses on classification (29:63 with one entry per dataset name) and 8:24 on regression, the margin small on classification and wider on regression, and the count leans the same way in each stratum and each benchmark. The JEPA arm (jepa) needs 1.42 times as many steps as the value-only arm (ds), and 1.66 times its wall-clock, to reach its plateau.

</details>


### [116] [DefaultGNN: A Dual-Perspective GNN Framework for Predicting Corporate Default from Buyer-Seller Transaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25542)
*Junghoon Kim,Hyunsung Kim,Seungyoon Choi,KyoungYong Park,Jihun Lee,YongGu Ji,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DefaultGNN，一种基于图神经网络的双视角企业违约预测框架，利用真实电子税务发票数据构建买方视角与卖方视角交易网络，实证发现交易驱动风险具有角色依赖性和规模依赖性，并显著提升违约预测性能与信贷审批率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信用模型依赖财务报表，而许多企业财务报表稀疏或缺失；企业交易网络能反映真实经济活动，但风险如何通过买卖关系传播尚缺乏深入探索。作者希望利用交易网络补充并改善企业违约预测。

Method: 基于六年真实电子税务发票数据，将交易历史与违约事件关联，进行大规模实证研究；据此构建多路复用的买方视角与卖方视角交易网络，并提出DefaultGNN双视角图神经网络框架，融合两种视角建模风险在交易关系中的流动。

Result: DefaultGNN相较属性型与图型基线取得显著提升，尤其对内在风险信号有限的企业效果更佳；可提供可视化、可解释的网络级解释；与持牌信用评级机构合作验证，DefaultGNN的预测能补充现有信用评分模型，在获批企业违约风险不升高的前提下将审批率提高7-11个百分点。

Conclusion: 交易网络中的风险传播具有角色依赖性与规模依赖性，双视角图神经网络能有效建模交易驱动风险，显著改善企业违约预测，并在真实信贷场景中与现有评分模型互补，带来可观的审批率提升，且已开源代码。

Abstract: Corporate default prediction is a core problem in financial risk management, yet traditional credit models rely heavily on financial statements that are often sparse or unavailable for many firms. Corporate transaction networks offer a complementary view of real economic activity, but how risk propagates through buyer-seller relationships remains underexplored. We conduct a large-scale empirical study using real-world electronic tax-invoice data spanning six years that links transaction histories with default events, revealing that transaction-driven risk is both role-dependent (buyer or seller) and scale-dependent. Based on these findings, we construct multiplex buyer-view and seller-view transaction networks and propose DefaultGNN, a dual-perspective graph neural network-based framework for corporate default prediction. DefaultGNN integrates both views to model how risk flows through transactional relationships, achieving strong improvements over both attribute-based and graph-based baselines, especially for firms with limited intrinsic risk signals. We further provide interpretable network-based explanations by visualizing how distressed trading partners contribute to default risk. In collaboration with a licensed credit rating agency, we validate that DefaultGNN's predictions complement existing credit scoring models, improving approval rates by 7-11%p without increasing default risk among approved firms. The source code can be found at https://github.com/jhkim611/DefaultGNN

</details>


### [117] [SambaGraph: Action-Reaction Spatio-Temporal Graphs for Soccer Tactical Response Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25569)
*Abel A. Reyes-Angulo,Henry O. Velesaca,Steven Araujo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SambaGraph——一个基于2022世界杯64场比赛追踪与事件数据构建的动作-反应时空图数据集与基准，用于足球战术响应建模，包含4,070个动作中心片段、26,270对攻防配对，并系统评估了响应分类、防守检索与LLM推理三个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球战术具有交互性：一次进攻动作会改变对手的防守问题，而观测到的防守响应取决于多智能体比赛状态。现有方法缺乏能够同时刻画攻击与防守双方、并支持双向（动作-反应）联合建模的时空图资源，因此需要构建可复现的图基准来推动足球战术响应研究。

Method: 从2022年FIFA世界杯全部64场比赛的追踪与事件数据中，构建4,070个以动作为中心的片段，表示为时间对齐的23节点（球员+球）图序列，含攻防双视图、响应标签及26,270对分割安全的攻防配对。基线包括：紧凑签名MLP、融合图-签名的双编码器、难负例采样，以及本地LLM的零样本分类与候选重排序。

Result: 签名MLP在响应分类上取得0.796±0.007的macro-F1；图-签名双编码器在全库防守检索中达到Hit@5=0.471±0.029、Hit@10=0.655±0.051；难负例能最大化配对判别力但未提升检索质量；本地LLM在直接分类上弱于监督编码器，在八候选重排序中未能超越强的原始排序，但能提供有依据的战术解释。

Conclusion: SambaGraph为基于图的足球策略-响应研究提供了可复现的基准，揭示了图结构表征在战术响应分类与防守检索中的价值，同时表明当前LLM在直接战术决策上仍不及专用监督模型，但在可解释推理方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Soccer tactics are interactive: an attacking action changes the opponent's defensive problem, and the observed response depends on the multi-agent match state. We introduce SambaGraph, an action--reaction spatio-temporal graph dataset and benchmark for soccer tactical response modeling. From tracking and event data for all 64 matches of the 2022 FIFA World Cup, we curate 4,070 action-centered episodes represented as temporally aligned 23-node player--ball graph sequences with attack/defense views, response labels, and 26,270 split-safe attack--defense pairs. We study three questions: whether observed responses can be classified from graph episodes, whether successful defenses can be retrieved for a query attack, and whether graph-derived summaries support grounded LLM reasoning. A compact signature MLP obtains $0.796\pm0.007$ macro-F1 for response classification, while a fused graph--signature dual encoder reaches $0.471\pm0.029$ Hit@5 and $0.655\pm0.051$ Hit@10 for full-bank defensive retrieval. Hard negatives maximize pair discrimination but not retrieval quality. Local LLMs underperform supervised encoders for direct classification and do not improve over a strong original order in eight-candidate reranking, but they provide grounded tactical rationales. These results position SambaGraph as a reproducible benchmark for graph-based soccer strategy-response research. Code and dataset are available at: https://github.com/areyesan/SambaGraph.

</details>


### [118] [EMGBlend: Heterogeneity-Aware Self-Supervised Pretraining for Gesture and Force Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25582)
*Yuwei Jia,Cheng Zhong,Jinyang Yu,Zhe Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EMGBlend，一种自监督框架，通过通道补丁共享、几何感知注意力、频带受限目标与数据源均衡，将 11 个异构公开表面肌电数据集统一预训练一个 109M 模型，在多种下游任务上优于随机初始化与波形重建基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开表面肌电（sEMG）数据集在电极布局、通道数、频率支持与规模上差异巨大，直接混合预训练会导致通道语义错位、引入部分设备无法观测的频谱目标，并使大数据集或高通道数据集主导学习，因此需要针对这些差异进行显式机制设计。

Method: 提出 EMGBlend 自监督框架：①共享通道补丁（shared channel patches）结合几何感知注意力（geometry-aware attention）以对齐跨布局的通道语义；②将频谱目标限制在每个记录所支持的频带内（band-aware targets）；③对数据源做均衡采样（source balancing）。在 11 个公开 EMG 数据源上预训练一个 109M 参数模型。

Result: EMGBlend 在手势识别、连续力回归和接触分类上一致优于匹配的随机初始化与波形重建对照组；固定预算的数据源对照表明多源预训练提升手势识别，并在力解码上与竞争基线相当；消融实验证实几何信息、频带感知目标和数据源均衡各自对迁移有贡献，但跨被试 NinaPro 力估计仍然困难。

Conclusion: EMGBlend 展示了如何通过显式机制设计而非简单拼接来组合异构 EMG 数据集，实现跨设备、跨任务的有效预训练迁移，代码已开源。

Abstract: Public surface electromyography (EMG) datasets vary widely in electrode layout, channel count, frequency support, and size. Simply mixing them for pretraining can misalign channel semantics, introduce spectral targets that some devices cannot observe, and let large or high-channel-count datasets dominate learning. We introduce EMGBlend, a self-supervised framework designed around these differences. It combines shared channel patches with geometry-aware attention, restricts spectral targets to each recording's supported frequency band, and balances exposure across data sources. We pretrain a 109M-parameter model on 11 public EMG sources and evaluate it on gesture recognition, continuous-force regression, and contact classification. EMGBlend consistently outperforms matched random initialization and waveform reconstruction controls. Fixed-budget source controls show that multi-source pretraining improves gesture recognition and remains competitive for force decoding. Ablations confirm that geometry, band-aware targets, and source balancing each contribute to transfer, although cross-person NinaPro force estimation remains difficult. Overall, EMGBlend shows how heterogeneous EMG datasets can be combined through explicit mechanism design rather than simple concatenation. Code is available at https://github.com/tamanano/EMGBlend

</details>


### [119] [Rewired or Gated? How Instruction Tuning Shapes Knowledge-Conflict Circuits in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25602)
*Shubham Santosh Pandere,Gautam Ranka,Ritika Varshney,Navya Deshmukh,Roushni Sareen,Roshan Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对基础模型与指令微调模型的“提示词-权重冲突消解电路”进行机制级对比，发现指令微调并未重建电路，而是对既有的、位于相同高层且可识别的一组注意力头进行门控/重加权；行为上微调让模型更偏向参数记忆（拒绝简短反事实上下文），但该“怀疑”依赖表述框架，对连贯的证据性段落无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调会改变模型在“相信提示词”还是“相信权重”冲突下的行为，但尚不清楚这种改变是重接了底层电路，还是仅仅门控/重加权了原本就存在的组件。缺乏基座-指令模型间冲突消解电路的系统性机制对比，阻碍可解释性与控制工具从基座模型向部署模型的迁移。

Method: 在三个模型家族（Llama-3.2-3B、Qwen-2.5-3B、Gemma-3-4B）上，综合运用五种独立方法：节点归因、边归因、叠加态角色分析、因果消融与路径修补（path patching），对冲突消解电路进行机制级定位与对比；并辅以行为实验（简短反事实上下文 vs. 连贯证据性段落）检验微调带来的行为偏移。

Result: 五种方法一致指向“门控”而非“替换”：相同的注意力头、相同的后期层在微调后被重加权，节点重叠度高达0.60-0.82。行为上，微调使模型更偏向参数记忆，指令模型拒绝简短反事实上下文的程度远高于基座模型——这与“更听话”的朴素预期相反；但当同一虚假陈述以连贯、有证据的段落呈现时，这种额外怀疑消失，说明其鲁棒性真实但范围狭窄。

Conclusion: 指令微调并未重建冲突消解电路，而是保留并重加权原有组件，因此针对基座模型校准的可解释性与控制工具应能直接迁移到部署的指令模型；同时，微调抵御简短注入的鲁棒性是真实但狭窄的，并高度依赖表述框架。

Abstract: In language models, the choice between believing the prompt and believing the weights is made by a handful of identifiable attention heads. Instruction tuning changes how models behave under conflict, but whether it rewires the underlying circuit or merely gates/reweights already present components, remains unknown. We provide the first mechanistic base-vs-instruct comparison of conflict-resolution circuits, across three families (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen-2.5-3B, Gemma-3-4B). Five independent methods, node and edge attribution, superposition role analysis, causal ablation, and path patching, converge on gating, with the same heads, in the same late-layers, are found to be reweighted rather than replaced with a high node overlap (0.60-0.82). Behaviorally, tuning shifts models toward parametric memory, making instruct models reject a terse counterfactual context far more than base ones, the opposite of a naive user-following expectation. Yet this added skepticism is a factor of framing since it disappears when the same false claim is delivered as a coherent, evidential passage. The robustness that instruction tuning buys against terse injection is therefore real but narrow. More broadly, we believe that because the conflict circuit is preserved rather than rebuilt, interpretability and control tools calibrated on base models should transfer directly to their deployed instruct siblings.

</details>


### [120] [What Should a Self-Teacher See? Privileged Context Design for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25623)
*Kanghui Tian,Siyuan Liu,Tianxiang Jiang,Shuai Dong,Yizhuo Li,Tian Ding,Yuan Guo,Songze Li,Haowen Hou,Congcong Wang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在自蒸馏中，教师模型并非信息越多越好；用更抽象、更精简的中间上下文（如命名策略、方法无关框架、问题类别）替代完整解题路径，可在数学竞赛任务上取得与完整解相当甚至更好的成绩，同时大幅减少提示token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"更多特权信息=更好教师"的直觉假设：在policy自蒸馏中，默认特权上下文是包含最终答案与某条推理路径的完整参考解，但这可能提供了学生无法有效利用的冗余信息。作者希望探究在不改变学生视图与训练设置的前提下，教师提供何种抽象层级的信息最能提升学生性能，并在不同规模和任务下考察其稳定性。

Method: 采用on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）框架，冻结基座模型副本作为教师，对学生的rollout在特权上下文下打分。在每个规模内固定学生视图与训练设置，比较默认的完整参考解与三种离线编译的抽象上下文（命名策略、方法无关框架、问题类别），以及一个仅保留答案、移除路径的对照组。在竞赛数学上进行主实验，并跨三个随机种子对比，同时考察教师-学生KL散度与下游性能的关系。

Result: 主实验中，最佳中间上下文相比完整解的域内峰值均值提升：4B规模+1.4分、8B规模+1.6分，且仅需约十分之一的提示token。跨三个种子的比较显示框架与类别上下文在两个规模上均有正向平均增益。仅答案条件在主实验中保持竞争力，与完整解差距在0.2分以内。偏好上下文随学生规模与任务而变化；初始教师-学生KL并不能预测下游性能。

Conclusion: 自教师应看到的不是它所能知道的全部信息，而是其学生仍能据此行动的抽象层级。更少但更合适的特权信息反而能造就更好的教师，且最优抽象粒度取决于学生规模与任务。

Abstract: More privileged information does not always make a better teacher. We study this tension in on-policy self-distillation (OPSD), where a frozen copy of the base model scores the student's own rollouts under privileged context, conventionally a complete reference solution that bundles the final answer with one particular reasoning path. Holding the student view and training fixed within each scale, we compare that default against three abstractions compiled offline, a named strategy, a method-independent framing, and a problem category, and against an answer-only control that keeps the destination but removes the path. In the primary runs on competition mathematics, the best intermediate contexts improve the in-domain peak mean over the full solution by 1.4 points at 4B and 1.6 at 8B, while storing an order of magnitude fewer hint tokens. Comparisons across three seeds also show positive mean gains for the framing and category contexts at both scales. Answer-only conditioning remains competitive in the primary runs, within 0.2 points of the full solution at these scales. The preferred context varies with student scale and task. Initial teacher-student KL does not order downstream performance. What a self-teacher should see is therefore not everything it could, but the level of abstraction its student can still act on.

</details>


### [121] [An Exploratory Replica-Overlap Probe of the Grokking Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25634)
*A. C. Opus,J. Q. Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究训练了 64 个独立种子网络（四种配置），发现预注册的"注册探针"因对齐步骤存在缺陷（仅置换隐藏单元而未同步置换偏置和头内部参数，不保函数）且数值精度不达标，导致预注册规则无法给出结论（UNDETERMINED，原因码 C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID），既未证实零假设，也未有效读出 Parisi 序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 RSB 启发的成对权重重叠分布是否在 grokking 转变中发生变化，并试图通过预注册的注册探针（基于对齐的权重重叠 q_wt 与函数重叠 q_fn）对这一假设给出可验证的判定。

Method: 训练 64 个独立种子网络（四种配置）直至持续收敛或 40,000 epoch 上限；对通过 12/16 检查点完备性要求的 frac40 配置，采用 Hartigan 双峰检验（dip test）分析重叠标准差的 Delta dip 区间，并计算重叠标准差的变化倍数；进行事后校准评估 dip 检验在模拟分离度下的功效；比较 q_wt（经对齐计算）与 q_fn（由未置换模型预测计算）的差异。

Result: 预注册规则给出 UNDETERMINED（C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID）；只有 frac40 配置通过 12/16 检查点完备性要求。其事后标准给出 Hartigan-dip 区间包含零（95% CI 为 [-0.017, 0.034]），但重叠标准差增大约 5.6 倍；事后校准显示 dip 检验在模拟分离度下功效为零，故该区间无信息量。标准差比值是唯一在观测效应下具有功效的统计量。集成损失仅在预设 1% 阈值下接近平坦。grokking 发生率（0/16、11/16、16/16）因训练分数与分组身份混杂，仅具描述性。

Conclusion: 预注册的注册探针存在根本性缺陷（对齐不保函数、数值精度不足），无法对 RSB 假设给出有效判定；现有数据既不能作为确证性零结果，也不能视为 Parisi 序参量的有效读数。事后分析中仅标准差比值具有功效，而 dip 区间无信息量；grokking 率被混杂因素限制，只能作描述性解读。

Abstract: We trained 64 independently seeded networks in four configurations, continuing each to sustained convergence or a 40,000-epoch ceiling. We then asked whether an RSB-inspired distribution of pairwise weight overlaps changes across the grokking transition. It is the alignment step, not the overlap statistic, that determines what this registered probe can report. The registered implementation permutes hidden units without the corresponding bias and head-internal permutations and therefore does not preserve the network function. Every q_wt value computed through this alignment inherits the defect; q_fn does not, because it is computed from predictions of the unpermuted models. The numerical-precision requirement also failed, and an audit found protocol deviations. Consequently, the pre-registered rule gives no verdict: registered outcome UNDETERMINED (reason code C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID). These data provide neither a confirmatory null nor a validated reading of the Parisi order parameter. Only frac40 cleared the 12/16 checkpoint-completeness requirement. For this configuration, a post-hoc criterion applied to the same data gave a Hartigan-dip interval containing zero (95% CI for Delta dip = [-0.017, 0.034]), whereas the overlap standard deviation increased by a factor of about 5.6. A post-hoc calibration assigns the dip test zero power at the simulated separations; the interval is therefore uninformative, not evidence of no change. The standard-deviation ratio is the only statistic here with power at the observed effect. Ensemble loss was near-flat only under the pre-specified 1% threshold. Finally, grokking rates of 0/16, 11/16 and 16/16 remain descriptive because train fraction is confounded with split identity.

</details>


### [122] [From Experts to Sub-experts: Fine-grained Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25655)
*Zhentao Tan,Chang Liu,Yao Liu,Yue Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NSFT（Neural Sub-expert Fine-Tuning），一种面向 MoE 大模型的细粒度参数高效微调框架，将专家内部的中间通道分解为子专家组，并结合路由重要性与激活显著性选择任务相关的子专家进行更新，在更少可训练参数下取得优于 LoRA 和专家级稀疏微调的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型规模激增，全参数微调成本过高，MoE 等稀疏模块化架构兴起。现有 PEFT 方法（如 LoRA）在预定义权重矩阵上操作，专家级稀疏微调则更新整个被选中的专家；但作者观察到被激活的专家内部本身是稀疏的，只有少量中间通道对下游任务有强响应，说明专家级更新仍过于粗糙，需要更细粒度的参数选择。

Method: NSFT 沿中间维度将每个专家分解为结构化的通道组（子专家），通过组合路由重要性（routing importance）与专家内部的激活显著性（intra-expert activation saliency）来选择任务相关的子专家；为优化稀疏部分更新，引入学习率缩放（learning-rate scaling）和动态梯度缩放（dynamic gradient scaling），以弥补有效更新幅度的降低。

Result: 在 OLMoE 和 Ling-mini-2.0 上，针对具有挑战性的领域特定任务和通用基准的实验表明，NSFT 一致优于代表性的 PEFT 基线和专家级稀疏微调基线，同时使用显著更少的可训练参数，并保持了有竞争力的通用能力。

Conclusion: 子专家级（sub-expert-level）适配是 MoE 大模型更精确、更高效的 PEFT 范式。NSFT 通过细粒度选择和稀疏更新机制，实现了参数效率与性能的更好平衡。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) scale rapidly, dense full-parameter adaptation becomes increasingly expensive, motivating sparse and modular architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. This shift raises a key question for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT): at what granularity should parameters be selected and updated? Existing PEFT methods such as LoRA operate on predefined weight matrices, while expert-level sparse tuning methods update entire selected experts. However, we observe that activated experts are internally sparse, with only a small fraction of intermediate channels strongly responding to downstream tasks, indicating that expert-level adaptation is still too coarse. We propose NSFT (Neural Sub-expert Fine-Tuning), a fine-grained PEFT framework that refines MoE adaptation from experts to sub-experts. NSFT decomposes each expert along the intermediate dimension into structured channel groups and selects task-relevant sub-experts by combining routing importance with intra-expert activation saliency. To optimize sparse partial updates, NSFT further introduces learning-rate scaling and dynamic gradient scaling to compensate for the reduced effective update magnitude. Experiments on OLMoE and Ling-mini-2.0 across challenging domain-specific tasks and general benchmarks show that NSFT consistently outperforms representative PEFT and expert-level sparse tuning baselines, while using substantially fewer trainable parameters and preserving competitive general capability. These results suggest that sub-expert-level adaptation is a more precise and efficient PEFT paradigm for MoE LLMs.

</details>


### [123] [Targeted Review for AI-Assisted Biodiversity Surveys: Active Continuous-Score Occupancy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25657)
*Timm Haucke,Lauren Harrell,Justin Kay,Mary Clapp,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ACORN 方法，将机器学习预测融入占域模型，并主动选择对下游生态分析最有信息量的样本供专家复核，从而以更少的人工审查达到接近全人工标注的生态结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家日益依赖机器学习为科学数据集打标签，但模型必然存在错误，尤其系统性偏差会传播到科学结论中。因此科学家通常需要复核大量 ML 标签。现有做法多以提高分类器精度为目标分配审查资源，而非服务于下游科学推断，导致在审查预算有限时效率低下。

Method: 提出 Active Continuous-Score Occupancy Modeling (ACORN)：将 ML 预测（连续分数）直接纳入占域模型（occupancy model），并根据其对下游生态分析的信息增益，策略性地挑选最值得专家复核的样本，而非随机或均匀抽样复核。

Result: 在相机陷阱（camera-trap）与生物声学（bioacoustic）数据集上，ACORN 恢复出的生态结论接近完全人工标注数据所得结论，且所需专家复核数量显著少于非针对性（non-targeted）复核策略。

Conclusion: ML 辅助的科学工作流应针对下游推断而非仅针对分类器精度来优化专家投入，尤其在人工审查预算有限时。代码已开源。

Abstract: We increasingly use machine learning to label scientific datasets. The models we develop and deploy are improving all the time, but they are not and will likely never be perfect. Mistakes matter, as errors can propagate into our scientific understanding, particularly when systematically biased. Very reasonably, scientists thus review substantial proportions of ML-generated labels to verify or correct mistakes in pursuit of ensuring their scientific findings are not biased by ML. In this work, we focus on helping scientists optimally allocate this reviewing effort relative to their scientific goals. We focus on a specific class of scientists (ecologists) and a specific, widespread, and impactful modeling target (occupancy modeling, which estimates where species are likely to occur, conditioned on environmental factors). We introduce Active Continuous-Score Occupancy Modeling (ACORN), a method that incorporates ML predictions into occupancy models and strategically selects samples for expert review that are maximally informative for downstream ecological analysis. Across camera-trap and bioacoustic datasets, our method recovers ecological conclusions close to those obtained from fully human-labeled data, while requiring substantially fewer expert reviews than non-targeted review policies. Our results suggest that ML-assisted scientific workflows should optimize expert effort for downstream inference, rather than for classifier accuracy alone, especially when human review budget is limited. Our code is available at https://github.com/timmh/acorn

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [What Does Chain-of-Thought Entropy Measure? A Channel Audit of Scaffolding, Routing, and Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25039)
*Marios Papamichalis,Regina Ruane*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将思维链(chain-of-thought)生成中的词汇划分为"脚手架(scaffold)"子集与"实质内容(content)"子集，从而精确分离next-token分布中的三种选择（是否输出连接性脚手架、选择哪个连接词、给出何种实质续写），并据此重新分析熵、KL散度及熵速度等统计量。结果表明原始与内容两种约定在判断"分叉位置"时存在系统分歧，内容通道结合泄漏项的界能刻画答案多样性，且在压缩与token打分任务中内容约定优于原始意外性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链生成中的熵等统计量混杂了"是否输出连接性脚手架、选择哪个连接词、实质续写是什么"三重选择，导致基于原始分布的统计量（如高熵token定位、策略梯度裁剪、塌缩检测）可能被脚手架噪声污染；现有做法缺乏对脚手架与内容两个通道的形式化分离，使得对关键现象（分叉位置、答案多样性、token重要性打分）的测量产生偏差。

Method: 作者指定一个"脚手架词汇"子集，将softmax策略的next-token分布精确分离为内容通道与脚手架通道，对熵、KL散度、一阶熵速度逐一建立精确分解；证明原始(raw)与内容(content)两种约定在某一显式开区域上对"哪个位置是更大的分叉"判断不一致；用"内容通道加泄漏项"给出答案多样性的界，并用实测证据(witness)对泄漏项进行certify；在23种配置、匹配tokenizer阶梯、数学语料步骤与蒸馏过程等设置下做实验，并用单通道相关性进行闭式预测。

Result: 在23种配置中，脚手架侧承载了高达41%的raw高熵集合；在匹配tokenizer阶梯上，耦合仅在数学语料步骤发生变化，而脚手架的熵占比在蒸馏过程中持续增长；单通道相关性的闭式预测在54点范围内将选择保留率跟踪到5个点以内（未拟合）。在压缩任务中，内容约定在每个单元格都优于raw意外性；答案泄漏审计修正了作者自身的头号控制组——回喂的链有四分之一到一半的准确率来自复述答案，剥离后没有任何token打分器优于随机连续块。

Conclusion: 将脚手架与内容通道分离，能消除思维链统计量中由连接性脚手架带来的系统性偏差；raw与content约定会在分叉判断上实质性地不一致，答案多样性可被内容通道加泄漏项所界限。内容约定在压缩与token打分上更可靠，而忽视答案泄漏会高估基于token的打分方法。作者呼吁在思维链推理分析中显式建模脚手架/内容分离。

Abstract: Entropy over chain-of-thought tokens decides which tokens receive the policy gradient, which get pruned, and whether a run has collapsed, yet each such statistic reads a next-token distribution mixing three choices: whether to emit connective scaffolding, which connective, and what the substantive continuation should be. Designating a scaffold vocabulary subset separates the three, exactly, for entropy, Kullback--Leibler divergence, and the first-order entropy velocity of a softmax policy. We prove the raw and content conventions disagree about which position is the larger fork on an explicit open region, and bound answer diversity by the content channel plus a leakage term a measured witness certifies. Across twenty-three configurations the scaffold side carries up to 41% of the raw high-entropy set; on a matched-tokenizer ladder, coupling changes only at the math-corpus step while the scaffold's entropy share keeps growing through distillation; a closed-form forecast from one channel correlation tracks selection retention over a 54-point range to five points, unfitted. On compression, the content convention beats raw surprisal in every cell; an answer-leakage audit then corrects our own headline control: re-fed chains earn a quarter to a half of their accuracy from restated answers, and once stripped, no token scorer beats a random contiguous block.

</details>


### [125] [FREESIA: Covariance-Aware Posterior Transport for Expressive and Scalable Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25085)
*Shiwei Ni,Yangwen Zhang,Hang Qi,Xiaofei Guan,Lili Ju*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无训练、渐近精确的后验传输方法，用于在高维稀疏观测条件下推断非线性/非单射观测算子诱导的多模态后验。方法结合协方差感知的后验传输方案与观测自适应提议及后验校正，并建立后验流理论证明渐近精确性与Wasserstein误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据同化需要在稀疏、高维观测下准确推断复杂动力系统的状态；现有集合滤波受限于强分布假设，而无训练的生成式滤波（EnSF、EnFF）虽放宽假设，却可能引入结构误差并在稀疏观测下阻碍信息传播，难以准确刻画非线性/非单射观测算子诱导的多模态后验。

Method: 提出一种无训练、渐近精确的后验传输方法：(1) 设计协方差感知的后验传输方案，将预报交叉协方差嵌入基于流的传输中，在保留非高斯后验结构的同时准确恢复未观测状态；(2) 结合易处理的观测自适应提议与后验校正，实现对非线性后验分布的准确逼近；(3) 建立后验流理论，推导该方法相对于有限集合代理的渐近精确性及Wasserstein误差界。

Result: 在Double-Well、Lorenz-96和Kolmogorov流上的实验表明，该方法能捕捉复杂后验结构，并在稀疏、非线性、非单射观测下保持准确；在稀疏非单射场景下，RMSE较最佳基线降低56%。

Conclusion: 所提出的无训练后验传输方法有效解决了非线性/非单射观测下多模态后验的准确推断问题，在稀疏观测条件下显著优于现有集合滤波和无训练生成式滤波基线，兼具理论保证与实证性能。

Abstract: Data assimilation aims to infer the state of complex dynamical systems based on observational data. However, accurate inference of the multimodal posteriors induced by nonlinear or non-injective observation operators remains a key challenge under high-dimensional and sparse observation conditions. Ensemble filters scale to high dimensions but are confined by restrictive distributional assumptions, while training-free generative filters (e.g., EnSF, EnFF) alleviate this limitation but may introduce structural errors and hinder information propagation under sparse observations. To address these issues, we propose a training-free, asymptotically exact posterior transport method. Firstly, a covariance-aware posterior transport scheme is designed, which embeds the forecast cross-covariance into flow-based transport and accurately recovers unobserved states while preserving the non-Gaussian posterior structure. Furthermore, the method combines a tractable observation-adaptive proposal with posterior correction, ensuring accurate approximation of the nonlinear posterior distribution. Finally, we establish the corresponding posterior flow theory, from which the asymptotic exactness of the proposed method relative to finite-ensemble surrogates and the Wasserstein error bound are derived. Experiments on Double-Well, Lorenz-96, and Kolmogorov flow show that the proposed method captures complex posterior structure and remains accurate under sparse, nonlinear, and non-injective observations. In the sparse non-injective setting, it reduces RMSE by 56% relative to the best baseline.

</details>


### [126] [Variational objectives for amortized Bayesian inference in inverse problems: The role of posterior conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25145)
*Abhishek Srivastava,Arijit Hazra,Rajesh Dubbaku*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文比较了三种变分自编码器（VAE）目标——VAE-KL、VAE-JS 与 VAE-JSWA——在贝叶斯反问题上的摊销后验推断性能，发现目标选择的效果强烈依赖于后验的条件数（病态程度），并据此提出几何自适应的变分推断方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE 为反问题提供了高效的摊销贝叶斯推断方法，但当反问题存在弱可辨识（weakly identified）参数方向时，后验精度会显著依赖于变分正则项的选择。作者希望厘清不同变分目标在后验病态程度不同情形下的行为差异，从而为实践中的目标函数选择提供依据。

Method: 文章考察三种目标：逆向 KL 的 VAE-KL、非对称 Jensen–Shannon 的 VAE-JS，以及用平方 2-Wasserstein 距离替换逆向 KL 正则项、同时保留正向 KL 后验监督的 VAE-JSWA。采用全协方差高斯编码器和预训练的物理代理模型进行摊销后验推断，并在广义 Fisher 基下做局部线性–高斯分析以刻画三种目标的方差相关梯度。先在具有解析后验的线性–高斯基准上验证，再在非线性物理反问题（线性 ODE 反问题及两个 PDE 约束问题）上测试。

Result: 在良态（well-conditioned）基准中，VAE-KL 略优于其他两者，但三种方法的后验近似相当；在强病态基准中，VAE-JSWA 的后验误差显著更低。非线性物理问题呈现类似的条件数依赖趋势：后验病态程度越高，基于 JS 的目标收益越大。

Conclusion: 后验条件数是选择变分目标的重要影响因素，不同目标在良态与病态问题上的优劣会发生反转。这推动作者提出面向贝叶斯反问题的几何自适应变分推断这一未来的研究方向。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) offer an efficient approach to amortized Bayesian inference for inverse problems, but posterior accuracy can depend strongly on the choice of variational regularization, particularly when the inverse problem contains weakly identified parameter directions. This study investigates three objectives: a reverse Kullback--Leibler formulation (VAE-KL), an asymmetric Jensen--Shannon formulation (VAE-JS), and a Jensen--Shannon--Wasserstein formulation (VAE-JSWA), which replaces the reverse Kullback--Leibler regularizer with the squared 2-Wasserstein distance while retaining forward-Kullback--Leibler posterior supervision. A full-covariance Gaussian encoder and a pre-trained physics-based surrogate are used for amortized posterior inference. A local linear--Gaussian analysis in the generalized Fisher basis is developed to characterize the variance-dependent gradients of the three objectives. The formulations are first evaluated using linear--Gaussian benchmarks with known posterior solutions and subsequently tested on nonlinear physics-based inverse problems, including an inverse problem governed by a linear ODE and two PDE-constrained problems. VAE-KL performs slightly better than the other formulations in the well-conditioned benchmark, where all three approaches yield comparable posterior approximations, whereas VAE-JSWA provides substantially lower posterior errors in the strongly ill-conditioned benchmark. The nonlinear physics-based problems exhibit a similar conditioning-dependent trend, with JS-based formulations providing greater benefit as posterior ill-conditioning increases. These results indicate that posterior conditioning is an important factor in selecting variational objectives and motivate geometry-adaptive variational inference for Bayesian inverse problems.

</details>


### [127] [Empirical Auditing of Edge-Private Graph Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25155)
*Anum Fatima,Stratis Limnios,James Adams,Lukasz Szpruch,Carsten Maple,Gesine Reinert,Andrew Elliott*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过统计有效的隐私损失下界，实证审计边缘相邻输入输出是否可区分，并比较直接边、局部结构与 GNN 三类攻击，揭示隐私泄露取决于机制与网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图隐私研究多依赖局部统计量或理论分析，缺乏对实际可区分性的实证审计，尤其是学习型表示是否泄露传统局部统计无法捕捉的信息。

Method: 构建一个审计框架，利用边缘相邻输入的输出可区分性，给出统计有效的隐私损失下界；在目标边周围的几何结构中比较三类攻击：直接边攻击、局部结构攻击和基于 GNN 的攻击；在两种生成机制与两种网络上开展实验。

Result: 隐私泄露同时依赖机制与网络；学习型表示泄露了传统局部统计量未能捕捉的信息。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了对图隐私泄露的实证审计方法，表明仅靠传统局部统计不足以评估隐私风险，需考虑机制、网络结构及学习型表示的影响。

Abstract: We empirically audit privacy leakage by testing whether outputs from edge-neighbouring inputs remain distinguishable, using statistically valid lower bounds on the privacy loss witnessed by our attacks. Our framework compares direct-edge, local-structural, and GNN-based attacks through the geometry surrounding a target edge. Experiments across two generators and two networks show that privacy leakage is both mechanism- and network-dependent, with learned representations revealing information not captured by conventional local statistics.

</details>


### [128] [Penalized Nonreversible Langevin for Constrained Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25381)
*Pervez Ali,Weihao Dong,Xiaoyu Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出带惩罚的非可逆Langevin算法，用于从紧致凸集约束的目标分布π(x)∝e^{-f(x)}1_C(x)中采样，通过平方距离惩罚结合斜对称扰动保持惩罚Gibbs分布，并给出非渐近误差界及非可逆加速的理论与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧致凸集上从约束分布中采样是一类基础问题，现有方法（如投影或拒绝采样）常面临效率或收敛性问题。作者旨在设计能保持惩罚后目标的非可逆动力学，并利用斜对称扰动实现非可逆加速，从而提升约束采样算法的收敛速度。

Method: 提出罚化非可逆Langevin算法：以平方距离惩罚逼近约束集，并叠加常数或兼容的状态依赖斜对称（skew-symmetric）扰动，这些扰动保持惩罚后的Gibbs分布不变。对光滑（可能非凸）的f，在log-Sobolev不等式下推导全梯度算法的非渐近总变差界；对无偏随机梯度，在全局收缩与Lipschitz条件下于适配二次度量中建立2-Wasserstein界。并在二维二次模型中通过将斜对称扰动调谐到惩罚引起的曲率不平衡，证明非可逆加速。

Result: 对固定惩罚参数，误差相对惩罚Gibbs分布以指数速度衰减到O(√η)邻域（η为步长），并给出惩罚Gibbs分布与约束目标之间的偏差界。在二维二次模型中，固定目标精度和较小曲率、初始Wasserstein距离一致有界时，调谐斜对称扰动将Euler迭代的充分迭代次数界从关于曲率比的线性改进为对数级。

Conclusion: 所提罚化非可逆Langevin算法在各类约束采样任务上有效，理论保证其指数收敛到步长决定的邻域，并通过曲率失衡调谐实现非可逆加速；数值实验在约束贝叶斯回归、分类、神经网络及截断采样上验证了算法性能与加速机制。

Abstract: We propose penalized nonreversible Langevin algorithms for sampling from $π(x)\propto e^{-f(x)}\mathbf 1_{\mathcal C}(x)$, where $\mathcal C\subset\mathbb R^d$ is a compact convex set. The algorithms combine a squared distance penalty with constant or compatible state dependent skew symmetric perturbations that preserve the penalized Gibbs distribution. For smooth, possibly nonconvex $f$, we derive nonasymptotic total variation bounds for the full gradient algorithm under a log Sobolev inequality. When unbiased stochastic gradients are available, we establish $2$-Wasserstein bounds under global contraction and Lipschitz conditions on the full drift in an adapted quadratic metric. For a fixed penalty parameter, the error relative to the penalized Gibbs distribution decays exponentially to an $\mathcal{O}(\sqrtη)$ neighborhood, where $η$ is the stepsize. We also bound the discrepancy between the penalized Gibbs distribution and the constrained target. In a two dimensional quadratic model, we establish nonreversible acceleration by tuning the skew perturbation to the curvature imbalance induced by penalization. With the target accuracy and smaller curvature fixed and initial Wasserstein distances uniformly bounded, tuning the skew perturbation improves the sufficient Euler iteration bound from linear to logarithmic in the curvature ratio. Numerical experiments evaluate the algorithms on constrained Bayesian regression, classification, neural networks, and truncated sampling, and examine the acceleration mechanism in a stochastic quadratic model.

</details>


### [129] [PICPIs: Prediction-Interval-Conditional Prediction Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25388)
*Xuelin Yang,Baihe Huang,Yilong Hou,Guido Imbens,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出预测区间条件预测区间（PICPI），一种基于自洽条件 E[Y | p(X)∈I]∈I 的新区间，既能定义预测值分层又能校准该层均值，构造算法在正则条件下覆盖率任意接近1且宽度以 n^{-1/3} 速率收敛，并拓展至概率预测与多分类场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计中"对何种可观测量作条件"问题。标准共形预测的边缘有效性在决策所依赖的预测值上分辨率有限，而对协变量的完全条件保证已被证明不可实现，故需要一个介于两者之间、以预测值为条件的新框架。

Method: 引入预测区间条件预测区间（PICPI），定义为满足自洽条件 E[Y | p(X)∈I]∈I 的区间 I，其中 p 为预测模型、X 为上下文协变量、Y 为结果。区间同时作为预测值分层并保证该层均值落在同一区间内，给出实际构造算法，并将其推广到概率预测与多分类分类的推断程序。

Result: 在预测分布满足正则性条件下，构造出的区间可覆盖除任意小比例外的所有预测值，宽度以 n^{-1/3} 速率（至多对数因子及预测误差）递减；识别出的局部校准区间可反哺下游决策。实证结果与现有基于区间的基线方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: PICPI 提供了一种数据自适应的、以预测值为条件的校准区间，弥合了边缘有效性与不可实现的全条件保证之间的空白，兼具理论速率保证与对下游决策的实用价值。

Abstract: A classical question in statistics is which observable quantities to condition on when drawing inferences about unobservable targets. For conformal prediction in nonparametric uncertainty quantification, standard marginal validity offers limited resolution at the prediction values on which decisions are based, and fully conditional guarantees with respect to the covariates are provably unattainable. We address this gap by introducing a prediction-based conditioning framework that we refer to as Prediction-Interval-Conditional Prediction Intervals (PICPIs). Formally, a PICPI is an interval $I$ satisfying a self-consistency condition: $$\mathbb{E} [Y \mid p(X) \in I] \in I,$$ for predictive model $p$, contextual covariate $X$, and outcome $Y$. Thus, an interval simultaneously defines a stratum of prediction values and certifies that the mean outcome in that stratum lies in the same interval. This self-consistency condition yields data-adaptive strata without altering the original prediction. Such intervals can be constructed using practical algorithms. Under regularity of the prediction distribution, the constructed intervals cover all but an arbitrarily small fraction of prediction values and have widths that decrease at rate $n^{-1/3}$, up to logarithmic factors and the prediction error. Moreover, identifying these locally calibrated intervals can, in turn, inform downstream decision-making. We derive inference procedures for PICPIs in probabilistic prediction and multi-class classification, accompanied by theoretical guarantees. Empirical results are provided that compare PICPIs with existing interval-based baselines.

</details>


### [130] [Scalable Minimum-Volume Simplex Estimation with Non-asymptotic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25576)
*Jun LI,Yanlong Guo,Zhaozhao Zeng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究从海量均匀采样点中估计 K 维单纯形（混合模型）的问题，提出深度隐式网络方法 DeepMVSA，将最小体积原则表示为神经隐式形式，使可训练内存降到 O(K^2)（与 N 无关），每次数据遍历代价为 O(NK^2)，并给出非渐近样本复杂度界、oracle 不等式、端到端误差预算与下界，在 N 达 1e8 的合成数据及 1e7 像素的真实场景上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多项式时间估计器要么每次样本需要立方级计算，要么需要 O(NK) 存储，在 N 达到 1e6–1e8 量级时不可行；因此需要一种内存与计算开销可控、可扩展至海量数据的最小体积单纯形估计方法。

Method: 提出 DeepMVSA：用轻量坐标网络生成混合权重，用三角 LU 型参数化表示对偶单纯形矩阵，将最小体积原则重新表达为神经隐式形式。由此训练状态内存降为 O(K^2)（不依赖于 N），单次数据遍历成本降为 O(NK^2)。理论上针对局部化的代理估计器证明了与多项式时间基准同阶的非渐近样本复杂度界；对神经目标的每个全局极小点给出 oracle 不等式（含体积膨胀控制与显式收缩偏差）；在显式包络事件上给出条件端到端误差预算，分离统计、近似、优化与包络残差四项；并证明两点下界：对任意独立于 N 的固定噪声水平 σ>0，N^{-1/2} 的标度在其 N 指数上不可改进。

Result: 在最多 N=1e8 的合成观测上的实验与预测精度和标度一致；并在约 1e7 像素的真实场景上验证了可行性。

Conclusion: DeepMVSA 通过神经隐式重构最小体积估计，实现了内存独立于样本数、单次遍历线性于 N 的可扩展算法，兼顾理论保证（样本复杂度、oracle 不等式、误差预算与下界）与大规模实验验证，为海量数据的单纯形估计提供了实用方案。

Abstract: We study the estimation of a $K$-dimensional simplex from $N$ i.i.d.\ points sampled uniformly from its interior; the observations are convex combinations of $K+1$ unknown prototypes. Existing polynomial-time estimators need cubic per-sample work or $O(NK)$ storage and are impractical at $N\sim 10^6$--$10^8$. We propose DeepMVSA, which re-expresses the minimum-volume principle in neural implicit form: a lightweight coordinate network generates the mixing weights and a triangular LU-type parameterization the dual simplex matrix, reducing the trainable-state memory to $O(K^2)$, independent of $N$, and the cost per data pass to $O(NK^2)$. We prove a non-asymptotic sample-complexity bound of the polynomial-time benchmark order for a localized surrogate estimator; an oracle inequality for every global minimizer of the neural objective, with volume-inflation control and an explicit shrinkage bias; a conditional end-to-end error budget separating statistical, approximation, optimization, and enclosure-residual terms on an explicit envelope event; and two-point lower bounds: at any noise level $σ>0$ fixed independently of $N$, the $N^{-1/2}$ scaling is unimprovable in its $N$-exponent. Experiments with up to $N=10^8$ synthetic observations are consistent with the predicted accuracy and scaling, and feasibility on real scenes of $\sim 10^7$ pixels is demonstrated.

</details>


### [131] [Generalized Deep Regression for Repeated Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25605)
*Kexuan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在具有重复二值、计数或连续响应且单元间存在未观测随机均值函数的边际回归估计中，使用 ReLU 深度神经网络进行拟合，建立了 oracle 不等式并给出收敛速率、集成推断理论与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在独立单元内存在重复测量的数据中，单元间的依赖由未观测的随机均值函数驱动，传统非参数回归方法难以有效分离条件测量变异与单元间变异，且缺乏针对此类相关结构的高维神经网络的严格统计理论。

Method: 采用 ReLU 深度神经网络，以凸的广义回归损失进行拟合；通过分离条件测量变异与单元间变异建立 oracle 不等式；利用 n 个单元、每单元 m 次测量的结构推导积分均方误差速率；对不等簇大小建立加权 oracle 不等式；对点式集成推断给出投影中心极限定理，并在显式渐近线性条件下证明无穷小折刀（infinitesimal jackknife）的一致性。

Result: 在 β-Hölder 类上，ReLU 网络可获得积分均方误差阶 n^{-1}+(nm)^{-2β/(2β+d)}（至对数因子）；对不等簇大小得到加权 oracle 不等式；对组合光滑函数也给出相应速率；集成推断方面建立了投影中心极限定理及无穷小折刀的一致性。

Conclusion: 所提方法为带重复测量和单元内随机均值的边际回归提供了坚实的理论保证，明确了 ReLU 网络在相关簇数据下的收敛速率与推断性质，并通过模拟和真实数据验证了理论发现与实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the estimation of a marginal regression function from independent units with repeated binary, count, or continuous responses using ReLU deep neural networks. In the model, we assume that the dependence is generated by an unobserved random mean function within each unit. We then fit a neural network with a convex generalized regression loss. We show an oracle inequality by separating conditional measurement variation from between-unit variation. In addition, we prove that with $n$ units and $m$ measurements per unit, ReLU networks can attain an integrated mean squared error of order $n^{-1}+(nm)^{-2β/(2β+d)}$, up to logarithmic factors, over $β$-Hölder classes. We also derive a weighted oracle inequality for unequal cluster sizes and a rate for compositionally smooth functions. For pointwise ensemble inference, we give a projection central limit theorem and prove infinitesimal jackknife consistency under an explicit asymptotic linearity condition. Simulations and real data examples are provided to support our theoretical findings and practical implications.

</details>


### [132] [On the Gradient Heterogeneity Dynamics of Adversarially Robust Federated Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25705)
*Leonardo F. Toso,James Anderson,Nirupam Gupta,Rafael Pinot*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在联邦学习中，不依赖先验的梯度差异假设，而是从每轮使用新鲜样本的线性与非线性回归统计模型出发，直接推导出梯度异质性界，并将其分解为诚实客户端真实模型参数的异质性、有限样本标签噪声和初始化三部分，从而证明在特定鲁棒聚合器条件下联邦学习可实现收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习分析通常通过梯度差异条件来控制统计异质性与对抗行为的交互，但该界是先验强加的，即使对最小二乘回归也可能给出保守的保证。作者希望直接从统计模型推导梯度异质性，以获得更紧致、更符合实际的收敛保证。

Method: 作者从线性与非线性回归的统计模型出发，在每轮使用新鲜数据样本的设定下，直接推导梯度异质性的界，而非依赖先验的梯度差异假设。进一步将该界分解为三项：诚实客户端真实模型参数之间的异质性、有限样本标签噪声、以及初始化差异。随后，针对 (f,κ)-鲁棒聚合器（其系数 κ=O(f/n)，f 为对抗客户端数，n 为客户端总数，且 f/n<1/2），证明在显式的样本 burn-in 阶段之后收敛成立。
