Result: 作者成功将梯度异质性从统计模型中显式推导出来，并将其分解为参数异质性、标签噪声和初始化三部分；证明了当鲁棒聚合器系数满足 κ=O(f/n) 且 f/n<1/2 时，联邦学习在经过一个显式样本 burn-in 阶段后可以收敛。

Conclusion: 通过从回归统计模型直接推导梯度异质性，可以避免传统保守的梯度差异假设，并给出更精确的界；在满足相应鲁棒聚合条件下，联邦学习能够在显式的样本 burn-in 后实现收敛，从而更好地刻画异构与对抗场景下的联邦学习行为。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is intrinsically heterogeneous: honest clients may have different data-generating models. On top of that, adversarial clients can make heterogeneity even more pronounced by sharing arbitrary updates. Existing analyses typically control the interaction between statistical heterogeneity and adversarial behavior through gradient-dissimilarity conditions. However, the underlying bound is imposed a priori and may yield conservative guarantees even for least-squares regression. We instead derive the gradient heterogeneity from the statistical model of linear and nonlinear regression with fresh data samples at every round. Our bounds separate heterogeneity among the honest clients' ground-truth model parameters, finite-sample label noise, and initialization. We then demonstrate that, for any $(f,κ)$-robust aggregator with coefficient $κ= O(f/n)$, where $f$ is the number of adversarial clients and $n$ the total number of clients (with $f/n < 1/2$), convergence holds after an explicit sample burn-in.

</details>


### [133] [Optimal Tradeoffs Between Network Size and Parameter Magnitude in Neural Approximation and Minimax Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25710)
*Baicheng Li,Zuowei Shen,Haizhao Yang,Shijun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了固定深度下神经网络宽度与参数幅值之间的精确权衡：用单一有界1-Lipschitz的Dyadic-Triangular激活函数，在单位β-Hölder球上给出最优L^p逼近误差阶[N^2 log(eNT)]^{-β/d}，并证明相应下界对任意固定全局Hölder激活成立；在此基础上，深度23的裁剪类上的近似最小二乘在无对数损失下达到经典Hölder极小极大风险，且存在从单位参数半径到固定网络规模的连续统计最优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的统计精度既取决于其逼近能力，也取决于从数据中拟合出的函数类的复杂度。增大网络规模可改善逼近，但参数幅值是另一个必须同时量化的资源；已有工作未能在固定深度下刻画宽度与幅值之间的精确（sharp）权衡，因此需要从逼近误差与复杂度两方面统一量化并给出匹配上下界。

Method: 作者采用一个初等的有界1-Lipschitz Dyadic-Triangular激活函数，在[0,1]^d上的单位β-Hölder球（0<β≤1）中进行构造性逼近，证明当宽度N≥2d+3且参数幅值有界于T≥1时，最优L^p（0<p<∞）逼近误差上界为[N^2 log(eNT)]^{-β/d}阶；对任意固定全局Hölder激活给出匹配下界（其Hölder指数只影响常数不影响速率）。随后在有界设计密度与独立中心化次高斯噪声下，考虑深度23的完全裁剪（clipped）类上的近似最小二乘估计，令N^2 log(eNT)∝M^{d/(2β+d)}，达到经典Hölder极小极大风险O(M^{-2β/(2β+d)})且无对数损失。还研究了固定规模下的解码方法：四隐藏层至多8d+7个非零参数给出近最优半径，六隐藏层至多8d+27个参数在逼近误差η处达到最优阶log T=O(η^{-d/β})，并指出同一解码方法可给出定规模Transformer逼近。

Result: （1）逼近上下界：对单位β-Hölder球，L^p最优逼近误差阶为[N^2 log(eNT)]^{-β/d}，宽度条件N≥2d+3、幅值界T≥1；下界对所有固定全局Hölder激活成立，激活的Hölder指数只影响常数。（2）统计最优：深度23的裁剪类近似最小二乘达到经典Hölder极小极大风险O(M^{-2β/(2β+d)})，无对数损失，条件为N^2 log(eNT)∝M^{d/(2β+d)}，由此产生从单位参数半径到固定网络规模的一整族（连续统）统计最优选择。（3）固定规模结果：四隐藏层、至多8d+7个非零参数给出近最优半径；六隐藏层、至多8d+27个参数在逼近误差η处达到最优阶log T=O(η^{-d/β})。（4）扩展：同一解码方法可给出定规模Transformer逼近。

Conclusion: 固定深度下，宽度与参数幅值之间存在精确且可量化的权衡：逼近误差由N^2 log(eNT)刻画，其Hölder激活的指数只改变常数而非速率。通过把网络容量与复杂度同时纳入分析，裁剪深度23近似最小二乘在温和条件下以无对数损失达到经典Hölder极小极大风险，且存在连续的统计最优（宽度-幅值）配置族，覆盖了单位参数半径与固定网络规模两种极限情形。定规模的解码构造进一步给出近最优半径与最优幅值阶，并可迁移至Transformer，揭示了Dyadic-Triangular激活与解码方法的通用性。

Abstract: The statistical accuracy of neural networks depends on both their approximation power and the complexity of the class fitted from data. While increasing network size is a natural way to improve approximation, parameter magnitude provides another resource whose role must be quantified in both respects. We establish a sharp width--magnitude tradeoff at fixed depth using one elementary bounded $1$-Lipschitz Dyadic--Triangular Activation. For the unit $β$-Hölder ball on $[0,1]^d$ with $0<β\leq1$, the optimal $L^p$ approximation error for $0<p<\infty$ is of order $[N^2\log(eNT)]^{-β/d}$ when the network width satisfies $N\geq2d+3$ and the parameter magnitudes are bounded by $T\geq1$. Matching lower bounds hold for every fixed globally Hölder activation; its Hölder exponent affects the constants but not the rate. Under bounded design densities and independent centered sub-Gaussian noise, approximate least squares over the full clipped class at depth $23$ attains the classical Hölder minimax risk $\mathcal{O}(M^{-\frac{2β}{2β+d}})$ without logarithmic loss whenever $N^2\log(eNT)\asymp M^{\frac{d}{2β+d}}$, where $M$ is the sample size. This yields a continuum of statistically optimal choices, ranging from unit parameter radius to fixed network size. At fixed size, four hidden layers with at most $8d+7$ nonzero parameters give a near-optimal radius, while six layers with at most $8d+27$ attain the optimal order $\log T=\mathcal{O}(η^{-d/β})$ at approximation error $η$. The same decoding method also yields fixed-size Transformer approximation.

</details>


### [134] [Statistical Gains from Looped Estimation under Parameter Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25778)
*Xinyu Tian,Xiaotong Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文理论分析表明：在相同参数预算下，循环（looped）估计器通过跨迭代共享参数重复应用同一算子，可在统计精度上优于传统的非共享（untied）对应方法，且对已知 Hölder 光滑度的目标能达到 minimax 最优速率（至多差对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能对内存需求的增长促使研究用更少可训练参数进行学习。作者探究循环估计器（在各迭代间共享参数的同一拟合算子）在相同参数预算下能否比传统每层独立参数的非共享估计器取得更好的统计精度。

Method: 针对一般似然模型，建立循环 sieve 极大似然估计的平方 Hellinger 风险上界，以及在调优非共享族上的 minimax 下界；并针对已知 Hölder 光滑度的目标，分析循环残差前馈网络和特定的后层归一化 Transformer，给出速率的上下界匹配论证。

Result: 揭示了参数—迭代—精度的权衡：重复计算可在不增加参数的情况下提升逼近能力，但会增加计算成本和拟合类复杂度。在固定有界实数参数下，循环残差网络与后层归一化 Transformer 达到 minimax 多项式速率（至多对数因子）；在大固定预算下，循环最坏风险随样本量增大趋于零，而最优非共享最坏风险保持严格大于零；在特定增长预算条件下，循环与非共享风险之比也趋于零。

Conclusion: 循环估计器在参数受限场景下具有统计优势，通过重复计算弥补参数不足，并在若干具体模型（高斯与拉普拉斯回归、二值响应、基于能量的密度估计）中得到理论印证。

Abstract: Growing memory demands in artificial intelligence motivate learning with fewer trainable parameters. We ask whether a looped estimator, which repeatedly applies one fitted operator with parameters shared across iterations, can improve statistical accuracy under a common parameter budget. Its conventional untied counterpart uses separate parameters at each iteration. For general likelihood models, we establish an upper bound on squared Hellinger risk for looped sieve maximum likelihood and a minimax lower bound over the tuned untied family. These bounds reveal a parameter--iteration--accuracy tradeoff: repeated computation can improve approximation without adding parameters, while increasing computational cost and fitted-class complexity. For targets of known Hölder smoothness, looped residual feedforward networks and a specified post-layer-normalized Transformer attain the minimax polynomial rate up to logarithmic factors with a fixed number of bounded real parameters. At sufficiently large fixed budgets, looped worst-case risk vanishes as sample size grows, whereas optimal worst-case untied risk remains bounded away from zero. Under specified growing-budget conditions, the loop-to-untied risk ratio also tends to zero. Gaussian and Laplace regression, binary response, and energy-based density estimation illustrate the theory.

</details>


### [135] [Conditional Tensor Diffusion: Distributional Counterfactual Learning and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25924)
*Xinbing Kong,Zeyu Li,Junfan Mao,Bin Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Counterfactual Tucker Diffusion (CFTDiff)，将处理掩码与潜在 Tucker 结构融入条件扩散模型，在高维面板/张量设定下恢复缺失对照结果的联合条件分布，用于因果推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运营与管理决策依赖因果推断，但在高维面板或张量数据中，决策可能依赖于缺失对照结果的联合条件分布，而现有方法难以在降维的同时精确恢复这一分布。

Method: CFTDiff 将处理掩码（treatment mask）与潜在 Tucker 结构整合进条件扩散模型，在低维 Tucker 核心中进行高效非线性分数学习；掩码 Tucker 分数在保留张量各模态间依赖的同时，将非线性分数学习的维度从各模态维度之积降至 Tucker 秩之积。并建立了条件分数估计的高概率误差界。

Result: 误差界取决于 Tucker 秩、最大模态维度与经因子强度调整的缺失结果数量，并可转化为缺失对照结果条件分布的恢复保证。模拟显示在不同缺失率下点恢复优于常见因果面板及矩阵/张量补全方法；与嵌套扩散设定的对比进一步证明掩码条件化与 Tucker 降维的增益。在挪威 iFlex 实验中，CFTDiff 恢复缺失结果更准确，并给出反事实预测区间与目标达成概率，可用于评估定价干预。

Conclusion: CFTDiff 通过掩码条件化与 Tucker 降维，在高维面板/张量因果推断中有效恢复缺失对照结果的联合条件分布，兼具理论保证与实证优势，可用于定价干预的需求减少幅度与可靠性评估。

Abstract: Causal inference guides operational and managerial decisions but remains challenging in high-dimensional panel or tensor settings, where decisions may depend on the joint conditional distribution of missing control outcomes. We develop \emph{Counterfactual Tucker Diffusion} (\CFTDiff), which integrates the treatment mask and latent Tucker structure into conditional diffusion to recover this distribution given observed control outcomes through efficient nonlinear score learning in a low-dimensional core. The masked Tucker score preserves dependence across tensor modes while reducing the dimension of nonlinear score learning from the product of mode dimensions to the much smaller product of Tucker ranks. We establish high-probability error bounds for conditional score estimation that depend on the Tucker ranks, largest mode dimension, and the factor-strength-adjusted number of missing outcomes, and show how these bounds translate into recovery guaranties for the conditional distribution of the missing control outcomes. Across missing rates, simulations show more accurate point recovery than common causal panel and matrix/tensor completion methods; comparisons with nested diffusion specifications further demonstrate the gains from masked conditioning and Tucker dimension reduction. In Norway's iFlex experiment, \CFTDiff recovers missing outcomes more accurately than competing methods; when applied to causal analysis, its estimated conditional distributions yield counterfactual prediction intervals and target-attainment probabilities, allowing pricing interventions to be evaluated by demand-reduction magnitude and reliability.

</details>


### [136] [Learning to Fluctuate: Statistical Foundations for Causal Tabular Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26290)
*Zhiheng Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出波动监督预训练（FSP），通过在合成表的标签中加入有效影响函数的波动项，使因果表格基础模型在单次冻结前向传播中直接编码固定部署总体的重复样本响应，从而将有限层因果标签预测风险降至 n⁻² 阶，并配套给出有限预训练误差界、互补下界及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果表格基础模型通过跨合成机制摊销效应估计，但其潜在效应监督只奖励后验收缩，未直接编码固定部署总体所需的重复样本响应，导致标签模糊性和弱重叠下的头部误差。

Method: 提出波动监督预训练（FSP）：每个合成表的标签设为其平均处理效应（ATE）加上有效影响函数波动项 ψ_P；在路径 T_{λ,P}=θ(P)+λP_nψ_P 上证明端点跃迁，固定 λ<1 时标签模糊度约为 (1-λ)²/n，而完全波动（λ=1）使高斯标签可观测，并将最优有限层因果标签预测风险降至 n⁻² 阶。构建有限预训练界，整合标签、网络、情节采样与优化误差，并用抽样缺陷控制固定机制偏差、MSE、方差、高斯近似与（借助方差头精度）studentized 覆盖；同时给出互补下界，分离部署观测无法消除的局部 n⁻¹ ATE 风险与通用有限字典情节学习问题的 log N/M 超额风险。

Result: 实验追踪了学习到的抽样响应：在原始行列骨干网络上，FSP 相比潜在监督将大效应偏移 RMSE 降低 69.8%，相比已发布的 CausalPFN 检查点（在匹配表上）降低 39.5%。连续协变量实验、已知效应半合成及两项随机化研究评估将抽样律保真度与点风险收缩区分开，并暴露了两个学习头中的弱重叠误差。

Conclusion: FSP 通过显式编码重复样本响应，显著优于仅做后验收缩的潜在监督，其理论界与下界共同刻画了固定机制学习误差的来源与边界，实验验证了抽样律保真度的提升，同时揭示了弱重叠条件下学习头的不足。

Abstract: Causal tabular foundation models amortize effect estimation across synthetic mechanisms, but latent-effect supervision rewards posterior shrinkage instead of directly encoding the repeated-sample response needed in a fixed deployment population. We introduce fluctuation-supervised pretraining (FSP): each synthetic table is labeled by its average treatment effect plus its efficient influence-function fluctuation, while deployment remains a single frozen forward pass. Along the path $T_{λ,P}=θ(P)+λP_nψ_P$, we prove an endpoint transition: every fixed $λ<1$ retains label ambiguity of order $(1-λ)^2/n$, whereas full fluctuation makes the Gaussian label observable and reduces optimal finite-stratum causal label-prediction risk to order $n^{-2}$. One finite-pretraining bound combines label, network, episode-sampling, and optimization errors; its resulting sampling defect controls fixed-mechanism bias, mean squared error, variance, Gaussian approximation, and, with variance-head accuracy, studentized coverage. Complementary lower bounds separate the local $n^{-1}$ ATE risk that deployment observations cannot erase from the $\log N/M$ excess risk of a generic finite-dictionary episode-learning problem. Experiments trace the learned sampling response. With a raw-row/column backbone, FSP reduces large-effect-shift RMSE by 69.8% relative to latent supervision and by 39.5% relative to a released CausalPFN checkpoint on matched tables. Continuous-covariate experiments, known-effect semisynthesis and two randomized-study evaluations separate sampling-law fidelity from point-risk shrinkage and expose weak-overlap errors in both learned heads.

</details>


### [137] [Error Bounds for Statistical Estimators in BTL Model with Parametric Multivariate Utility Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26326)
*Yicheng Li,Huifu Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文研究 Bradley-Terry-Luce (BTL) 模型下的偏好诱导，在非均匀、确定性、任意成对查询设计（仅需满足联合可识别性条件）下，刻画无显式紧性约束或正则的情况下典型极大似然估计 (MLE) 何时有限且具有尖锐误差界，并推导了极小极大下界、样本量阈值及 MLE 的误差分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好诱导中真实 partworth 向量未知，需借助诱导出的偏好信息估计该参数；现有理论多依赖紧性约束或外部正则化，作者希望弄清何时典型 MLE 仅由响应数据本身决定（而非外部正则）即有限、唯一，并获得尖锐的非渐近误差界。

Method: 在标准有界动态范围条件下推导极小极大下界，揭示 Fisher 信息几何（与经典 Cramér-Rao 下界一致）决定有限样本难度；结合似然得分方程的非渐近展开与不动点定位论证，确定设计依赖的样本量阈值；并利用同一展开将估计误差分解为线性随机项、显式二阶偏差与高阶余项。

Result: 得到设计依赖的样本量阈值，超过该阈值时无约束典型 MLE 以高概率存在且唯一；证明在充分样本量条件下典型 MLE 达到极小极大速率（至多相差对数因子与常数因子）；初步数值结果与理论一致。

Conclusion: 建立了参数效用诱导的统一非渐近理论，揭示了推断何时由响应数据本身而非外部正则化所决定，并给出有限、唯一性与误差界成立的样本量条件。

Abstract: We study preference elicitation under the Bradley-Terry-Luce (BTL) model where the true partworth vector is unknown and has to be estimated as a parameter with elicited preference information. The set of selected pairwise queries is non-uniform, deterministic, and arbitrary over a collection of alternatives, provided that it satisfies a joint identifiability condition. We focus on understanding when the canonical maximum likelihood estimator (MLE) is finite and admits sharp error bounds without explicit compactness constraints on the feasible set or external regularizers. To this end, we derive minimax lower bounds under the standard bounded dynamic range condition, and find that the same Fisher-information geometry in the classic Cramér-Rao lower bounds underpins the finite-sample difficulty of the estimation problem. By combining a non-asymptotic expansion of the likelihood score equation with a fixed-point localization argument, we identify a design-dependent sample size threshold above which the unconstrained canonical MLE exists and is unique with high probability. The same expansion yields a decomposition of the estimation error into a linear stochastic term, an explicit second-order bias, and a higher-order remainder. A refined analysis gives sufficient sample size conditions under which the canonical MLE attains the minimax rates up to logarithmic and constant factors. These results provide a unified non-asymptotic theory for parametric utility elicitation and reveal when the inference is determined by response data alone rather than by external regularization. Preliminary numerical results are consistent with the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [138] [SuperPCA: subspace analysis and an efficient algorithm for high-dimensional PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26406)
*Irina-Beatrice Haas,Maike Meier,Yuji Nakatsukasa,Taejun Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 SuperPCA 算法，利用样本协方差矩阵前几个特征向量张成的子空间（而非单个特征向量）来高效、准确地估计信号，并通过子采样坐标显著降低数据采集成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维、多信号场景下，经典 PCA 的单个特征向量往往需要较大样本量才能收敛到总体主成分，导致信号估计效率低、精度差、数据采集成本高。作者希望利用特征子空间所携带的早期信息，在样本量较小的情况下更早、更准确地恢复信号。

Method: 聚焦尖峰协方差模型（spiked covariance model），数据由若干正交信号加各向同性高斯噪声构成。作者利用奇异向量扰动理论，推导出期望信号张成的子空间与样本协方差矩阵前几个特征向量张成的子空间之间夹角的先验/后验界，证明该子空间在单个特征向量收敛之前就已包含大量信号信息。在此基础上提出 SuperPCA（SUbsPace subsamplER PCA）算法，仅利用少量子采样坐标，借助样本协方差矩阵的近似特征子空间来估计前导信号。

Result: 理论上证明，多个前导特征向量张成的子空间在单个特征向量收敛到总体主成分之前就已蕴含关于目标信号的重要信息。数值实验表明，在相同测量数量下，SuperPCA 相比经典 PCA 可将精度提升约 10 倍，且在信号近似稀疏时能大幅节省数据采集成本。

Conclusion: 利用特征子空间而非单个特征向量，结合坐标子采样，SuperPCA 能在高维多信号场景下比经典 PCA 更高效、更准确地估计前导信号，并显著降低数据采集成本。

Abstract: Principal component analysis (PCA) is a fundamental tool to reduce the dimensionality of the data in many applications. PCA finds a few signal directions that contain most of the variability of the data by computing the eigenvectors of the sample covariance matrix. In this work, we focus on the spiked covariance model, in which the data vectors are defined by a few orthogonal signals plus an isotropic Gaussian noise, and our goal is to estimate one or more of the leading signals. Our main theoretical finding is that the subspace spanned by several leading eigenvectors of the sample covariance matrix contains significant information about the desired signals long before the individual eigenvectors converge to the population principal components. To prove this, we derive a posteriori bounds for the angle between the subspace spanned by the desired population signals and the subspace obtained from the sample using perturbation theory for singular vectors. This leads to a new algorithm, SuperPCA (SUbsPace subsamplER PCA), which capitalizes on an approximate eigenspace of the sample covariance matrix to find the leading signals far more efficiently and accurately than classical PCA in the high-dimensional, multi-signal setting. SuperPCA exploits only a small number of subsampled coordinates of the data, which can lead to tremendous savings in data acquisition cost, especially when the signals are approximately sparse. For the same number of measurements, SuperPCA can offer a factor $10$ improvement in accuracy compared to the classical PCA method.

</details>


### [139] [A Practical Guide on Graphical Model Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26445)
*Mario V. Wüthrich*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理并形式化了产险精算建模中最常用的模型验证工具，涵盖图形工具与经典统计工具，并特别强调校准与区分度应在政策加权还是风险暴露加权的人群测度下进行研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产险精算建模中缺乏对各类模型验证工具的统一形式化表述，尤其是校准（calibration）与区分度（discrimination）究竟应在何种人群测度（政策加权 vs 风险暴露加权）下评估往往被忽视或混淆，这直接影响保费方案是否在正确的尺度上实现校准。

Method: 对最常用的模型验证工具进行数学形式化，包括图形工具（校准图、实际对预期图、提升图、Murphy 图）以及经典统计工具（Bregman 损失、偏差损失、基本损失、Murphy 分解与 Gini 得分），并系统分析这些工具在政策加权与风险暴露加权两类概率测度下的行为差异。

Result: 文章给出了各验证工具的统一定义与形式化框架，明确指出校准与区分度的评估高度依赖于所选择的加权测度：在政策加权（按保单数加权）与风险暴露加权（按暴露量加权）下可能得出不同的结论。

Conclusion: 正确区分并理解政策加权与风险暴露加权两种人群测度，是确保保费方案在正确尺度上实现校准的关键；该形式化框架为产险精算从业者选择和应用模型验证工具提供了清晰的理论基础。

Abstract: This manuscript formalizes the most popular model validation tools used in general insurance actuarial modeling. These include graphical tools like calibration plots, actual-vs-expected plots, lift charts, Murphy diagrams, as well as classical statistical tools such as Bregman losses, deviance losses, elementary losses, Murphy's decomposition and Gini scores. Particular emphasis is placed on whether calibration and discrimination are studied under a policy-weighted or an exposure-weighted population measure. This distinction is crucial in ensuring that premium schemes are calibrated on the correct scale.

</details>


### [140] [On Basis Function Selection for Sparse Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26624)
*Marnix Van Soom,Ivan De Boi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏高斯过程（Sparse Gaussian Processes）在固定基展开中的基函数选择问题，提出三种基于信息论的排序准则，用于有选择地保留对数据最相关的 M 个基函数，而非传统的前 M 个截断。在六个 UCI 回归基准及三种基族（HSGP、VFF、VISH）上的实验表明，无数据准则是一个安全默认选项，数据感知准则则对最常用的 HSGP 带来显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏高斯过程通过固定基展开实现 O(N) 推理，实践中通常简单截断基的前 M 项，但这一做法没有形式上的必然性，可能把计算预算浪费在无信号的基函数上。作者希望引入一种更优的基函数选择（而非截断）策略，以避免资源浪费，因此需要一个对候选基函数排序的准则。

Method: 作者从信息论视角看待基函数选择问题，提出三种排序准则，分别对应选择时刻的三种知识状态：无数据（no-data）状态、无先验（no-prior）状态以及中间（in-between）状态。无数据准则仅依赖基函数本身；无先验准则利用数据但忽略先验；中间准则同时利用数据和先验信息。随后在六个 UCI 回归基准上，对三种基族——Hilbert-space 高斯过程（HSGP）、变分傅里叶特征（VFF）和变分诱导球谐函数（VISH）——系统比较了截断策略与选择策略的性能。

Result: 实验表明，无数据准则是一个安全默认选项，在 HSGP、VFF、VISH 上均匹配或优于截断，且在 VISH 上增益显著，并优于近期针对该基族提出的一种选择启发式方法。数据感知的无先验准则与中间准则则主要对实际中最广泛使用的 HSGP 带来显著增益。

Conclusion: 基函数选择优于传统的截断策略，应成为稀疏高斯过程的默认做法。无数据准则在无额外信息时提供稳健且普遍有效的默认选择；当数据可用时，数据感知准则（尤其针对 HSGP）能带来进一步的大幅提升。不同基族对准则的敏感度不同，未来可根据基族特性选择合适准则。

Abstract: Sparse Gaussian processes achieve $O(N)$ inference by replacing the kernel with an appropriate expansion in a fixed basis $\{φ_j\}$ on the input space. Given a compute budget $M \ll N$, practitioners conventionally truncate the basis to its first $M$ entries. Nothing in the formalism, however, prevents one from selecting only those $M$ basis functions that matter for the data at hand. This would avoid spending budget on basis functions where there is no signal, but it requires a criterion for ranking the candidates. We propose three such criteria derived from an information-theoretic view of the basis-function selection problem. Each criterion matches a different state of knowledge at selection time: a no-data state, a no-prior state, and an in-between state. We then study the performance of truncation versus selection strategies on six UCI regression benchmarks across three basis families: Hilbert-space Gaussian processes (HSGP), variational Fourier features (VFF), and variational inducing spherical harmonics (VISH). We observe that the no-data criterion is a safe default, matching or improving on truncation for HSGP, VFF and VISH, with substantial gains for VISH and improvements over a recently developed selection heuristic for that basis family. The data-aware no-prior and in-between criteria provide substantial gains over truncation specifically for HSGP, which is the most broadly used of the three families in practice.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Upworthy标题A/B测试的真实点击数据做sim-to-real验证，发现用LLM扮演“合成用户画像”来预测受众反应，反而不如不用画像、直接让模型判断“典型读者是否会点击”来得准——合成画像不仅预测力弱，而且比不使用更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销人员日益用LLM作为“合成用户画像”预测文案效果，业内证据表明按画像条件化的LLM能模仿人类样本；但作者质疑这一预测对真实行为的有效性，以及画像机制本身是否真的有帮助，因此希望在地面真值上检验其模拟到现实的预测效度。

Method: 以Upworthy研究档案（数千个标题A/B测试、真实流量、实测点击率）作为留出的地面真值；对比两种方案：①十人画像面板（基于真实受众人口统计特征，让模型以特定画像身份判断点击可能性）；②无画像零样本基线（不问画像，直接问模型“典型读者有多大可能点击”）。二者在可靠子集（n=399）上比较排序与命中指标，并通过三个独立数据切分、跨领域新闻数据集、不同种子/提示措辞/模型（Gemini三档及OpenAI gpt-4.1）做鲁棒性检验。

Result: 第一，地面真值可靠性是瓶颈：多数A/B测试无统计上可区分的胜者，故效度只能在可靠子集（n=399）上评估。第二，与画像模拟的前提相反，画像条件化反而降低预测效度：无画像基线排序更优（Kendall τ=0.361，中等效应；top-1准确率49.2%），画像面板更差（τ=0.084；top-1 34.6%），且置信区间不重叠。直接询问模型能提取准确的人群总体先验，强制角色扮演特定画像则注入偏差与噪声。结果在三个Upworthy切分上复现，在跨领域新闻数据集中方向一致，并对种子、提示措辞和模型选择稳健（Gemini三档及OpenAI gpt-4.1均显著配对差距）。

Conclusion: 预测聚合用户参与度时，朴素的LLM排序器优于画像模拟——合成用户画像不仅是弱预测器，甚至不如不用它。作者提供公开的、artifact-first的可复现包，所有数字均可重新生成。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [142] [Do Existing Preconditioners Improve Biomedical Tabular Foundation Learning? An Empirical Study on TabPFN Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25013)
*M. Sajid,Pinki Khatun,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对微调 TabPFN v2.5 的五种 AdamW 预条件策略在 59 个生物医学数据集上进行了系统实证研究，发现原始 AdamW 优化器性能与统计排名最优，现有曲率感知预条件器未能提供可靠提升，呼吁开发面向医疗表格基础模型的生物医学专用预条件器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（尤其是 TabPFN）在低数据量结构化生物医学分析中展现潜力，但优化与预条件策略对生物医学微调的影响尚未被充分探索，缺乏系统性实证证据。

Method: 在 59 个生物医学数据集（涵盖阿尔茨海默病、乳腺癌、精神分裂症、显著记忆关注 SMC、KEEL 生物医学数据集及 UCI 生物医学基准）上，对 5 种基于 AdamW 的预条件策略微调 TabPFN v2.5，并从预测性能、计算效率与统计显著性三个维度进行评估。

Result: 实验表明原始 AdamW 优化器在整体性能与统计排名上始终最优，而现有曲率感知预条件器在多样化的生物医学学习场景中未能可靠地带来改进。

Conclusion: 通用预条件方法可能无法充分刻画生物医学表格学习的优化特性，需开发面向医疗表格基础模型的生物医学专用预条件器。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have recently shown strong potential for structured biomedical data analysis. Among them, TabPFN has emerged as an effective approach for low-data tabular classification tasks. However, the impact of optimization and preconditioning strategies on biomedical fine-tuning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical investigation of five AdamW-based preconditioning strategies for fine-tuning TabPFN v2.5 on 59 biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease, breast cancer, schizophrenia, significant memory concern (SMC), KEEL biomedical datasets, and UCI biomedical benchmarks. The evaluation considers predictive performance, computational efficiency, and statistical significance analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that the original AdamW optimizer consistently achieves the best overall performance and statistical ranking, while existing curvature-aware preconditioners fail to provide reliable improvements across diverse biomedical learning scenarios. The findings suggest that generic preconditioning approaches may not adequately capture the optimization characteristics of biomedical tabular learning, motivating the development of biomedical-aware preconditioners specifically tailored for healthcare-oriented tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [143] [4DGS-JEPA: Temporally Compositional Joint-Embedding Prediction for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25036)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 4DGS-JEPA，一个基于动态高斯泼溅的联合嵌入预测架构，通过层级表征、时间组合原则与混合对应机制，实现动态 3D 场景的因果多步未来预测，并在理论与受控实验上验证了其路径一致性、误差累积与几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅方法主要面向重建、未来帧生成或渲染，缺乏对可复用预测性动态的学习能力；作者希望建立一种以预测为核心、支持因果多步外推与递归滚动的动态高斯世界表征，使到达同一未来状态的不同时序路径产生相容的预测，从而学习更稳定、可迁移的动力学。

Method: 提出高斯原生联合嵌入预测架构 4DGS-JEPA，采用场景级、运动组级与高斯级三层表征，配合以时间步长为条件的转移算子，既支持直接预测也支持递归 rollout。核心为时间组合原则：到达同一未来终点的不同时序路径应产生相容预测状态，并以终点监督与多步路径监督锚定目标嵌入。通过选择性几何解码器和几何级组合将动力学约束在一致的组运动与高斯几何上，无需完整未来外观重建。同时引入混合对应机制，结合持久规范身份与残差最优传输匹配，以应对重排序与拓扑变化。

Result: 从理论上刻画了零损失路径一致性与有限误差 rollout 累积；三个受控实验在机制层面证明：时间组合降低潜在路径依赖且保持预测精度、几何级组合提升解码运动一致性、混合对应在对应关系模糊时仍保持可靠身份并具备鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 4DGS-JEPA 提供了一种预测性的、时间组合式的动态高斯世界表述，将动态高斯泼溅从单纯重建/渲染拓展到可复用、可多步外推的预测动力学学习。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting provides an explicit representation of evolving 3D scenes, but existing approaches are primarily optimized for reconstruction, future-state generation, or rendering rather than for learning reusable predictive dynamics. We propose 4DGS-JEPA, a Gaussian-native joint-embedding predictive architecture for causal multi-horizon prediction over dynamic Gaussian scenes. The model uses a hierarchical scene-, motion-group-, and Gaussian-level representation together with a horizon-conditioned transition operator that supports both direct prediction and recursive rollout. Its central principle is temporal composition: different chronological transition paths reaching the same future endpoint should produce compatible predictive states. Endpoint and multi-horizon path supervision anchor these predictions to future target embeddings, while a selective geometry decoder and geometry-level composition ground the learned dynamics in consistent group motion and Gaussian geometry without requiring complete future appearance reconstruction. We further introduce a hybrid correspondence mechanism that combines persistent canonical identity with residual optimal-transport matching under reordering and topology change. We characterize zero-loss path agreement and finite-error rollout accumulation theoretically. Three controlled experiments provide mechanism-level evidence that temporal composition reduces latent path dependence while retaining predictive accuracy, geometry-level composition improves consistency of decoded motion, and hybrid correspondence preserves reliable identity while remaining robust when correspondence becomes ambiguous. Together, 4DGS-JEPA provides a predictive, temporally compositional formulation of dynamic Gaussian worlds.

</details>


### [144] [An Accurate and Interpretable Hyper Graph Neural Network for GBM Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25088)
*Mushahid Intesum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态框架，将层超图神经网络、概念瓶颈监督与扩展充分性测试正则化相结合，在胶质母细胞瘤生存预测中同时实现高精度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GBM生存预测模型将准确性与可解释性视为对立目标，且多依赖单一影像模态，未能充分利用多模态MRI与临床元数据的互补信息。作者认为这一权衡并非固有，图神经网络可同时提取可解释表征并保持判别能力。

Method: 提出三组件融合框架：(1) 层超图神经网络，通过有向非对称消息传递捕捉组织块间的高阶关系；(2) 概念瓶颈层，将学习到的表征压缩为临床可理解的概念，实现事前可解释性；(3) 扩展充分性测试(EST)正则化器，在训练时惩罚不忠实的解释。临床与基因组特征通过门控融合引入，既保留分子标志物的主导预后信号，又维持概念层的可追踪性。

Result: 在UPenn-GBM数据集593例患者上做5折交叉验证，框架取得0.643的一致性指数，且在所有对比模型中折间标准差最低(std=0.015)。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个将层超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督与EST正则化统一用于脑MRI可解释生存预测的方法，证明了准确性与可解释性可以同时达成。

Abstract: Survival prediction for glioblastoma multiforme (GBM) demands models that are both accurate and interpretable, yet existing approaches treat these objectives as com- peting, where performant models sacrifice transparency, while interpretable models accept degraded predictive power. We argue that this trade-off is not inherent. Graph neural net- works offer a structural foundation for extracting interpretable, explainable representations without compromising discriminative ability. Furthermore, current methods typically rely on a single imaging modality, underutilizing the complementary information available across multi-modal MRI and clinical metadata. We propose a multi-modal framework that inte- grates three components to address both objectives simultaneously: (1) a sheaf hypergraph neural network that captures higher-order relationships among tissue patches through direc- tional, asymmetric message passing; (2) a concept bottleneck layer that compresses learned representations into clinically grounded concepts, enforcing ante-hoc interpretability; and (3) an extension sufficiency test (EST) regularizer that penalizes unfaithful explanations during training, ensuring that model explanations genuinely reflect the internal decision process. Clinical and genomic features are incorporated through gated fusion, preserving the dominant prognostic signal of molecular markers while retaining concept-level traceabil- ity. Evaluated on 593 patients from the UPenn-GBM dataset under 5-fold cross-validation, our framework achieves a concordance index of 0.643 with the lowest fold-level variance among all compared models (std = 0.015). To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify sheaf hypergraph convolution, concept bottleneck supervision, and EST regularization for interpretable survival prediction from brain MRI

</details>


### [145] [Ovis-Embedding: Pushing the Frontiers of Universal Omni-Modal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25165)
*Ovis-Embedding Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ovis-Embedding 是一个采用共享多模态骨干网络、原生统一文本/图像/视频/音频表征的全模态嵌入模型家族，通过三项关键设计（原生全模态初始化、数据为中心的训练、嵌入特化的训练与推理优化）在五大基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型通常为各模态分别构建独立的"模态塔"，导致模态碎片化，难以实现统一的任意模态间检索。作者旨在通过单一共享骨干网络，将不同模态编码到共同的表征空间，从而克服模态割裂，推动通用全模态嵌入模型的发展。

Method: 该工作提出三项核心设计：(1) 原生全模态初始化——以预训练 Qwen-omni 为骨干，通过带低秩初始化的对比训练进行适配；(2) 数据为中心的全模态训练——构建覆盖文本、图像、视频、音频及交错多模态数据的高质量语料，并引入同源采样以形成任务一致的批次、提供有信息量的批内负样本；(3) 嵌入特化的训练与推理优化——使用焦点损失强调困难样本，采用基于相似度的嵌入蒸馏从互补专家模型迁移细粒度相似结构；推理端利用低秩特征分解获得维度灵活、性能损失极小的紧凑嵌入。

Result: 在 MMEB-v3、MMEB-v2、MVEB、MAEB 和 RTEB 五个基准上均取得领先（state-of-the-art）性能，验证了其在文本、图像、视频和音频各模态上的有效性。

Conclusion: 统一的端到端全模态训练能够克服模态碎片化，推动面向任意模态间检索的通用嵌入模型发展，Ovis-Embedding 系列即体现了这一潜力。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce \textbf{Ovis-Embedding}, a state-of-the-art omni-modal embedding family built on native integration of text, image, video, and audio. Instead of assembling separate modality towers, Ovis-Embedding uses a shared multimodal backbone to encode different modalities in a common representation space. Specifically, we make \textbf{three key advances}: (1) \textbf{native omni-modal initialization}: we adopt a pretrained Qwen-omni model as the embedding backbone and adapt it through contrastive training with low-rank initialization; (2) \textbf{data-centric omni-modal training}: we construct a broad, high-quality corpus spanning text, images, video, audio, and interleaved multimodal data. To improve data efficiency, we introduce homogeneous-source sampling to form task-consistent batches with informative in-batch negatives; and (3) \textbf{embedding-specific training and inference optimization}: we use focal loss to emphasize hard examples and similarity-based Embedding Distillation to transfer fine-grained similarity structure from complementary experts. At inference time, low-rank feature decomposition enables compact embeddings with flexible dimensionality and minimal performance loss. Empirical evaluations show that the \textbf{Ovis-Embedding} family achieves state-of-the-art performance on \textbf{MMEB-v3}, \textbf{MMEB-v2}, \textbf{MVEB}, \textbf{MAEB}, and \textbf{RTEB}, demonstrating its effectiveness across text, image, video, and audio modalities. These results highlight the potential of unified omni-modal training to overcome modality fragmentation and advance universal embedding models for any-to-any retrieval.

</details>


### [146] [X-Planner: Event-Structured Task Planning for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25187)
*Howard Lu,Shalfun Li,Porter Pan,Cris,Lumen,Cyril,Eric Hu,Lily Li,Maeve Zhang,Robert Wang,KZ Zheng,Viggo Chen,Tim Ding,Regsis Cheng,YJ Xiao,Kian,Hai Lin,Alan Song,Elise Ma,Gody Li,Victor Yao,Yohann Tang,Ingrid Yu,Jason He,James Wang,Ryan Yu,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Vincent Chen,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-Planner 是一个面向 VLA 系统的任务规划前端，通过层次化多源规划数据与事件结构化表示（离散事件状态 + 潜变量 CoT 状态）同时解决具身推理的监督与表示问题，在离线规划评测与真机实验中均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 VLA 系统在长程操作中常把任务规划这一中间结构隐式化；现有 CoT 规划器要么依赖粗粒度任务级标注，要么逐 token 序列化冗长推理轨迹，导致规划监督信号不足、推理表示低效，难以支撑可解释、可纠错的具身推理。

Method: 提出 X-Planner 规划前端：(1) 数据侧融合 Ego、UMI、遥操作三种来源，采用层次化粒度并按来源设定不同标注深度，同时利用 takeover-time（接管时刻）标注与人为设计的失败案例监督在线错误识别；(2) 模型侧以共享 VLM 骨干输出两种事件结构化规划形式——离散接口产生可解释的事件状态，潜变量接口通过 Staircase Decoding 跨错开的 Transformer 深度传递连续 CoT 状态；(3) 以冻结的 latent-to-text 重建目标作为潜变量表示的语义锚点。

Result: 在离线两步规划评估中，X-Planner 在四个被评测模型中于 BERTScore-F1 与基于裁判的 Overall 分数上均位列第二；在真机实验中，X-Planner 在规划文本质量与下游执行表现上均优于所评测基线。

Conclusion: 通过层次化、来源自适应的规划监督与事件结构化的双接口表示，X-Planner 有效提升了 VLA 系统的任务规划文本质量及下游真机执行效果，验证了显式事件结构与潜变量 CoT 表示在具身推理中的价值。

Abstract: Task planning bridges high-level instructions and executable behavior in long-horizon manipulation, yet modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems often leave this intermediate structure implicit. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) planners also tend to rely on coarse task-level annotations or serialize long reasoning traces token by token. We present X-Planner, a planning front-end that addresses both the supervision and representation of embodied reasoning. Our planning data combine Ego, UMI, and teleoperation under a hierarchy granularity with source-dependent annotation depth. Takeover-time annotations and human-designed failures supervise ongoing error recognition. On the model side, a shared VLM backbone exposes two event-structured plan forms: a discrete interface that emits interpretable event states and a latent interface that relays continuous CoT states across staggered Transformer depths through Staircase Decoding. A frozen latent-to-text reconstruction objective provides a semantic anchor for the latent representation. Offline two-step planning evaluation places X-Planner second among four evaluated models on both BERTScore-F1 and a judge-based Overall score. In real-robot experiments, respectively, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results characterize planning-text quality and downstream execution.

</details>


### [147] [Lean Pool: An AI-Maintained Archive of Formalized Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25199)
*Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lean Pool 是一个由 AI 智能体（AI agents）参与增长、维护与优化的形式化数学知识库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在构建一个规模可持续扩大、质量可靠且高效组织的形式化数学知识库（repository），以推动数学证明的机械化与自动化进程。

Method: 通过部署一类 AI 智能体系统，对知识库执行三类核心任务：增长（growth，即持续扩充形式化定理与定义）、维护（maintenance，即保障库中内容一致性与可编译性）以及优化（optimization，即改进证明的结构、简洁性与重用性）。

Result: 形成了一个由 AI 驱动的、持续演进的形式化数学仓库 Lean Pool，实现了内容的自动扩充与长期稳定维护。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，借助 AI 智能体可以实现形式化数学知识库的自动化增长、维护与优化，为形式化数学的规模化发展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Lean Pool is a repository of formalized mathematics. It is grown, maintained and optimized by AI agents.

</details>


### [148] [The AI Neuroscientist: An Interactive Agentic Interface for Neuroimaging Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25254)
*Aakash Patel,Panos Ketonis,Shreya Saxena,Smita Krishnaswamy,David van Dijk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI Neuroscientist"——一个结合大语言模型与神经影像工具集的语言智能体，支持用户用自然语言对神经影像数据进行质量控制、建模与可视化，并以fNIRS数据验证其能力，同时构建了专门的基准测试套件评估其表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像数据分析需要专业的编码和统计技能，限制了无计算背景研究者的使用。作者旨在提供一种透明、交互式的替代方案，替代传统脚本化流程，降低小规模数据探索的门槛。

Method: 构建"AI Neuroscientist"语言智能体，将大语言模型（LLM）与神经影像工具集集成，实现质量控制、建模和可视化功能；用户通过自然语言查询数据质量并指定分析参数。在功能性近红外光谱（fNIRS）数据上演示能力，并构建自定义fNIRS基准测试套件，与带代码沙箱的通用LLM智能体进行对比评估。

Result: 该系统能够支持研究者用自然语言进行数据质量查询和分析参数设定，在fNIRS数据上展示了质量控制、建模与可视化能力；其专用架构在自定义fNIRS基准测试中与通用LLM智能体进行了对比评估。

Conclusion: "AI Neuroscientist"为缺乏编程背景的研究者提供了透明、交互式的小规模神经影像数据探索方式。未来将把架构推广到功能磁共振成像（fMRI）等其他模态，并扩展基准测试套件以覆盖更多fNIRS任务。

Abstract: Analyzing neuroimaging data requires specialized coding and statistical expertise, which limits accessibility for researchers without computational backgrounds. We present the AI Neuroscientist, a language agent for interactive data exploration. The system integrates a large language model (LLM) with a neuroimaging toolset to perform quality control, modeling, and visualization. This allows researchers to query data quality and specify analysis parameters directly in natural language, providing a transparent and interactive alternative to conventional scripted pipelines for small-scale data exploration. We demonstrate these capabilities using functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) data, and evaluate the agent on a custom fNIRS benchmarking suite against general-purpose LLM agents with code sandboxes. Future extensions will generalize the architecture to additional modalities, including functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, and expand the benchmarking suite to additional fNIRS tasks.

</details>


### [149] [MedGate-Fusion: Integrating First-Encounter Semantic Narratives and Physiological Biomarkers for Prospective Stroke Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25272)
*Hemn Khdr,Mohammad Noaeen,Karim Keshavjee,Aziz Guergachi,Zahra Shakeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MedGate-Fusion，一种多模态门控架构，融合初次就诊临床叙述的 Transformer 嵌入与十项常规记录的风险标志物，基于加拿大初级保健哨点监测网络(CPCSSN)的电子病历数据，对五年卒中结局进行前瞻性风险分层，并通过词典式脱敏卒中相关术语以减少数据泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在初级保健中，前瞻性卒中风险分层具有挑战性，因为早期风险信号分散在常规生物标志物与非结构化的临床叙述文本中，单一模态难以充分捕捉这些信号。

Method: 提出 MedGate-Fusion 多模态门控架构，将初次就诊叙述的 Transformer 嵌入与十项常规记录的风险标志物进行整合；使用 CPCSSN 电子病历数据，从 808,921 条就诊级观察中构建初次就诊队列，保留 102,736 份具有非空叙述且数据足以评估五年卒中结局的患者记录；在语义编码前对叙述中的卒中相关术语进行基于词典的脱敏，以降低由诊断提及引起的显式目标泄漏。

Result: 构建了包含 102,736 名患者的初次就诊队列，具备非空临床叙述与充足的五年卒中结局评估数据，并实现了卒中相关术语的词典式脱敏处理。

Conclusion: 本文（摘要部分）尚未给出具体实验结论，主要呈现了模型设计、数据构建与防泄漏策略，后续结果需结合完整论文。

Abstract: Prospective stroke risk stratification in primary care is challenging because early risk signals are distributed across routine biomarkers and unstructured clinical narratives. We propose MedGate-Fusion, a multi-modal gated architecture that integrates transformer-based embeddings of first-encounter narratives with ten routinely recorded risk markers. We used electronic medical record data from the Canadian Primary Care Sentinel Surveillance Network (CPCSSN). Starting from 808,921 encounter-level observations, we constructed a first-encounter cohort and retained 102,736 unique patient records with non-empty narratives and sufficient data to evaluate a five-year stroke outcome. To reduce explicit target leakage from diagnostic mentions in notes, we applied dictionary-based redaction of stroke-related terms prior to semantic encoding.

</details>


### [150] [When LLM Agents Fail to Read the Room: ReAdapt for Relational Social Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25284)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Yunda Liu,Fei Wang,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReAdapt框架，为LLM智能体显式建模结构化社交关系状态z=(G,B,R,N,D)，并在每次工具观察后执行类型化的Adapt步骤，从而在关系线索与内容线索冲突时修正决策。在500个合成社交世界、1000个查询的基准上，ReAdapt将温暖引荐准确率提升14个百分点、反应选择准确率提升8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交智能体的基本决策（是否回应帖子、该联系谁）本质上依赖潜在的人际关系（关系强度、互惠性、共同连接），而非单纯的内容显著性。但标准LLM智能体循环不显式表示新的关系证据应如何修正当前社交假设，导致当关系线索与内容线索不一致时，智能体倾向于选择表面显而易见的选项。

Method: 作者构建了一个关系推理基准，包含500个合成社交世界（好友、关注、反应历史、信息流），生成1000个查询，覆盖反应选择和温暖引荐两个任务；其中约53%的查询构成"翻转子集"，即表面明显候选与基于关系的真实答案不同。在此之上提出ReAdapt，在ReAct循环中加入显式结构化社交状态z=(G,B,R,N,D)，分别捕获目标、信念、关系、规范和披露，每次工具观察后运行类型化的Adapt步骤更新状态并发出策略操作（continue/switch/abandon/clarify），再选择下一步动作。

Result: 在Gemini-3-Flash上、每任务150个查询的分层子集中，ReAdapt将温暖引荐准确率从37%提升到51%（+14点），反应选择准确率从69%提升到77%（+8点）；Oracle遗憾分别从0.260降至0.152、从0.095降至0.053。在模型、工具和环境保持不变的前提下，结果说明显式关系状态适应有助于LLM智能体把检索到的社交证据转化为修正后的决策。

Conclusion: 

Abstract: A social agent's most basic decisions (should I react to this post? who should I reach out to?) are not purely content problems. The right action often hinges on the latent relationship between people -- tie strength, reciprocity, mutual connections -- rather than on which content is most salient. Standard LLM agent loops do not explicitly represent how new relational evidence should revise the agent's current social hypothesis, leaving them prone to surface-obvious choices when relational and content cues diverge. We formalize this failure mode with a relationship-reasoning benchmark: 500 synthetic social worlds with friendships, follows, reaction histories, and feeds, yielding 1,000 queries over two tasks, reaction selection and warm introduction (finding the best bridge to a target person). By construction, the surface-obvious candidate differs from the relationship-grounded oracle in about 53% of queries, forming an overturn subset where the agent must use relational evidence to revise an initially plausible choice. We propose ReAdapt (Relationship-Adaptive Agent with Policy-driven sTate), which augments the ReAct loop with an explicit structured social state z = (G, B, R, N, D) capturing goal, belief, relationship, norm, and disclosure. After each tool observation, ReAdapt runs a typed Adapt step that updates this state and emits a policy operation (continue, switch, abandon, or clarify) before choosing the next action. With Gemini-3-Flash on a stratified subset of n = 150 queries per task, ReAdapt improves warm-introduction accuracy from 37% to 51% (+14 points) and reaction-selection accuracy from 69% to 77% (+8 points). Oracle regret drops from 0.260 to 0.152 and from 0.095 to 0.053, respectively. Holding the model, tools, and environments fixed, these results suggest that explicit relational-state adaptation helps LLM agents turn retrieved social evidence into revised decisions.

</details>


### [151] [Attention as a Routing Graph: Live Circuit Extraction from a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25285)
*Ash Manvi,Samreena Tajreen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本方法：将注意力视为单次前向传播的路由图，保留少数指向答案的路由边，并通过消融实验验证这些边是否真实携带因果信号。在已知“正确”电路的 induction 与 IOI 任务上，消融提取边比消融等量随机边对模型伤害更大，提取成本仅为一次前向，远低于逐头 patch 扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上在语言模型中寻找电路需要大量精细干预实验，成本高昂。作者希望探索一种更简单的方法，判断是否可以仅凭一次前向传播中注意力的路由结构，廉价地提取出对答案有真实因果影响的边。

Method: 将注意力视为一次前向传播的“路由图”，保留少量指向答案的路由边；随后通过消融实验检验这些边是否真的重要，并与消融等量随机边对比。在 induction 和 IOI（已知正确电路的任务）上，对 GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium、Pythia-410M 各评估 n=100 个 prompt，使用配对差距检验与 bootstrap 置信区间进行统计；提取步骤仅需一次前向，而逐头 patch 扫描成本约高两个数量级。

Result: 在 induction 与 IOI 任务上，消融提取出的边对模型的伤害显著大于消融等量随机边，说明这种廉价“草图”确实携带真实因果信号。作者同时指出该方法存在明确的失效模式，并非完整的电路图谱。

Conclusion: 此方法并非宣称构建完整电路图谱，而是提供一个低成本的“草图”，在已知任务上携带真实因果信号，并清晰标注其失效情形。代码与评估产物已开源。

Abstract: Finding circuits in language models usually means running many careful interventions. We try something simpler: treat attention as a routing map from one forward pass, keep a small set of routes that point toward the answer, and ask whether those routes actually matter.
  They often do. On induction and IOI (tasks where the "right" circuit is already known), ablating our extracted edges hurts the model much more than ablating a random set of the same size. We evaluate n=100 prompts per cell on GPT-2 Small, GPT-2 Medium, and Pythia-410M, with paired gap tests and bootstrap confidence intervals. The extract step costs one forward; a head-by-head patch sweep costs about two orders of magnitude more.
  We are not claiming a complete circuit atlas. We are claiming a cheap sketch that carries real causal signal on known tasks, with clear failure modes when it does not. Code and evaluation artifacts are at https://github.com/Aquinf03/live-circuit-routing.

</details>


### [152] [Learned Enterprise Data Comprehension: Compression and Routing for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25286)
*Ethan Torres,Eric Mills*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种latent equivalence learning（潜在等价学习）框架，将agent的语义推理与学习系统的结构组织分工，通过“持久身份+数据集相关实例”的分离，利用支持/反对证据塑造的Gaussian原型和软成员资格、以及可学习的query-prototype兼容性函数，将跨schema证据预先组织好，从而显著提升企业结构化数据agent的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中结构化数据agent需要在分散于多schema、关系、策略和反复出现的业务角色之间的复杂证据环境中推理。现有agentic系统常用可复用的markdown记忆/技能文件保存已发现信息以避免重复探索，但这种方式模糊了天然的分工：agent擅长语义推理，而学习系统擅长预测和组织反复出现的结构。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，避免每次查询都重建跨schema结构。

Method: 提出latent equivalence learning框架，将持久的任务相关身份与其数据集相关的实现分离：(1) 支持与反对证据共同塑造“support-realized Gaussian prototypes”，学习身份在特定数据环境中的表达方式；(2) 软成员资格(soft-membership)档案保留硬分配下丢失的区分信息；(3) 独立的可学习query-prototype系统表征反复出现的证据需求，并通过可学习的兼容性函数映射到同一持久身份结构。由此实现身份因式分解、查询条件的路由，将数据集相关证据物化为下游推理的已组织状态。

Result: 在Data Agent Benchmark（12个异构数据集、54条查询）上，完整实现经过5轮完整试验取得94.67%的dataset-macro stratified Pass@1，并成功完成258/270条原始查询尝试；对比基准参考agent Claude Opus 4.6仅55.51%，在提交时位列40个榜单条目第一。

Conclusion: 通过latent equivalence learning将agent的语义推理能力与学习系统的结构预测/组织能力分工，以身份因式化、查询条件的路由预先组织证据，使agent在已组织好的证据状态下操作，显著优于依赖可复用记忆文件的方法，并在基准测试中取得领先性能。

Abstract: Structured-data agents in enterprise settings must reason over complex data environments whose relevant evidence is distributed across schemas, relationships, policies, and recurring business roles. Modern agentic systems often address this burden through reusable markdown-style memory or skill files that preserve previously discovered information for later queries, reducing the need to rediscover the same structure repeatedly. This is useful, but it obscures a natural division of labor: agents are well suited to semantic reasoning, while learned systems are well suited to predicting and organizing recurring structure. We introduce latent equivalence learning to bridge this gap. The framework separates persistent task-relevant identities from their dataset-relative realizations. In our realization, supporting and opposing evidence shape support-realized Gaussian prototypes that learn how those identities are expressed in a particular data environment, while soft-membership profiles retain distinctions lost under a hard assignment. A separate learned query-prototype system represents recurring evidential requirements and maps them through a learned compatibility function into the same persistent identity structure. This identity-factorized, query-conditioned routing materializes the relevant dataset-specific evidence for downstream reasoning, allowing the agent to operate over an already organized evidential state rather than reconstructing cross-schema structure at every query. On the Data Agent Benchmark, spanning 54 queries across 12 heterogeneous datasets, our full implementation achieves 94.67% dataset-macro stratified Pass@1 over five complete trials and 258/270 successful raw query attempts, compared with 55.51% for the benchmark's Claude Opus 4.6 reference agent, ranking first among 40 leaderboard entries at submission.

</details>


### [153] [Making Agents More Consistent: Skills Should Form Habits for Repeat Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25299)
*Travis Weber,Rohit Taneja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 智能体（agent）在重复任务上输出不一致且浪费巨大；论文提出"技能习惯形成"机制，将已验证的推理固化为确定性技能并配合四道防护门，在保证可审计性的同时大幅提升一致性、降低 token 消耗，并量化了由此带来的准确性代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在重复工作上表现不一致且资源浪费严重：42 个任务各重复三次，38%–74% 的回答彼此不一致；同时 95.3%–97.2% 的生成量浪费在重新推导系统已知的计划上。买方、审计方和监管方需要一致性，而现有智能体无法保证，因此需要一种兼顾一致性、可审计性与效率的机制。

Method: 提出"技能习惯形成"（skill habit formation）：智能体从自身执行历史中挖掘候选技能，生成确定性变体与现有方案竞争而非直接替换。候选技能声明其可覆盖的输入空间区域，常见情况按脚本执行，其余回退到推理。设四道成本递增的准入门，核心门将候选技能的执行轨迹与保留的参考轨迹比对，容差由该参考轨迹自身的逐次运行变异性度量。此外将路由（routing）与参数提取分离以提升端到端准确性。

Result: 在 text-to-SQL 任务上，四个推理臂中有三个在 42 个重复问题上仅 11–13 次完全复现自身输出，第四个为 26/42；而习惯形成后的变体在全部 456 次重复调度中均复现，且不劣于其所替换的每个臂（p<0.0001），token 消耗减少 14%–56%，7–53 次复用后转正。准确性代价：防护门在 2.6% 的自然改写和 26% 边界附近输入上错误放行本应延迟的工作；13 个此类失败中有 11 个在任何阈值下都无法被轨迹一致性门察觉。确定性错误会原样重复——坏习惯与好习惯同样可靠。将路由与参数提取分离使端到端准确率从 0.888 提升到 0.952，成本仅为 43%。

Conclusion: 习惯形成能以极低的额外成本换取严格的一致性与可审计性，并在多数情况下保持甚至提升准确性，显著降低 token 消耗；但确定性意味着错误也会精确复现，这是可审计性所固有的代价，需通过路由与参数提取分离、轨迹一致性门等设计加以缓解。

Abstract: On repeated work, agents are inconsistent. We ran 42 tasks three times each and found that, depending on the model, 38% to 74% returned answers that did not agree. Consistency is what a buyer, an auditor, or a regulator requires, and agents do not have it. They are wasteful too: 95.3% to 97.2% of what an agent generates goes to re-deriving a plan the system already knows.
  We propose skill habit formation. An agent mines its own execution history for candidate skills, deterministic variants that compete against the incumbent rather than replacing it. A candidate declares the region of input space it claims, so the common case runs as a script and the rest falls through to reasoning. Four gates of ascending cost admit candidates; the central one tests a candidate's execution trace against a retained reference, within a tolerance measured from that reference's own run-to-run variability.
  On text-to-SQL, three of four reasoning arms reproduced their own output on 11 to 13 of 42 repeated questions and the fourth on 26 of 42, while a habit-formed variant reproduced on all 456 dispatches we repeated and was non-inferior to every arm it replaced (p<0.0001). It also used 14% to 56% fewer tokens, turning net positive after 7 to 53 reuses.
  We measured what this costs in accuracy. The guard admitted work it should have deferred on 2.6% of natural paraphrases and 26% of inputs near its boundary, and 11 of 13 such failures were invisible to the trace-conformance gate at any threshold. Deterministic errors repeat exactly: a bad habit is as reliable as a good one, and that is the price of the property that makes the system auditable. Separating routing from parameter extraction raised end-to-end accuracy from 0.888 to 0.952 at 43% of the cost.

</details>


### [154] [Potential for Enhanced Learning in Machine Learning Classes by Using Wiki LLM Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25303)
*Brian Wright*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了在真实机器学习课程语料上，基于向量RAG与LLM编译的wiki两种知识表示方式。结果显示：单事实问答两者相当，但跨单元关联问题上，wiki表现显著更优且完全可溯源，而RAG得分更低且仅64%可溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为课程专属辅导工具的效果取决于是否植根于经过审核的教学材料，而这些材料在学期中常被修订。前作发现固定检索策略在RAG中表现次优，由此引出核心问题：语料在摄入阶段的结构化方式是否比查询阶段的检索量更为关键。

Method: 在同一份课程语料（Foundations of Machine Learning）上进行受控的正面比较：(A) 向量RAG，复现前作最佳配置；(B) LLM编译的wiki（Karpathy框架），在摄入阶段将语料合成为带显式交叉引用和源材料引用的关联概念页面。通过59道涵盖单事实回忆、跨单元概念连接、综合解释及大纲修订后时效性的问题，由LLM评审依据人工编写的评分细则进行打分。

Result: 单事实问题两者表现接近（RAG 9.33 vs wiki 9.96，满分10）；跨单元问题上差距显著：wiki保持准确且有依据（9.93分，100%可溯源至引用来源），而RAG得分较低且可溯源性明显不足（8.14分，64%）。wiki的引用机制使学生和教师能将任何论断追溯到引入该概念的讲座，形成机器学习课程所需的动态检索层。

Conclusion: 虽然尚需进一步测试，但在机器学习课程中借助AI辅助教学的教师应考虑采用基于wiki的结构，因其有望支撑最佳教学实践的基础要素。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as course-specific tutors, but their usefulness depends on grounding in vetted instructional materials that are often revised mid-semester. Our prior work built a multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system over an authentic machine learning course corpus (Foundations of Machine Learning) and found that retrieval improved contextual grounding, but that fixed retrieval strategies were suboptimal. That motivates a different question: whether how a corpus is structured at ingest time matters more than how much is retrieved at query time. We present a controlled head-to-head comparison of two knowledge representations over an identical classroom corpus: (A) vector RAG, replicating the best-performing configuration from our prior study, and (B) an LLM-compiled wiki (Karpathy framework), in which the corpus is synthesized at ingest into linked concept pages with explicit cross-references and citations back to source materials. We evaluate 59 questions spanning single-fact recall, cross-unit concept linking, synthesis and explanation, and currency after a syllabus revision, scored by an LLM judge against a human-authored rubric. Both representations answered single-fact questions about equally well (9.33 vs. 9.96 of 10), but diverged sharply on questions requiring links across course units. The compiled wiki remained accurate and grounded (9.93; 100% grounded in cited sources), while retrieval scored lower and was markedly less grounded (8.14; 64%). The wiki's citations let students and instructors trace any claim back to the lecture that introduced it, adding a layer of dynamic retrieval that machine learning courses require. While further testing is needed, instructors using AI to support learning in ML courses should consider wiki-based structure for its potential to support foundational elements of best practice.

</details>


### [155] [Clarification Is Not Correction: LLMs Fail to Let Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25337)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Fei Wang,Robert Douglas,Rajeshkumar Golani,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出语言模型对话失败的核心并非"遗忘"，而是"过早承诺"——早期模糊意图过早坍缩为单一任务状态，后续澄清被当作附加语境而非纠错信号，导致顺序效应；作者通过写作、规划、编码等控制实验验证了这一机制，并主张研究重心应从"保留更多上下文"转向"保留不确定性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究习惯于把语言模型的对话失败归因于记忆问题（上下文过长、摘要丢失信息、约束被遗忘）。作者认为这掩盖了更深层的问题：在很多对话中模型并没有遗忘，而是"承诺得太早"——一个模糊的早期话轮过早坍缩为单一隐藏解释，后续澄清都被这一既有承诺过滤，即"早期后验坍缩"（early posterior collapse）。

Method: 作者定义了"早期后验坍缩"这一失败模式，并设计受控对话任务（涵盖写作、规划、编码三类），使用 Gemini-2.5-Pro 与 Gemini-2.5-Flash，在数千次试验中比较相同信息以不同顺序呈现时的结果差异（顺序效应实验）。同时评估了标准提示策略与记忆策略（摘要、思维链等）的效果。

Result: 数千次试验显示，即使最终对话含有等价的任务相关信息，信息顺序不同也会产生不同结果，证明后续澄清被当成额外语境而非纠错信号——它只精化旧的（stale）任务状态而不使其失效。编码任务尤其脆弱，早期假设易被嵌入接口、控制流等结构化产物中。标准提示与记忆策略不可靠：摘要可能使模糊性坍缩，思维链能减少推理痕迹中的显式错误承诺却并不提升最终任务成功率。

Conclusion: 这些发现促使作者提出"保不确定性状态管理"（uncertainty-preserving state management）：若助手无法放下早期解读，鲁棒性就不能只依赖事后纠错，而须阻止模糊早期话轮固化为单一任务状态——在模糊尚存时保持试探性假设，当高影响模糊持续时先询问再执行，当后续证据推翻早期解读时从修正后的状态重建。作者的目标不是给出某个提示词修复方案，而是将交互式大语言模型的研究方向从"保留更多上下文"转向"保留不确定性"。

Abstract: Dialogue failures in language models are usually framed as memory failures: context too long, summaries lossy, a constraint forgotten. We argue this misses a deeper problem: in many conversations the model does not forget, it commits too early. An ambiguous early turn collapses into a single hidden interpretation, and later clarification is filtered through that commitment. We call this early posterior collapse: unresolved user intent collapsing into a committed task state before ambiguity is resolved. We study it with controlled dialogue tasks in writing, planning, and coding using Gemini-2.5-Pro and Gemini-2.5-Flash. Across thousands of trials, the same information in different orders yields different outcomes, even when the final dialogue contains equivalent task-relevant information. This order effect suggests later clarification is treated as extra context rather than a corrective signal: it refines a stale task state without invalidating it. Coding tasks are especially vulnerable, suggesting early assumptions get embedded in structured artifacts such as interfaces and control flow. Standard prompting and memory strategies do not reliably help: summaries can collapse ambiguity, and chain-of-thought can reduce explicit wrong commitment in reasoning traces without improving final task success. These findings motivate uncertainty-preserving state management. If assistants cannot let go of early interpretations, robustness cannot rely on post hoc correction alone; it must keep ambiguous early turns from hardening into one task state. Assistants should hold tentative hypotheses while ambiguity remains, ask before executing when high-impact ambiguity persists, and rebuild from a revised state when later evidence invalidates an earlier reading. Rather than one prompting fix, we aim to redirect research for interactive LLMs from retaining more context toward preserving uncertainty.

</details>


### [156] [From Decorative to Load-Bearing: Task Difficulty Shapes the Causal Role of Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25366)
*Renee Jia,Di Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 continuation-based causal testing(基于续写的因果测试),通过截断并扰动思维链来度量 CoT 对最终答案的"承重性"(load-bearingness)。研究发现 CoT 承重性随任务难度变化:简单任务上模型静默绕过自身推理,困难任务上模型跟随被破坏的步骤并传播错误;任务难度对错误传播的主导作用远大于扰动类型;强化学习抑制了错误传播;行为模式可被线性探针读取但难以通过激活引导可靠控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链(CoT)监控只有在书面推理确实因果性地约束答案时才有意义。论文旨在检验模型是否真正"依赖"其 CoT 推理,而非仅在简单任务上生成看似自洽但实际被静默绕过的文字,从而揭示 CoT 作为监督信号在可读性与可靠性之间的结构性张力。

Method: 提出 continuation-based causal testing:对某一步推理施加扰动(ablation-patch),截断思维链,并强制模型从被破坏的前缀继续生成,度量该 CoT 对最终答案的承重性(一种区别于机制性忠实度的行为概念)。在 Gemma-2-9B-IT、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B 三个模型上,于 GSM8K、MMLU、BIG-Bench Hard 三个基准进行实验;采用 2x2 配对分析、28,584 次续写的方差分解、四变体裁判敏感性分析与双注释者盲评(n=500,Cohen's kappa=1.00)、以及线性探针与加性激活引导实验。

Result: CoT 承重性与模型相对任务难度相关:简单任务上模型静默绕过自身推理,困难任务上跟随损坏步骤并传播错误。错误传播率从 GSM8K 到 BBH 多步算术上升 16 倍;方差分解将 98.8% 的可解释偏差归因于任务难度,仅 0.8% 归因于扰动类型。推理专用强化学习抑制错误传播并压缩该梯度。错误传播/非传播标签对裁判提示不敏感,双注释者完全一致(kappa=1.00)。线性探针能区分静默绕过、自我纠正与错误传播,但加性激活引导的因果控制有限,最多仅翻转约 25% 的错误传播样本。

Conclusion: 该梯度对基于 CoT 的监督与 AI 安全监控构成结构性难题:在痕迹易读之处信号微弱,而在信号重要之处错误在监控者干预前已经传播。行为模式可读但不可靠可控,提示需要超出被动读取 CoT 的监控与控制方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is only meaningful if written reasoning causally constrains the answer. We introduce continuation-based causal testing, an ablation-patch intervention that perturbs one reasoning step, truncates the chain, and forces the model to continue from the corrupted prefix. It measures how load-bearing a CoT is for the final answer, a behavioral notion distinct from mechanistic faithfulness. Across Gemma-2-9B-IT, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B on GSM8K, MMLU, and BIG-Bench Hard, CoT load-bearingness tracks model-relative task difficulty: on easy tasks models silently bypass their own reasoning; on hard tasks they follow corrupted steps and propagate errors. A matched 2x2 analysis shows task difficulty dominates perturbation type: error propagation rises 16x from GSM8K to BBH multistep arithmetic, and a variance partition over 28,584 continuations attributes 98.8% of explained deviance to task difficulty versus 0.8% to perturbation type. Reasoning-specific RL suppresses error propagation and compresses the gradient. A four-variant judge-sensitivity analysis and blind two-annotator study (n=500) show the error-propagation vs. non-propagation label is invariant to judge prompt, with perfect inter-annotator agreement (Cohen's kappa = 1.00). This gradient creates a structural problem for CoT-based oversight and AI safety monitoring: where the trace is easy to read it carries little signal, and where it matters errors propagate before a monitor can intervene. Linear probes on hidden states separate silent bypass, self-correction, and error propagation, but additive activation steering provides limited causal control, flipping only about 25% of error-propagation cases at best. Behavioral mode is readable but not reliably controllable.

</details>


### [157] [Robust Failure, Conservative Repair: Textual Knowledge Distillation from Cross-Model Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25400)
*Andrew Ren,Haokun Liu,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于失败的文本知识蒸馏，提出 RFCR（鲁棒失败、保守修复）方法，通过从多模型共享失败中提取规则、用边界样例收紧规则适用边界、并在无有效规则时选择弃权，成功将 BIG-Bench Hard 上基准 cheat sheet 的准确率从 68.50% 提升至 71.25%，且不损害原本正确的样例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于失败的文本知识蒸馏旨在通过分析模型任务错误来发现知识缺口，但该知识的跨模型迁移并不稳定：规则原子可能是可迁移的任务知识，也可能是仅适用于源模型、会误导其他模型的推理补丁；此外注入的规则可能被误用到无关场景，导致模型因无关信息而错误翻转答案。因此需要一种更稳健的失败衍生规则提炼与注入方式。

Method: 作者提出 RFCR（Robust Failure, Conservative Repair），一种文本蒸馏流程。它从多个模型共享的失败中（而非单一模型的失败）提炼规则，利用边界样例（boundary cases）收紧规则的适用边界，并在找不到有效规则时采取弃权策略（abstain）。该流程建立在将训练样例蒸馏为任务专属 cheat sheet 的既有流水线之上，并设计了消融实验与跨模型诊断加以验证。

Result: 在 400 项的 BIG-Bench Hard 任务集上，RFCR 将基准 cheat sheet 的准确率从 68.50% 提升到 71.25%（+2.75 个百分点，95% 置信区间 [+1.25, +4.50]），且未对原本正确的样例造成性能退化。早期实验显示，仅从单一模型失败中蒸馏规则，在非源模型家族上表现不及基准 cheat sheet，进一步支持了跨模型共享失败与保守控制策略的有效性。

Conclusion: RFCR 表明，基于失败的文本知识蒸馏可以通过聚焦跨模型共享失败、收紧规则适用边界以及保守弃权来稳定跨模型迁移。其准确率提升来自新知识的注入与严格的规则应用控制两方面，为以可解释文本形式提升模型推理能力提供了稳健方案。

Abstract: Failure-based textual knowledge distillation aims to discover gaps in a model's knowledge by examining its task errors. The distilled knowledge can be useful for the reasoning of both this model ("source model") and other models. However, this transfer of knowledge may not be stable. We define a rule atom to be a standalone rule injected into a model's textual input at inference time. A rule atom can encode transferable task knowledge or model-specific reasoning patches that can confuse other models. Also, the injected rule atoms can be misapplied to unrelated cases, causing the model to incorrectly flip its answer based on irrelevant information. Building on a pipeline that distills training examples into task-specific cheat sheets that aid model reasoning, we examine when failure-derived rules can improve these cheat sheets. Our early experiment shows rule distillation from a single model's failures underperforms the baseline cheat sheet on non-source model families. This motivates Robust Failure, Conservative Repair (RFCR), a textual distillation procedure that derives rules from failures shared across models, sharpens their application boundaries using boundary cases, and abstains when no useful rule is found. On a 400-item BIG-Bench Hard task set, RFCR improves the baseline cheat sheets from 68.50% to 71.25% (+2.75 pp; 95% CI [+1.25,+4.50]) without performance degradation on previously correct cases. Ablations and cross-model diagnostics support that accuracy gains come from both new knowledge injection and strict rule-application control.

</details>


### [158] [Efficient Iterative Retrieval with Heterogeneous Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25405)
*Dohyun Park,Hubertus Franke,Daniel G. Waddington,Swaminathan Sundararaman,Yongjoo Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Orthrus，一个在统一推理循环中进行异构批处理的模型服务系统，将嵌入与生成模型共同调度，以提升吞吐量并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代信息检索越来越多地同时使用嵌入模型和生成模型来处理复杂查询，但现有服务系统将两类模型孤立执行，导致吞吐量低、GPU 利用率差。粗粒度的分区策略（如将 GPU 专用于特定任务）无法适应动态工作负载，并产生计算"气泡"（bubbles）。

Method: Orthrus 在统一推理循环中执行异构批处理，核心思路包括：1) 分块嵌入（chunked embedding）与增量池化（incremental pooling），以统一嵌入与生成两类计算模式；2) 以工作负载感知（workload-aware）的方式调整批次组成，优化 GPU 资源利用并消除计算气泡。

Result: 在四块 A100 GPU 上的评测显示，相比基线部署，Orthrus 在受控工作负载上实现了 1.28 倍至 4.52 倍的吞吐量提升，并在迭代式 RAG 基准上将端到端 p99 延迟最多降低 55.8%。

Conclusion: Orthrus 通过统一推理循环、分块嵌入与增量池化以及工作负载感知的批次组成调整，有效解决了嵌入与生成模型孤立执行带来的利用率低和计算气泡问题，在吞吐量与延迟上均显著优于基线系统。代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval increasingly employs both embedding and generative models to handle complex queries. However, current serving systems suffer from low throughput and poor GPU utilization because they execute these models in isolation. Coarse-grained partitioning, such as dedicating GPUs to specific tasks, fails to adapt to dynamic workloads and creates computational "bubbles". To address these, we present Orthrus, a serving system that performs heterogeneous batching within a unified inference loop. The primary challenge lies in unifying embedding and generation workloads with conflicting computational patterns while optimizing batch composition for high performance. Orthrus addresses these challenges through chunked embedding with incremental pooling and by adjusting batch composition in a workload-aware manner. Evaluation on four A100 GPUs shows that, relative to baseline deployments, Orthrus achieves 1.28$\times$--4.52$\times$ higher throughput on controlled workloads and up to 55.8% lower end-to-end p99 latency on an iterative-RAG benchmark. We release our code at https://github.com/illinoisdata/Orthrus .

</details>


### [159] [From Offline Proxies to Online Decisions: A Layered Engagement Evaluation Framework for Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25408)
*Xuanyi Li,Vaskar Nath,Hossein Amirkhani,Jay Li,Alex Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复用的离线代理信号构建与诊断流程，通过"行为标签→产品结果、分类器→候选行为、离线信号→实验效应"三重对齐，将离线评分转化为对在线A/B实验效应的可靠预估。实测显示复合信号F1达81.1%，远超原始分类器的34.3%，且无错向判定，可用于在稀缺实验流量分配前筛选候选模型与提示词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线A/B实验是衡量用户参与度的决策标准，但流量与读数时间限制了能测试的对话式AI改动数量。作者希望在不消耗treatment臂用户暴露的前提下，构建一个可离线计算的信号，并验证它是否与在线实验结果一致，从而在稀缺实验资源分配前对候选方案进行优先排序。

Method: 将离线代理视为三重对齐的链式结构：(1)行为标签对齐产品结果；(2)学习到的分类器对齐候选助手行为；(3)聚合离线信号对齐实验效应。配套评估协议以区间感知的决策一致性（对比离线与在线置信区间而非点估计）和实验内排序进行审计。实例化包含：固定评估套件对候选行为打分、一个训练用于预测会话/提示级参与度的参与分类器、以及将样本级分数差映射为在线模型级参与度增量的校准层。

Result: 在27个部署的多轮助手上收集489对离线-在线对比（候选臂对对照组）进行审计。主测试使用映射冻结后运行的8个实验中的113个对比，复合信号F1达81.1%（原始分类器仅34.3%），无错向判定（原始分类器有31次）。所有离线预测均在实验运行前计算，防止过拟合。

Conclusion: 证据支持使用该复合代理信号在稀缺实验流量分配前筛选候选方案，已实际应用于训练检查点和系统提示词调优的选择。

Abstract: Online A/B experiments are the decision standard for user engagement, but traffic and readout time limit how many conversational-AI changes can be tested. We ask whether an offline signal designed to be computable without treatment-arm user exposure agrees with the outcomes of those experiments. We contribute a reusable construction and diagnosis checklist that treats an offline proxy as a chain of three alignments: behavioral label to product outcome, learned classifier to candidate-assistant behavior, and aggregated offline signal to experiment effect. A companion evaluation protocol audits the whole composite by interval-aware decision agreement, which compares offline and online confidence intervals instead of point estimates, and by within-experiment ranking. The instantiation we evaluate comprises a fixed evaluation suite on which candidate behavior is scored, an engagement classifier trained to predict session/prompt level engagements, and a calibration layer mapping sample-level score differences to online model-level engagement deltas. We then report the audit: 489 paired offline-online contrasts (one candidate arm against its control) from 27 experiments on a deployed multi-turn assistant, spanning model checkpoints to system-prompt tuning. Our primary test uses the 113 contrasts from eight experiments that ran after the map was frozen: on these the composite reaches 81.1% F1, against 34.3% for the raw classifier score it is built on, and makes no wrong-direction calls where that raw score makes 31. Every offline prediction was computed before its experiment ran to prevent overfitting. The evidence supports using the composite to prioritize candidates before scarce experiment traffic is allocated---in our deployment of the experiment, selecting among training checkpoints and tuning system prompts.

</details>


### [160] [ZeroGate: Trust-Preserving Fast Paths for Governed AI Agent Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25443)
*Zexun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZeroGate 将“精确动作批准”与“持久化本地准入”分离：签发方发放短时效的 ActionPass，可信运行时适配器重建最终动作，本地门限校验绑定并消耗 nonce，SQLite 事务将 nonce 消耗、配额更新与准入回执耦合。论文阐述条件性决策保持命题，并通过合成基准、并发/崩溃实验与 Azure Blob 研究对比同步与预执行模式，指出预执行缩短了准入到派发边界但并非整体加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将授权环节前移可以缩短智能体（agent）的派发边界，且无需移除授权工作；但这也可能放行一个在其载荷、权限或相关状态已发生变化的动作。现有方案缺乏对“动作在最终执行时仍被同一策略授权”的显式保证，因此需要一种在保持授权一致性的前提下，把授权成本从派发关键路径上移开的机制。

Method: ZeroGate 将精确动作批准与持久化本地准入分离：签发方签署一个短生命周期的 ActionPass，可信运行时适配器在执行前重建最终动作，再由本地门限（gate）校验其绑定并消费其 nonce；用 SQLite 事务耦合 nonce 消耗、适用的配额更新和准入回执。论文提出条件性决策保持命题，即：在批准健全、所有策略依赖都被表示且最新、观察忠实、消费原子这四个前提下，本地准入成功意味着某一指定的同步策略在准入时点也会授权同一动作。实施层面仅凭实现本身并不保证“当前世界新鲜度”或“恰好一次”的远端效果。

Result: 评估分为三部分：手写的语义固定装置（fixtures）、受控并发与崩溃实验、以及一个 Azure Blob 研究，后者通过同一签发方和门限对比同步与预执行两种执行路径。两种模式都会铸造 exact-action pass；生命周期延迟包含准备与预执行批次驻留时间。在 4800 次云尝试中，预执行模式从准入到派发的 p95 为 9.802–11.374 ms，而同步模式在相同并发水平下为 25.018–334.000 ms。但预执行的平均完整生命周期在每个并发水平上都更长，说明边界改善并非净加速。

Conclusion: 贡献是一个显式的再验证契约、一个可持久化的参考边界，以及一份可审计的“授权成本支付位置”对比，而非一个新的密码学原语或通用性能前沿。

Abstract: Moving authorization earlier can shorten an agent's dispatch boundary without removing authorization work. It can also admit an action whose payload, authority, or relevant state has changed. ZeroGate separates exact-action approval from durable local admission: an issuer signs a short-lived ActionPass, and a trusted runtime adapter reconstructs the final action before a local gate checks its binding and consumes its nonce. A SQLite transaction couples nonce consumption, applicable quota updates, and an admission receipt. We state a conditional decision-preservation proposition: successful local admission implies that a specified synchronous policy would authorize the same action at the admission point, provided approval is sound, all policy dependencies are represented and current, observations are faithful, and consumption is atomic. The implementation alone establishes neither current-world freshness nor exactly-once remote effects. Evaluation separates authored semantic fixtures, controlled concurrency and crash experiments, and an Azure Blob study comparing synchronous and prepared execution through the same issuer and gate. Both modes mint an exact-action pass; lifecycle latency includes preparation and prepared-batch dwell. Across 4800 cloud attempts, prepared worker-admission-to-dispatch p95 ranges from 9.802 to 11.374 ms, versus 25.018 to 334.000 ms synchronously, across the tested concurrency levels. Prepared mean complete lifecycle is longer at every level: the boundary improvement is not a net speedup. The contribution is an explicit revalidation contract, a durable reference boundary, and an auditable comparison of where authorization cost is paid, not a new cryptographic primitive or a universal performance frontier.

</details>


### [161] [Rollout Efficiency in Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning Large Language Models: A Taxonomy and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25463)
*Niloofar Gholipour,Marcos Assuncao,Gursimran Singh,Timothy Yu,Rajkumar Buyya,Julien Gascon-Samson,Zhenan Fan,Yong Zhang,Xiaojie Xu,Yaqiang Yao,Xiaolong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了面向推理型强化学习（reasoning-oriented RL）中rollout效率优化的研究，从机制与瓶颈两个视角建立分类体系，分析各类技术的适用场景、组合机会与冲突，并指出现有效率评估的不足与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型强化学习虽能显著提升大语言模型在数学、编程等多步任务上的表现，但将大量训练成本转移到rollout阶段（即为策略更新生成轨迹的过程）。为降低这部分成本，同时保证训练数据的新鲜度（freshness）、一致性（consistency）与统计有效性（statistical validity），亟需高效的rollout机制。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，对近期关于推理型RL rollout效率的研究进行系统性梳理，从"机制（mechanism）"和"瓶颈（bottleneck）"两个维度建立分类体系（taxonomy），并对不同技术家族进行归类与对比分析。

Result: 建立了一套关于rollout效率的系统分类法；分析了不同技术家族如何针对不同的低效来源进行优化；揭示了不同技术组合的机会与潜在冲突；识别了当前在效率收益的评估与报告方面存在的缺口。

Conclusion: rollout效率是推理型RL的重要优化方向，需在降低成本与维持数据新鲜度、一致性和统计有效性之间取得平衡。该领域仍存在评估标准不统一、效率收益报告不完整等问题，未来应关注统一的评估框架与跨技术的系统性组合优化。

Abstract: Reasoning-oriented reinforcement learning enables large language models to solve mathematical, coding, and other multi-step tasks, but shifts a substantial portion of the training cost to rollout, where trajectories are generated for policy updates. Efficient rollout mechanisms are therefore essential to reduce this cost while maintaining the freshness, consistency, and statistical validity of training data. This survey provides a systematic taxonomy of recent research on rollout efficiency for reasoning-oriented reinforcement learning, classifying existing approaches from both mechanism and bottleneck perspectives. Based on this taxonomy, we analyze how different technique families address distinct sources of rollout inefficiency, examine opportunities and potential conflicts for combining them, identify gaps in the evaluation and reporting of efficiency gains, and discuss open challenges and future research directions.

</details>


### [162] [Direct Optimization of Generators for Search in Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25575)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何让微调大语言模型更好地服务于自动定理证明中的树搜索。作者将计算对齐训练(Compute-Aligned Training, CAT)扩展到策略引导的树搜索场景，提出可追踪的有迹损失(trace-supported losses)以及搜索无关的统一分配(UA)损失，两者都以标量权重调制逐策略的交叉熵梯度。在Lean基准上，两种方法在六种搜索策略上均比交叉熵取得更高证明成功率，且在预算较小(16次扩展)时相对交叉熵的优势大于预算较大(256次扩展)时，表明CAT随测试时计算量扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将LLM作为单次生成器时，交叉熵目标与扁平搜索(如聚合、过滤)中的实际部署目标不一致，已有工作开发新损失修正这种偏差。但将这种对齐扩展到树搜索更困难：证明发现依赖探索和从未被监督演示揭示的"离轨迹(off-trace)"状态中恢复，监督数据无法直接指导这些状态的训练信号。

Method: 作者将Compute-Aligned Training(CAT)框架扩展到策略引导搜索，通过对策略引导搜索过程做抽象，推导出可追踪、有迹(trace-supported)的损失函数；同时提出搜索无关的统一分配(uniform-allocation, UA)损失，该损失在不指定具体搜索策略的情况下考虑预算分配。两种损失都表现为对逐策略(per-tactic)交叉熵梯度施加标量权重。文中还刻画了离轨迹行为如何影响搜索感知权重的性质，并给出在大预算下近似误差消失的条件。

Result: 在Lean基准上，搜索感知损失和UA损失在六种搜索策略上都实现了比交叉熵更高的观察证明成功率，其中单个共享的UA适配器取得强结果。预算扫描实验显示，在16次扩展时相对交叉熵的增益大于256次扩展时，说明CAT带来的收益随测试时计算量缩放(即预算越受限时提升越明显)。

Conclusion: 将计算对齐训练扩展到树搜索是可行的，搜索感知损失与搜索无关的UA损失都能通过标量加权交叉熵梯度有效纠正训练与部署目标之间的错位，并优于纯交叉熵基线。方法展现了对搜索策略的泛化性(单一UA适配器即可适配多种搜索)和随测试时计算的可扩展性，为更高效的自动化定理证明模型训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) significantly advance Automated Theorem Proving (ATP), but are often deployed as guiding policies within tree search rather than for single-attempt generation. Recent work shows cross entropy is suboptimal for an LLM used in flat search strategies such as aggregation or filtering and that work has developed new loss functions to correct this misalignment. Extending this alignment to tree search is more challenging: proof discovery depends on exploration and recovery through off-trace states that supervised demonstrations do not reveal. We extend Compute-Aligned Training (CAT) to this setting through an abstraction of policy-guided search, deriving tractable, trace-supported losses. Alongside these search-aware losses, we introduce a search-agnostic uniform-allocation (UA) loss that accounts for the budget without specifying the specific search. Both induce scalar weights on per-tactic cross-entropy gradients. We characterize how off-trace behavior affects the search-aware weights, including conditions for vanishing approximation error at large budgets. On a Lean benchmark, both approaches achieve higher observed proof-success rates than cross-entropy across six search strategies, with strong results from a single shared UA adapter. Budget sweeps show larger gains over cross-entropy at 16 than at 256 expansions, implying CAT scales with test time compute.

</details>


### [163] [Real-Time Hand Gesture Recognition for OpenXR Using Transformer-Based Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25466)
*Salar Rezayani,Russell Butler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Transformer的实时手势识别系统，利用OpenXR标准在Unity中采集手部关节位置与手腕旋转角数据，捕捉短时间窗口内的时序依赖，实现跨手部朝向与尺寸的稳健手势分类，并展望了手势间运动流（过渡）检测的扩展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手势识别是人机交互的关键组成部分，可为游戏、虚拟现实、机器人等应用提供直观接口。现有方法在手部朝向和尺寸变化下的鲁棒性不足，且难以充分建模手势的时序动态特征。

Method: 本研究将基于Transformer的机器学习模型集成到实时手势识别中：通过OpenXR标准在Unity中捕获手部跟踪数据，提取手部关节的位置信息与手腕旋转角度作为特征；利用Transformer的序列建模能力，在短时间手势窗口内捕捉时序依赖关系，从而对手势进行分类。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在手势分类准确率上取得了显著提升，并能在不同手部朝向和手部尺寸下实现稳健的分类。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的方法能有效提升实时手势识别的准确性与鲁棒性；未来工作将扩展到检测手势之间的过渡（运动流），以支持连续手势序列的识别。

Abstract: Hand gesture recognition is a key component in human-computer interaction (HCI), enabling intuitive interfaces for applications in gaming, virtual reality (VR), robotics, and more. This study integrates transformer-based machine-learning models for real-time hand gesture recognition, using hand-tracking data captured through the OpenXR standard in Unity. We leverage positional data of hand joints and wrist rotation angles to train a custom gesture recognition system. By utilizing the sequential modeling capabilities of transformers, the system captures temporal dependencies within short gesture windows and classifies gestures robustly across hand orientations and sizes. The results show a significant improvement in gesture classification accuracy. Building on this, we outline how the approach can be extended toward detecting the flow of movement, i.e., the transitions between gestures, as future work.

</details>


### [164] [ShowTellArena: Evaluating Business Workflow Understanding from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25467)
*David Garg,Ritobrata Sarkar,Ehsan Azarnasab,Siddhartha Borah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShowTellArena 是一个用于检验智能体能否通过"边演示边讲解"的业务工作流课程进行学习的基准协议与公开数据集，v1.0 包含 50 个业务工作流任务和 502 道测验题，并提供了三个系统的探索性评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类往往通过"边示范边解释决策"来教同事，但缺乏一个统一的方法来检验智能体从同一堂讲解式业务演示课中实际学到了什么。因此需要一个固定业务场景与测验、允许多种教学界面采集课程的标准协议和公开数据集。

Method: 构建 ShowTellArena 基准协议与数据集：v1.0 涵盖 50 个业务工作流任务（金融、招聘、采购、客户决策、库存、物流），提供录像、截图、讲解、fixture 种子及 502 道问题。协议固定业务场景与测验，允许各产品通过自身教学界面采集课程，问题覆盖操作规则、边界、异常及自动化方案中的错误。对三个系统在 39 个工作流案例中的 218 次试点尝试进行分析（其中 28 个案例由三个系统均尝试过）。

Result: 探索性试点分析暴露了答案错误和未完成教学体验两类失败。同时说明了发布版本的验证缺口，以及试点中覆盖不均、排除项和评分来源等问题。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一个可检视、可由他人扩展的数据集与评测工作流；所选试点并非产品间的受控排名，仅为探索性参考。

Abstract: We often teach a colleague by showing the work and explaining the decisions as we go. How can we check what an agent understood from the same lesson? We introduce ShowTellArena, a benchmark protocol and public dataset for comprehension after narrated business demonstrations. The v1.0 release contains 50 business workflow tasks, with recordings, screenshots, narration, fixture seeds, and 502 questions. Tasks span finance, hiring, procurement, customer decisions, inventory, and logistics. The protocol holds the business scenario and quiz fixed while allowing each product to capture the lesson through its own teaching interface. Questions test operational rules, boundaries, exceptions, and errors in proposed automations. We analyze 218 selected pilot attempts across 39 workflow cases, including 28 cases attempted by all three evaluated systems. These exploratory results expose both answer errors and failures to complete the teaching experience. We describe the release's verification gaps and the pilot's uneven coverage, exclusions, and grading provenance. The contribution is an inspectable dataset and assessment workflow that others can extend; the selected pilot is not a controlled product ranking.

</details>


### [165] [RAG-NAROK: Retrieval-Aware Knowledge Corpus Poisoning in RAG with Source-specific Refutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25469)
*Abdullahil Kafi,Alvi Ataur Khalil*

Main category: cs.AI
