TL;DR: 本文提出 RAG-NAROK，一种针对检索增强生成（RAG）系统的查询自适应攻击框架。它利用 RAG 管线的透明性，先提取被检索到合法来源的身份，再生成明确贬低这些来源的"锚点针对性驳斥"文档，并借助时效性与权威性偏差诱导生成器输出目标答案，从而显著超越静态投毒基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识库投毒攻击是静态的：对抗文档被提前计算并注入，完全不考虑受害者系统针对具体查询实际会检索到什么内容，使得攻击对其在生成器上下文窗口中与其它文档竞争的情况"盲目"。作者旨在填补这一被忽视的对抗脆弱性研究空白。

Method: 提出 RAG-NAROK（Retrieval-Anchored Generation Negation And Response Quality Collapse）框架：利用 RAG 管线自身的透明特性，先提取被检索到的合法来源身份，再生成"锚点针对性驳斥（Anchor-Specific Refutation）"文档，这些文档明确点名并贬低被检索到的来源，同时利用生成模型对时效性（recency）和权威性（authority）的偏差，把文本生成引导至攻击者设定的目标答案。

Result: 实验表明，RAG-NAROK 在多个不同领域上显著优于静态投毒基线，成功诱导生成器输出目标答案并导致回答质量坍塌。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了 RAG 系统的透明性与 AI 安全之间存在根本性张力：RAG 依赖可验证外部知识所必需的透明管线，恰恰成为可被利用的攻击面，需要在系统设计上加以权衡与防护。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) systems have emerged as the dominant architecture for grounding large language model (LLM) outputs in verifiable external knowledge, yet their structural reliance on a dynamic retrieval pipeline introduces a largely unexplored class of adversarial vulnerability. Existing knowledge-base poisoning attacks are fundamentally static. Adversarial documents are pre-computed and injected without any awareness of what the victim system will actually retrieve for a given query, leaving the attack blind to the competitive documentary landscape that surrounds its payload in the generator's context window. Unlike traditional static poisoning attacks that are blind to the retrieved context, we introduce RAG-NAROK (Retrieval-Anchored Generation Negation And Response Quality Collapse), a RAG attack framework that adapts to the query text. RAG-NAROK exploits the transparency inherent in RAG pipeline to first extract the legitimate source identities, then generate Anchor-Specific Refutation documents that explicitly name and devalue retrieved sources while leveraging recency and authority biases to steer the text generation toward a target answer. Our results demonstrate that RAG-NAROK significantly outperforms static baselines across diverse domains, revealing a fundamental tension between RAG transparency and AI security.

</details>


### [166] [Spectra: A Rules-Driven LLM Pipeline for Automated KYC Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25474)
*Miray Wahib,Ethan Tran,Rea Mourad,Mira Muti,Nikita Dvornik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了资本市场KYC入职流程，将其四角色流程重组为可自动化的两角色流程，并提出Spectra平台，该平台结合规则引擎与LLM分类、抽取和验证代理，在真实KYC文档上实现100%分类准确率、89.4%抽取准确率，人工复核负担降低96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资本市场KYC入职流程依赖人工对异构文档进行分类、结构化数据抽取和合规校验，耗时耗力（单个客户需数周，且存在串行交接延迟）。作者通过分析发现该流程包含大量可重复、适合AI自动化的组件，因此动机在于重构流程以消除交接延迟，并将剩余步骤自动化，从而大幅缩短入职周期并减轻人工负担。

Method: 作者首先将传统四方流程整合为共享大部分工作、可共同自动化的两方流程，消除中间交接；随后提出Spectra平台：1) 结构化规则引擎，将合规政策编码为可查询数据库，实现聚焦式上下文注入，在提升抽取精度的同时降低token消耗；2) 将文档处理分解为相互隔离、可审计的多个阶段（而非单一宏大提示词），每个阶段独立优化并可与具体政策条款对应溯源；3) 结合LLM进行分类、抽取与验证代理。

Result: 在真实KYC文档上的评估显示：Spectra达到100%的分类准确率和89.4%的抽取准确率，同时人工复核负担下降了96%。

Conclusion: KYC入职流程中的可重复组件非常适合AI自动化。通过流程重构消除串行交接，并结合结构化规则引擎与分阶段、可审计的LLM代理，Spectra能够在保持高准确率（分类100%、抽取89.4%）的同时显著降低人工复核负担（下降96%），证明了该混合方案在资本市场合规场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Know Your Client (KYC) onboarding in capital markets requires analysts to manually classify documents, extract structured data from heterogeneous sources, and validate compliance against complex regulatory policies. This process requires significant analyst time per client, with end-to-end onboarding often stretching to multiple weeks due to sequential handoffs. In this work, we analyze an on-boarding process and find that it comprises repeatable components well-suited to AI automation. We therefore propose a restructured workflow to be amenable to automation: we consolidate the traditional four-party process into two parties that share most of the work and can be automated together, eliminating intermediate handoffs that compound delays. To automate the remaining steps, we introduce Spectra, an AI-assisted document processing platform that combines a structured rules engine with LLM-based classification, extraction, and validation agents. The rules engine encodes compliance policy as a queryable database, enabling focused context injection that reduces token usage while improving extraction precision. Rather than a single monolithic prompt, the system decomposes document processing into isolated, auditable stages, each optimized independently and traceable to specific policy clauses. In evaluation on real KYC documents, Spectra achieves 100% classification accuracy and 89.4% extraction accuracy. Human review burden dropped by 96%.

</details>


### [167] [Queer inclusion in speech datasets: An audit and taxonomy of practical tensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25491)
*Brooklyn Sheppard,Anaelia Ovalle,Adina Williams,Levent Sagun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文审计了多个语音数据集，发现其中 LGBTQIA+（酷儿）发言人的可测量占比极低（0-1.4%），不足以支撑稳健的差异测量，并提出一套张力分类法来解释语音技术数据集中边缘群体声音缺失的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音技术数据集普遍缺乏 LGBTQIA+（酷儿）等边缘群体的声音，作者希望探究这种缺失的深层原因，以及收集边缘群体语音数据所面临的挑战与张力。

Method: 以酷儿群体为案例，对六个多样的语音数据集进行审计，统计其中可测量的酷儿发言人比例；同时额外审计两个由酷儿社群“为酷儿、与酷儿共同”创建的语音科学数据集作对比；在此基础上归纳并构建一套描述数据收集价值观冲突的张力分类法。

Result: 审计发现六个语音数据集中可测量的酷儿代表性低（占发言人 0-1.4%），不足以进行稳健的差异测量；同时发现 AI 与语音技术研究中语音数据集收集的许多惯例，可能与边缘群体参与式方法所强调的价值观相冲突。

Conclusion: 语音技术数据集对酷儿群体的代表性严重不足，需要正视数据收集惯例与边缘群体参与式价值观之间的张力，并通过分类法加以描述和化解。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine speech datasets for their inclusion of LGBTQIA+, or queer, voices and provide a taxonomy of tensions to better understand why there is a lack of such voices in current speech technology datasets. Through an audit of six diverse speech datasets, we find that measurable queer representation is low (0-1.4% of speakers) - insufficient for robust disparity measurement. We take this community as a case study to consider what challenges and tensions are associated with collecting speech data from marginalized communities. For comparison, we audit an additional two datasets from the speech sciences that were created by, for, and with the queer community. We note that many customs in speech dataset collection efforts in AI and speech technology research may conflict with values emphasized in participatory approaches with marginalized communities, and provide a taxonomy describing these tensions.

</details>


### [168] [Towards participatory speech dataset curation: A queer case study and conceptual framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25496)
*Brooklyn Sheppard,Anaelia Ovalle,Adina Williams,Levent Sagun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以酷儿（LGBTQIA+）社区为案例，论证了建立参与式语音数据集创建框架的必要性，并提出一个由社区定义、项目制定、参与模式与个人自主四个双向重叠过程构成的概念框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 酷儿社区对AI存有已记录的担忧与伤害（如曾试图开发"gaydar"技术以识别个体性取向），而现有语音数据收集实践不适合与酷儿说话者合作，因此需要更公平、以社区为中心的参与式数据创建方式。

Method: 通过案例研究，回顾常见语音数据收集实践及其不适用的原因，梳理酷儿社区参与的参与式AI既有工作，以及其他边缘化社区语音数据收集的参与式尝试；在此基础上借鉴协同设计与知识共享的洞见，发展出参与式语音数据策展的概念框架。

Result: 提出一个由四个重叠且双向的过程构成的参与式语音数据策展框架：社区定义、项目制定、参与模式和个人自主，强调由边缘化社区主导、参与并受益。

Conclusion: 参与式、以社区为中心的语音数据策展框架能够更好地保障边缘化社区（如酷儿社区）在语音技术开发中的自主权与权益，为未来更公正包容的语音数据集建设提供指引。

Abstract: In this paper, we motivate the need for a participatory speech dataset creation framework through a case study of the LGBTQIA+, or queer, community - a community with documented concerns about AI and reported harms, including attempts to develop 'gaydar' technologies that purportedly identify individuals as queer. We review common speech data collection practices, why these methods may be unsuitable for engaging with queer speakers, and discuss previous efforts in participatory AI with queer community engagement, as well as participatory endeavours specific to speech data collection for other marginalized communities. From this review, we develop a conceptual framework for participatory speech data curation by, for, and with marginalized communities drawing on insights from co-design and knowledge sharing. We propose a framework comprising overlapping and two-way processes of defining a community, project formulation, modes of participation, and personal autonomy.

</details>


### [169] [Improved Multiplayer Bandit Algorithm for Bernoulli Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26213)
*Khang Nguyen,Ricardo Parada,William Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究信息不对称的多玩家多臂老虎机问题，用 KL 散度置信区间替代 Hoeffding 型区间，提出 mKL-UCB、mKL-UCB-Intervals、mKL-DSEE 三个算法，在三种信息结构下获得严格更紧的遗憾界，改进因子至少为 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息不对称多玩家多臂老虎机研究多依赖 Hoeffding 型置信区间，其置信界较宽松，导致遗憾界不够紧。作者希望借助 KL 散度（Bernoulli 奖励情形下比 Hoeffding 界更精确）来严格收紧遗憾保证，尤其在奖励均值接近 0 或 1 时获得更大改进。

Method: 针对三种信息结构——动作不对称、奖励不对称、两者兼有——分别设计算法：mKL-UCB、mKL-UCB-Intervals 和 mKL-DSEE。核心思路是用 KL 散度置信区间替换 Hoeffding 型区间；通过 Pinsker 不等式证明改进因子至少为 2；对于奖励不对称情形，证明两支臂的 KL 区间在确定样本量后即可分离，且 M 个独立玩家可进一步加速淘汰。

Result: KL 型算法在三种信息结构下均获得严格更紧的遗憾界；由 Pinsker 不等式可知改进因子至少为 2，且在奖励均值接近 0 或 1 时改进更大；奖励不对称情形下两支臂的 KL 区间在确定性样本数后分离，M 个独立玩家进一步加速淘汰过程。

Conclusion: 用 KL 散度置信界替代 Hoeffding 型区间能系统性地收紧信息不对称多玩家老虎机问题的遗憾界，改进幅度至少 2 倍，且对极端概率更显著，体现了 KL 型界在 Bernoulli 奖励场景下的优势。

Abstract: We study the multiplayer multi-armed bandit problem with information asymmetry under Bernoulli rewards, for three information structures: asymmetry in actions, in rewards, and in both. Replacing the Hoeffding-style confidence intervals of prior work with Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence-based bounds gives strictly tighter regret guarantees in each case. We propose \texttt{mKL-UCB}, \texttt{mKL-UCB-Intervals} and \texttt{mKL-DSEE}, and show that the improvement factor is at least two by Pinsker's inequality and far larger when reward means are near zero or one. For asymmetry in rewards we prove that two arms' KL intervals separate after a deterministic number of samples, and that $M$ independent players accelerate elimination further.

</details>


### [170] [SMTB: Fast Structure-Mapping with Tight Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25508)
*Daniel Weitekamp,Christopher MacLellan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的结构映射算法 SMTB，它基于共享结构而非表面特征进行类比，相比 SME 快 5–15 倍，在大规模嵌套领域中找映射的准确率提升约 50%，并作为认知规则引擎 CRE 的核心组件，提供 Python 接口和跨语言兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构映射引擎 SME 偏向树状谓词逻辑中的高阶对应，导致其在任意关系图上表现不佳，且在处理大规模嵌套领域时效率与准确率不足；作者希望设计一种不偏袒高阶关系、更通用且更快的结构映射算法。

Method: 提出 SMTB（Structure-Mapping with Tight Bounds）算法，通过最大化关系连通性而非赋予高阶关系特权来实现结构映射；并将其集成到认知规则引擎 CRE 框架中，该框架提供多语言兼容和 Python 接口，底层为 C++ 实现。SMTB 支持任意关系图及树状嵌套谓词逻辑等多种表示方式。

Result: SMTB 比 SME 快 5–15 倍，在大规模嵌套领域中找映射的效果提升约 50%；在 SME 语料库 5845 个领域对上完成了对比评估，并展示了 SMTB 如何规避 SME 因偏袒高阶关系而遇到的失败模式。

Conclusion: SMTB 通过不偏袒高阶关系、最大化关系连通性，在任意关系图上与树状域中均表现良好，克服了 SME 的局限，证明了其在性能和通用性上的优势，使其成为 CRE 中结构映射的核心算法。

Abstract: Structure-mapping forms analogies by aligning systems of relationally connected elements based on shared structure instead of surface features. We introduce a new structure-mapping algorithm: Structure-Mapping with Tight Bounds (SMTB) that is 5--15x faster than the structure-mapping engine (SME) and about 50\% better at finding mappings in large nested domains. SMTB is part of the broader Cognitive Rule Engine (CRE) project, a flexible multi-language-compatible framework with an accessible Python interface to state-of-the-art C++ implementations of core algorithms commonly used in cognitive systems such as pattern matching, planning, and structure-mapping. CRE and SMTB are designed to work with a wide range of representation choices. Unlike SME, which biases higher-order correspondences in tree-like predicate logic, SMTB maximizes relational connectivity without privileging higher-order relations. This allows SMTB to work just as well over arbitrary relational graphs as it does in tree-like domains of nested predicate logic. We discuss situations where privileging "higher-orderness" in structure-mapping can cause issues, and illustrate how SMTB avoids failure modes that SME would encounter in these situations. We also provide an evaluation comparing SMTB to SME v4 over 5845 domain pairs from the SME corpus.

</details>


### [171] [Weakly Supervised Quantum Error Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25555)
*Seyed Mohamad Ali Tousi,G. N. DeSouza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需理想标签的无监督量子错误缓解方法：从电路结构与硬件校准中构建16个启发式标签函数，用概率标签模型融合其分歧，得到每个量子比特的读出错误概率，再通过逆读出通道缓解测量分布偏移。在IBM两台设备的147,000个五比特电路上，KL散度分别降低24.3%和28.8%，显著优于最强已发表基线（15.4%/21.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有监督的量子错误缓解需要理想输出作为标签，而生成理想输出依赖无噪声经典模拟，其代价随系统规模指数增长。这意味着在最需要缓解的大规模系统中反而无法获得监督信号，因此亟需能取代理想标签的廉价、可单独获取的信号来源。

Method: 方法为无监督/弱监督框架：先基于电路结构和硬件校准构建16个启发式标签函数（稳定子约束、宇称约束、弛豫与读出特征、局部深度、门计数、邻近活动等），再用概率标签模型（probabilistic label model）调和这些标签函数间的分歧，生成每个量子比特的错误概率，将其视为读出通道，取逆通道对实测分布进行缓解。整个训练路径不引入任何理想输出。

Result: 在IBM Algiers与Hanoi两台设备上执行147,000个五比特电路，方法分别移除24.3%和28.8%的KL散度（相对理想分布），而最强已发表解析基线仅移除15.4%和21.5%；该优势在双设备上一致且远超bootstrap区间。相比之下，训练于理想分布的有监督神经模型在有标签时仍更强，作者量化了这一差距，而非回避。

Conclusion: 廉价的结构与校准信号能够作为理想标签的无监督替代品用于读出错误缓解，并在实验中稳定超越现有解析基线。方法的成立边界明确限定于无理想标签（即经典模拟不可行）的规模区间；在有标签可得时，有监督神经模型仍占优，作者对这一差距进行了定量刻画。

Abstract: Supervised approaches to quantum error mitigation learn a map from noisy circuit outputs to ideal ones, and therefore require the ideal outputs. Producing those ideal outputs demands noiseless classical simulation, whose cost grows exponentially with system size, so supervision is unavailable in exactly the regime where mitigation matters most. We ask whether cheap, individually unreliable signals drawn from circuit structure and hardware calibration can take the place of ideal labels. We assemble sixteen heuristic labeling functions (stabilizer and parity constraints, relaxation and readout characteristics, local depth, gate counts, and neighboring activity), reconcile their disagreements with a probabilistic label model, and read the resulting per-qubit error probabilities as a readout channel whose inverse mitigates the measured distribution. No ideal output enters the training path. On $147{,}000$ five-qubit circuits executed on two IBM devices, the method removes $24.3\%$ (Algiers) and $28.8\%$ (Hanoi) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence to the ideal distribution, against $15.4\%$ and $21.5\%$ for the strongest published analytical baseline, a margin that holds on both devices and lies far outside its bootstrap interval. Supervised neural models trained on ideal distributions remain stronger where such labels exist, and we quantify that gap rather than setting it aside; the method's claim is to the regime where they do not, since the labels they require cannot be computed for the circuits mitigation is needed for. The codes will be released shortly.

</details>


### [172] [Recovering Agentic Sovereignty: Mitigating the Consensus Paradox via Contrastive Epistemic Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25570)
*Dahlia Shehata,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对比认知解码（CED），一种无需微调、基于单一模型双前向传播的零样本推理干预方法，用于抑制大语言模型对"对抗性群体共识"的谄媚（sycophancy）偏见，在不破坏语法的情况下显著提升复杂基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）存在对"对抗性群体共识"的参数化脆弱性，即会屈从或谄媚于模拟的群体意见，导致"认知偷懒"（cognitive loafing）与性能下降。现有对比解码（CD）依赖较弱的辅助模型，部署成本高且不够精准，因此需要一种无需额外模型、仅靠单一架构即可隔离并抑制这种从众偏见的干预方法。

Method: 提出零样本推理干预方法 CED（Contrastive Epistemic Decoding）。与依赖较弱次级模型的标准 CD 不同，CED 在单一模型上执行双前向传播，以隔离"从众偏见"信号；并引入新颖的非对称、零下界概率钳制（asymmetric, zero-bounded probability clamp）与离散 top-k 截断掩码，从数学上抑制"有毒共识"词元，同时避免语法崩溃。在 Gemma-2 (9B)、Llama-3.1 (8B)、Mistral v0.3 (7B) 上，于 GAIA、SWE-bench、Multi-Challenge 等复杂基准的 7,200 条成对轨迹上评估。

Result: CED 成功中和了架构性与位置性偏见；将认知偷懒降低最高 33.00%（绝对值），带来最高 30.75% 的准确率恢复。恢复"主权"后模型表现出不同的架构性行为——Gemma-2 呈现被动任务专注，Llama-3.1 则主动反驳模拟群体——表明 CED 在无需微调的情况下剥离了"顺从"与"能力"之间的耦合。

Conclusion: CED 作为一种单模型、零样本的推理干预，能有效消除 LLM 对对抗性群体共识的谄媚偏见，既压制有毒共识词元又维持语法完整性，显著恢复性能，并将"合规（compliance）"与"能力"解耦，无需任何微调。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit a parametric vulnerability to adversarial swarm consensus. To mitigate this sycophancy, we introduce Contrastive Epistemic Decoding (CED), a zero-shot inference intervention. Unlike standard Contrastive Decoding (CD) which relies on a weaker secondary model, CED utilizes a dual forward-pass on a single architecture to isolate conformity bias. By introducing a novel asymmetric, zero-bounded probability clamp and discrete top-k truncation mask, CED mathematically suppresses toxic consensus tokens without causing grammatical collapse. Evaluated across 7,200 paired trajectories on complex benchmarks (GAIA, SWE-bench, Multi-Challenge) using Gemma-2 (9B), Llama-3.1 (8B), and Mistral v0.3 (7B), CED successfully neutralizes architectural and positional biases. By reducing cognitive loafing by up to 33.00% absolute, CED drives significant performance gains, yielding up to a 30.75% accuracy recovery. Regaining sovereignty induces distinct architectural behaviors---passive task-focus in Gemma-2 and active refutation of the simulated swarm in Llama-3.1---showing CED decouples compliance from capability without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [173] [A Behavioral Trait Leaks into Preferences: Diagnosing Trait Interference in LLM User Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25572)
*Chaehyun Kim,Sein Kim,Hongseok Kang,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于LLM的用户模拟器中偏好属性与行为活跃度特质之间的独立性失效问题，并提出页面级质量锚定方法PQA加以修复，从而提升推荐系统评估的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的用户模拟器通过注入特质来弥合推荐系统评估中的离线-在线差距，其中偏好属性决定用户参与什么内容、行为活跃度特质决定浏览时长。作者发现这种预期的特质独立性在实际模拟中会崩溃，导致两个关键失败：特质干扰（活跃度放大扭曲偏好边界，迫使用户持续浏览不匹配物品）和评估失效（满意度分数随活跃度驱动的页面数膨胀，使评估偏向特质分布而非推荐器性能）。

Method: 提出PQA（Page-level Quality Anchoring，页面级质量锚定）方法：使用反映每位用户内在偏好标准的个性化锚点来引导模拟器，在进一步浏览前判断当前页面是否达到该质量标准，从而使模拟器能够主动退出低质量页面，让活跃度特质仅在其预期角色——调节符合偏好页面内的浏览深度——上发挥作用。

Result: 实验表明PQA能够缓解特质干扰，并在活跃度变化下提升基于LLM的模拟器评估的可靠性。

Conclusion: PQA通过页面级质量锚定恢复了用户模拟器中偏好与活跃度特质的独立性，有效解决了特质干扰和评估失效问题，为基于LLM的推荐器离线评估提供了更可信的方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM-based user simulators aim to bridge the offline-online gap in recommender evaluation by emulating users through injected traits, where preference attributes determine what a user engages with and a behavioral activity trait governs how long they browse. However, we show this intended trait independence collapses during simulation, causing two failures: (i) Trait Interference, where amplified activity distorts preference boundaries and forces interactions with mismatched items to sustain browsing, and (ii) Evaluation Invalidity, where satisfaction scores inflate with activity-driven page counts despite taste mismatches, biasing evaluation toward trait distributions rather than recommender performance. To resolve this, we propose PQA, a page-level quality anchoring method that guides simulators using a personalized anchor reflecting each user's intrinsic preference standard. By assessing whether a page meets this standard before further browsing, PQA enables proactive exits from low-quality pages, letting the activity trait retain its intended role of modulating browsing depth within preference-conforming pages. Experiments show PQA mitigates trait interference and improves the reliability of LLM-based simulator evaluation under activity shifts. Our code is available at https://github.com/chaehyun1/PQA

</details>


### [174] [Gaze responses to false-positive computer-aided detection prompts during colonoscopy: a paired-video and real-time eye-tracking study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25581)
*Te Luo,Yan Zhu,Peiyao Fu,Ruijie Yang,Xian Yang,Quanlin Li,Pinghong Zhou,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过事件锁定眼动追踪，在回顾性与前瞻性研究中量化了结肠镜检查中CADe假阳性提示对操作者凝视吸引与注意力占用的影响，发现假阳性提示频繁捕获注意力且持续时间远超提示可见时长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机辅助检测（CADe）的假阳性提示可能转移内镜医师的注意力，但单个提示对注意力的具体影响尚不清楚，缺乏定量证据。

Method: 采用事件锁定眼动追踪方法，结合两项互补研究：回顾性配对视频实验（3名高年资与2名低年资内镜医师观看60段有/无CADe辅助的结肠镜视频）和前瞻性实时研究（9名高年资内镜医师执行42例实时CADe辅助结肠镜，记录凝视数据）。将专家标注病灶窗口之外的CADe提示界定为假阳性伪影事件，量化凝视吸引率、注意力占用时长及时间放大倍数。

Result: 假阳性提示在48.6%（68/140）的回顾性观察和65.2%（533/817）的前瞻性事件中吸引凝视。在完全恢复的吸引事件中，注意力占用中位时长为回顾性1000ms、前瞻性1100ms，而对应提示中位显示时长仅为33ms和267ms，时间放大倍数分别为17.55倍与5.15倍。配对回顾性比较显示，可见伪影提示比无辅助的同坐标参考点更能将凝视拉向提示区域。病灶凝视识别率无CADe为98.0%、有CADe为99.0%，且有CADe时首次进入病灶区域的凝视提前147.8ms。

Conclusion: 在受控与实时临床环境中，假阳性CADe提示频繁捕获凝视，且注意力持续时间超出提示可见时长。研究结果支持在CADe的评估与设计中纳入提示相关注意力负担的考量。

Abstract: False-positive computer-aided detection (CADe) prompts may divert endoscopists' attention during colonoscopy, yet the attentional impact of individual prompts remains unclear. We used event-locked eye tracking to quantify gaze attraction and attention occupation in complementary retrospective and prospective studies. In a retrospective paired-video experiment, 3 senior and 2 novice endoscopists viewed 60 colonoscopy videos with and without CADe. The prospective study recorded gaze during 42 real-time CADe-assisted colonoscopies performed by 9 senior endoscopists. Screened CADe prompts outside expert-annotated lesion windows were classified as false-positive artifact events. False-positive prompts attracted gaze in 48.6% (68/140) of retrospective observations and 65.2% (533/817) of prospective events. Among attraction events with complete recovery, median attention occupation lasted 1000 ms in the retrospective study and 1100 ms in the prospective study. Corresponding median prompt durations were 33 ms and 267 ms, with median time amplifications of 17.55-fold and 5.15-fold, respectively. In paired retrospective comparisons, visible artifact prompts drew gaze closer to the prompted region than did the same-coordinate unassisted reference. Secondary retrospective analyses showed high lesion gaze recognition without and with CADe (98.0% versus 99.0%). First gaze entry into lesion regions occurred 147.8 ms earlier with CADe. Across controlled and real-time clinical settings, false-positive CADe prompts frequently captured gaze, with attention persisting beyond prompt visibility. These findings support considering prompt-related attentional burden in CADe evaluation and design.

</details>


### [175] [Transformer Heads Looking for Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25588)
*Jasper van Doornmalen,Alexander Kozachinskiy,Corinna Mathwieser,Tomasz Steifer,Felipe Urrutia,José Verschae,Przemysław Andrzej Wałȩga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明：判断一个比特序列是否有序这一问题，无法由单头单层 Transformer 完成，但可以由双头单层 Transformer 完成；且与前人类似结果不同的是，本文假设的模型中 Transformer 带有输出 MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有工作研究了 Transformer 的表达能力（尤其关于序列顺序判别等任务的上下界），但这些结果通常建立在特定的架构假设上（例如无输出 MLP）。本文旨在澄清在“带输出 MLP”这一更贴近实际 Transformer 架构的设定下，单头与双头单层 Transformer 在“检查比特序列是否有序”这一任务上的能力边界。

Method: 本文采用表达能力（可能性/不可能性）分析方法：一方面构造性地给出一个双头单层（带输出 MLP）Transformer，证明其能够完成“比特序列是否有序”的判断；另一方面通过反证/归约论证，证明任何单头单层（带输出 MLP）Transformer 都无法解决该问题。核心在于单头注意力的信息聚合限制，以及输出 MLP 无法弥补注意力头缺失所导致的判别能力不足。

Result: 得到结论：在带输出 MLP 的单层 Transformer 模型中，“比特序列是否有序”判定问题对单头而言不可行，而对双头可行，从而在该架构设定下明确区分了 1-head 与 2-head 的能力差异。

Conclusion: 本文在带输出 MLP 的更现实架构假设下，建立了单层 Transformer 的注意力头数与该序列判定任务可实现性之间的严格对应关系，补充并细化了此前关于 Transformer 表达能力的理论结果。

Abstract: In this note, we show that the problem of checking, whether a sequence of bits is ordered, is not doable by 1-head 1-layer transformers but is doable by a 2-head 1-layer transformer. Unlike similar previous results, our results assume the model where transformers have an output MLP.

</details>


### [176] [Evaluating Coding Agents on Kernel Exploit Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25591)
*Junyoung Jang,Gwanhyun Lee,Hwiwon Lee,Kyuheon Kim,Jongseong Kim,Jinho Jung,Lingming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KEX-bench 是一个评估编码智能体针对真实操作系统内核生成漏洞利用原语能力的基准，包含 40 个 Linux/Windows CVE 的 45 个任务，结果表明当前智能体在无参考 PoC 时成功率有限、有参考 PoC 时显著提升，暴露出从内核崩溃到形成利用原语之间的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体虽能发现生产软件中的真实漏洞，但漏洞发现结果无法衡量智能体能否进一步构造可用的利用原语（如地址泄露、IP 控制、堆读写、任意地址写等），因此需要一个专门针对利用原语生成的基准来量化这一关键能力。

Method: 提出 KEX-bench 基准：覆盖 40 个 Linux 和 Windows CVE、共 45 个任务实例，涵盖内核地址泄露、指令指针控制、堆读、堆写和任意地址写五类利用原语；每个任务在隔离虚拟机中运行，暴露受控的工具集，并用确定性的验证器按原语类型判定是否成功；在固定工具调用预算下，评估搭配前沿模型与开放权重模型的最先进编码智能体配置。

Result: 在无参考 PoC 的条件下，最强配置在 20 个 Windows 任务中仅解决 1 个（5.0%），在 25 个 Linux 任务中解决 14 个（56.0%）；提供参考 PoC 后，最强配置解决 45 个任务中的 31 个（68.9%）。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了智能体能够触发内核崩溃、却难以将内核状态塑造成可利用原体的显著能力差距；KEX-bench 已在 https://kex-bench.github.io 开源，以支持 AI 辅助利用领域的可复现研究。

Abstract: Coding agents now find real vulnerabilities in production software. However, bug discovery results do not measure whether agents can construct exploit primitives. We introduce KEX-bench, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on exploit primitive generation against real operating-system kernels. KEX-bench contains 45 task instances across 40 Linux and Windows CVEs, covering kernel address leak, instruction-pointer control, heap read, heap write, and arbitrary address write. Each task runs in an isolated virtual machine, exposes controlled tools, and uses a deterministic verifier to check primitive-specific success. We evaluate state-of-the-art coding agents paired with frontier and open-weight models under fixed tool-call budgets. Without a reference proof of concept (PoC), the strongest configuration solves 1 of 20 Windows tasks (5.0%) and 14 of 25 Linux tasks (56.0%). With a reference PoC, the strongest configuration solves 31 of 45 tasks (68.9%). This highlights the gap where agents reach kernel crashes but fail to shape kernel state into exploit primitives. We release KEX-bench for reproducible research on AI-assisted exploitation at https://kex-bench.github.io.

</details>


### [177] [ArticleMiner: Ontology-Guided Knowledge Graph Construction from Scientific Publications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25607)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Craig A. Knoblock,Jay Pujara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ArticleMiner 框架，通过"通用论文解析+证据融合+人为编写的领域任务模块"这一中间路径，从论文及补充材料中恢复结构化科学事实，并在四个领域（药物化学、材料科学、机器学习、矿物地球化学）的163篇论文上验证其优于同 LLM 的 few-shot 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格中数字的含义依赖于表头、标题、单位、分析方法及领域惯例，恢复表格行列不等于恢复其表达的科学事实。现有语义表格解释方法假设已存在干净表格，而多数文献级抽取系统仅面向单一领域，缺乏一个在通用解析与领域知识之间取得平衡的方案。

Method: 提出 ArticleMiner 框架：采用"阅读论文及其补充文件—聚合多个解析器与一个语言模型产生的证据—协调整合这些证据"的共享流程；同时为每个任务提供一个有界限的、人工编写的任务模块，该模块列明图可用的规范名称、可映射到它们的表面形式、少量派生规则与有效性约束、身份键以及用于写 RDF 的绑定，从而定义任务允许输出什么，而不试图枚举领域的所有惯例。文章构建了四个任务模块（药物发现化学、材料科学、机器学习、矿物地球化学）。

Result: 在163篇论文上评测，包含一个专家标注的新的地球化学基准。与同一 LLM 的 few-shot 基线相比，点估计在全部四个任务上均有利于 ArticleMiner，但两个较小基准上存在不确定性。地球化学对比还包含对补充文件的访问，因此其提升不能仅归因于领域引导，也说明补充材料解析带来的收益。

Conclusion: 通过将通用证据收集流程与简洁的、人工编写的领域任务模块相结合，ArticleMiner 能以折中方式有效且可扩展地从科学论文中抽取结构化事实，且在多个领域上优于同 LLM 的 few-shot 基线。

Abstract: Scientific papers keep much of their quantitative content in tables and supplementary files, where a number means something only through its header, caption, unit, analytical method, and the conventions of its field. Recovering the rows and columns of a table is therefore not the same as recovering the scientific fact it reports. Most semantic table-interpretation methods assume that a clean table is already available and subsequently map its cells or columns to ontology terms, whereas most publication-level extraction systems are designed for a single domain. We study a middle path: a shared process that reads a paper and its supplementary files, gathers evidence from several parsers and a language model, and reconciles that evidence, while a bounded human-authored task module for each task supplies the domain meaning. The module lists the canonical names the graph may use, the surface forms that map to them, a small set of derivation rules and validity constraints, an identity key, and the bindings used to write RDF. It defines what a task is allowed to emit; it does not try to list every convention of a field. We build four such modules (for drug-discovery chemistry, materials science, machine learning, and mineral geochemistry) in the ArticleMiner framework, and evaluate them on 163 papers, including a new geochemistry benchmark with expert-curated ground truth. In comparisons against a same-LLM few-shot baseline, the point estimates favor ArticleMiner on all four tasks, with uncertainty on the two smaller benchmarks. The geochemistry comparison also includes access to supplementary files, so its improvement cannot be attributed to domain guidance alone.

</details>


### [178] [Reasoning-Preserving Fine-Tuning of Post-RL LLMs with Null-Basis LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25618)
*Wenzhi Fang,Nicholas Tzou,Lazar Valkov,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 NB-LoRA，一种参数高效的适配方法，通过固定近似零空间基对 LoRA 更新做重参数化，在让 RL 后训练 LLM 适配新知识领域/行为的同时，保留其已获得的推理能力，且额外训练开销很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 后训练已能有效激发 LLM 的推理能力，但后续用 SFT 适配新领域或行为会严重覆盖这些推理能力。现有缓解遗忘的方法（经验回放、专门初始化、基于梯度投影的约束优化）要么保留效果有限，要么训练开销过大。作者的分析发现推理激活集中于低维子空间，留下了大量可适配的零空间容量，且近似零空间可从小量样本可靠估计。

Method: 将推理保留形式化为逐层隐藏状态保留约束，并从推理激活中构造固定的近似零基；随后将 LoRA 更新通过该零基重新参数化，在整个微调过程中始终满足保留约束。

Result: 在多个 RL 训练后的 LLM 和多种下游任务上的实验表明，NB-LoRA 在适配性能上与标准 LoRA 相当，推理准确率保持在微调前水平附近，且该保留能力泛化到未见过的（held-out）推理基准上。

Conclusion: NB-LoRA 能有效平衡"新能力适配"与"已有推理能力保留"，以较小的参数开销和训练开销实现近似零空间引导的约束微调，为 RL 后训练模型的安全适配提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL)-based post-training has become an effective approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs). However, adapting post-RL models to new knowledge domains or behaviors through subsequent supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can severely overwrite these capabilities. Existing approaches mitigate such forgetting through experience replay, specialized initialization, or constrained optimization using gradient projection, but either provide limited preservation or incur substantial training overhead. Our analysis shows that reasoning activations concentrate in low-dimensional subspaces, leaving substantial null-space capacity for adaptation, and that the corresponding approximate null spaces can be reliably estimated from a modest number of examples. Motivated by these observations, we propose Null-Basis Low-Rank Adaptation (NB-LoRA), a parameter-efficient method for adapting post-RL LLMs while preserving their acquired reasoning ability. We formulate reasoning retention as a layer-wise hidden-state preservation constraint and construct a fixed approximate null basis from reasoning activations. LoRA updates are then reparameterized through this basis, enforcing the preservation constraint throughout fine-tuning. Extensive experiments across multiple RL-trained LLMs and diverse downstream tasks show that NB-LoRA matches standard LoRA in adaptation performance, maintains reasoning accuracy near pre-fine-tuning levels, and generalizes this preservation to held-out reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [179] [ChatT2: An Adaptive Framework for Developing a Large Language Model-Based Agent for Natural Product Domain Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25620)
*Yihan Wang,Qiandi Gao,Yihui Zhuang,Liangjun Ge,Heqian Zhang,Jiaquan Huang,Zhiwei Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChatT2，一个专为细菌 II 型聚酮类天然产物定制的、基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（导师-执行者-评估者），旨在降低新手探索微生物天然产物的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物天然产物的科学探索对新手存在显著挑战，包括微生物系统复杂性、生化多样性、技术技能要求以及生物信息学与数据分析流程的高门槛；通用大语言模型在理解有限且专业化的语料和复杂生物信息方面能力不足，难以有效支持这一领域。

Method: 构建 ChatT2——一个面向细菌 II 型聚酮的自主多智能体框架，包含三个角色：导师（mentor）作为用户与系统之间的中介，采用思维链提示（chain-of-thought prompting）细化用户意图；执行者（executor）在导师指导下，通过检索增强生成（RAG）技术综合多模态信息，并无缝整合生物信息学与化学信息学工具；评估者（evaluator）评估执行者输出，确保所检索信息的丰富性与准确性。

Result: 研究表明，基于该多智能体框架设计的 ChatT2 能够有效应对通用大语言模型在有限专业化语料与复杂生物信息理解上的不足，为专家和新手提供探索各类天然产物的实用工具。

Conclusion: ChatT2 通过导师-执行者-评估者的多智能体协作架构，结合思维链与检索增强生成，成功弥合了通用 LLM 与天然产物领域专业需求之间的差距，其 Web 服务器已上线（https://chatt2.site/#/chat）。

Abstract: Scientific investigations into microbial natural products (NPs) present significant challenges for novices, largely due to the complexity of microbial systems, biochemical diversity, technical skill requirements, and the demands of bioinformatics and data analysis processes. To address these issues, we introduce ChatT2, a large language model (LLM)-based agent that is specifically tailored to the unique characteristics of bacterial type II polyketides. These polyketides form a structurally distinct and therapeutically important NP family. ChatT2 was developed within an autonomous multiagent framework composed of a mentor, an executor, and an evaluator, each with defined responsibilities. The mentor acts as an intermediary between ChatT2 and the user, utilizing chain-of-thought prompting to refine the intent of the user. Under the guidance of the mentor, the executor synthesizes multimodal information via retrieval-augmented generation techniques and seamlessly integrates bioinformatics and cheminformatics tools. The evaluator ultimately assesses the output of the executor to ensure the richness and accuracy of the retrieved information. Our research highlights how ChatT2, designed with this multiagent framework, addresses the challenges faced by general LLMs in terms of understanding limited, specialized corpora and complex biological information and provides both experts and novices with a valuable tool for exploring various NPs of interest. The ChatT2 webserver can be accessed at https://chatt2.site/#/chat.

</details>
